
-- adempiere.cpma_ipa_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.cpma_ipa_reportview
AS SELECT cp.c_project_id AS record_id,
    cp2.c_projectline_id AS line_id,
    cp.ad_client_id,
    cp.ad_org_id,
    ( SELECT ao2.name
           FROM ad_org ao2
          WHERE ao2.ad_org_id = cp.ad_org_id) AS orgname,
    cp.docstatus,
    ( SELECT arl.name
           FROM ad_ref_list arl
             JOIN ad_reference ar ON ar.ad_reference_id = arl.ad_reference_id
          WHERE ar.name::text ~~ '_Document Status'::text AND arl.value::text = cp.docstatus::text) AS documentstatus,
    cp.c_doctypetarget_id,
    ( SELECT cd2.name
           FROM c_doctype cd2
          WHERE cd2.c_doctype_id = cp.c_doctypetarget_id) AS document_type,
    cp.isactive,
    cp.isapproved,
    cp.created,
    cp.createdby,
    cp.updated,
    cp.updatedby,
    ( SELECT au.name
           FROM ad_user au
          WHERE au.ad_user_id = cp.createdby) AS createdbyusername,
    ( SELECT cb_1.name
           FROM c_bpartner cb_1
             LEFT JOIN ad_user au ON cb_1.c_bpartner_id = au.c_bpartner_id
          WHERE au.ad_user_id = cp.createdby) AS createdbybpname,
    ( SELECT au.name
           FROM ad_user au
          WHERE au.ad_user_id = cp.updatedby) AS updatedbyusername,
    ( SELECT cb_1.name
           FROM c_bpartner cb_1
             LEFT JOIN ad_user au ON cb_1.c_bpartner_id = au.c_bpartner_id
          WHERE au.ad_user_id = cp.updatedby) AS updatedbybpname,
    cp.documentno,
    cp.datedoc AS document_date,
    cp.plannedqty AS total_planned_qty,
    cp.plannedamt AS total_planned_ammount,
    cp.m_product_category_id,
    ( SELECT concat(mpc.value, '_', mpc.name) AS concat
           FROM m_product_category mpc
          WHERE mpc.m_product_category_id = cp.m_product_category_id) AS product_category,
    cp2.line AS line_no,
    cp2.m_product_id,
    ( SELECT concat(mp.value, '_', mp.name) AS concat
           FROM m_product mp
          WHERE mp.m_product_id = cp2.m_product_id) AS product,
    cp2.plannedqty AS planned_qty,
    cp2.c_uom_id,
    ( SELECT cu.name
           FROM c_uom cu
          WHERE cu.c_uom_id = cp2.c_uom_id) AS uom,
    cp2.plannedprice AS planned_price,
    cp2.plannedamt AS planned_ammount
   FROM c_project cp
     JOIN c_projectline cp2 ON cp2.c_project_id = cp.c_project_id
  WHERE cp.ad_client_id = (( SELECT ac.ad_client_id
           FROM ad_client ac
          WHERE ac.name::text = 'Poultry Breeder'::text));



-- adempiere.drafted_so_doc_fs_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.drafted_so_doc_fs_reportview
AS SELECT ad_client_id,
    ad_org_id,
    docstatus,
    dateordered,
    salesrep_id,
    salesrep,
    documentno,
    '(Menunggu Realisasi)'::text AS status,
    bpartner_area,
    sold_to_cperp_code,
    sold_to_name,
    ship_to_cperp_code,
    ship_to_theos_code,
    ship_to_name,
    product,
    material_code,
    qtyentered,
    ship_to_address,
    description,
    m_warehouse_id
   FROM ( SELECT co.ad_client_id,
            co.ad_org_id,
            co.docstatus,
            co.dateordered,
            co.salesrep_id,
            ( SELECT au.name
                   FROM ad_user au
                  WHERE au.ad_user_id = co.salesrep_id) AS salesrep,
            co.documentno,
            ( SELECT cb.z_kode_fmdc
                   FROM c_bpartner cb
                  WHERE cb.c_bpartner_id = co.c_bpartner_id) AS bpartner_area,
            ( SELECT cb.value
                   FROM c_bpartner cb
                  WHERE cb.c_bpartner_id = co.c_bpartner_id) AS sold_to_cperp_code,
            ( SELECT cb.name
                   FROM c_bpartner cb
                  WHERE cb.c_bpartner_id = co.c_bpartner_id) AS sold_to_name,
            ( SELECT cb.value
                   FROM c_bpartner cb
                  WHERE cb.c_bpartner_id = co.bill_bpartner_id) AS ship_to_cperp_code,
            ( SELECT cb.z_kode_theos
                   FROM c_bpartner cb
                  WHERE cb.c_bpartner_id = co.bill_bpartner_id) AS ship_to_theos_code,
            ( SELECT cb.name
                   FROM c_bpartner cb
                  WHERE cb.c_bpartner_id = co.bill_bpartner_id) AS ship_to_name,
            "substring"(mp.description::text, '\((.+)\)'::text) AS product,
            mp.value AS material_code,
            co2.qtyentered,
            ( SELECT (((COALESCE(cl.address1, ''::character varying)::text || COALESCE(', '::text || cl.address2::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(cl.address3, ''::character varying)::text) || COALESCE(', '::text || cl.address4::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(', '::text || cl.city::text, ''::text)
                   FROM c_bpartner_location cbl
                     JOIN c_location cl ON cl.c_location_id = cbl.c_location_id
                  WHERE cbl.c_bpartner_location_id = co.bill_location_id) AS ship_to_address,
            co.description,
            co.m_warehouse_id
           FROM c_order co
             JOIN c_orderline co2 ON co2.c_order_id = co.c_order_id
             JOIN m_product mp ON mp.m_product_id = co2.m_product_id
          WHERE co.c_doctypetarget_id = (( SELECT cd2.c_doctype_id
                   FROM c_doctype cd2
                  WHERE cd2.name::text = 'SO DOC FS'::text)) AND co.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar) a
  ORDER BY sold_to_name, documentno, dateordered;



-- adempiere.internal_marketing_so_doc_fs_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.internal_marketing_so_doc_fs_reportview
AS SELECT ad_client_id,
    ad_org_id,
    docstatus,
    dateordered,
    salesrep_id,
    salesrep,
    documentno,
    '(Menunggu Realisasi)'::text AS status,
    bpartner_area,
    sold_to_cperp_code,
    sold_to_name,
    ship_to_cperp_code,
    ship_to_theos_code,
    ship_to_name,
    product,
    material_code,
    qtyentered,
    ship_to_address,
    description,
    m_warehouse_id
   FROM ( SELECT co.ad_client_id,
            co.ad_org_id,
            co.docstatus,
            co.dateordered,
            co.salesrep_id,
            ( SELECT au.name
                   FROM ad_user au
                  WHERE au.ad_user_id = co.salesrep_id) AS salesrep,
            co.documentno,
            ( SELECT cb.z_kode_fmdc
                   FROM c_bpartner cb
                  WHERE cb.c_bpartner_id = co.c_bpartner_id) AS bpartner_area,
            ( SELECT cb.value
                   FROM c_bpartner cb
                  WHERE cb.c_bpartner_id = co.c_bpartner_id) AS sold_to_cperp_code,
            ( SELECT cb.name
                   FROM c_bpartner cb
                  WHERE cb.c_bpartner_id = co.c_bpartner_id) AS sold_to_name,
            ( SELECT cb.value
                   FROM c_bpartner cb
                  WHERE cb.c_bpartner_id = co.bill_bpartner_id) AS ship_to_cperp_code,
            ( SELECT cb.z_kode_theos
                   FROM c_bpartner cb
                  WHERE cb.c_bpartner_id = co.bill_bpartner_id) AS ship_to_theos_code,
            ( SELECT cb.name
                   FROM c_bpartner cb
                  WHERE cb.c_bpartner_id = co.bill_bpartner_id) AS ship_to_name,
            "substring"(mp.description::text, '\((.+)\)'::text) AS product,
            mp.value AS material_code,
            co2.qtyentered,
            ( SELECT (((COALESCE(cl.address1, ''::character varying)::text || COALESCE(', '::text || cl.address2::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(cl.address3, ''::character varying)::text) || COALESCE(', '::text || cl.address4::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(', '::text || cl.city::text, ''::text)
                   FROM c_bpartner_location cbl
                     JOIN c_location cl ON cl.c_location_id = cbl.c_location_id
                  WHERE cbl.c_bpartner_location_id = co.bill_location_id) AS ship_to_address,
            co.description,
            co.m_warehouse_id
           FROM c_order co
             JOIN c_orderline co2 ON co2.c_order_id = co.c_order_id
             JOIN m_product mp ON mp.m_product_id = co2.m_product_id
          WHERE co.c_doctypetarget_id = (( SELECT cd2.c_doctype_id
                   FROM c_doctype cd2
                  WHERE cd2.name::text = 'SO DOC FS'::text))) a
  ORDER BY sold_to_name, documentno, dateordered;



-- adempiere.invoice_listing_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.invoice_listing_reportview
AS SELECT ad_client_id,
    ad_org_id,
    record_id,
    line_id,
    orgname,
    c_doctypetarget_id,
    document_type,
    isactive,
    isapproved,
    documentstatus,
    dateinvoiced,
    invoice_no,
    warehouse,
    do_no,
    doc_code,
    customer_code,
    customer_name,
    toleransi_ssales,
    toleransi_sfree,
    toleransi_dead,
    toleransi_sales,
    total_toleransi_plus_sample,
    quantity,
    qtyinvoiced AS quantity_gross,
    list_price * qtyinvoiced AS gross_amount,
    discount,
    discountamt * quantity AS discount_amount,
    z_discount_tolerance AS toleransi_discount,
        CASE
            WHEN total_toleransi_plus_sample IS NULL THEN quantity
            ELSE total_toleransi_plus_sample + quantity
        END * list_price - (list_price - price) * quantity -
        CASE
            WHEN toleransi_sales IS NULL THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE toleransi_sales * list_price
        END AS net_amount,
    freight_amount,
        CASE
            WHEN ppn_amount = 0::numeric THEN COALESCE(ppn_amount2, 0::numeric)
            ELSE ppn_amount
        END AS ppn_amount,
    list_price,
    freight_out,
    price
   FROM ( SELECT ci.ad_client_id,
            ci.ad_org_id,
            ci.c_invoice_id AS record_id,
            ci2.c_invoiceline_id AS line_id,
            ( SELECT ao2.name
                   FROM ad_org ao2
                  WHERE ao2.ad_org_id = ci.ad_org_id) AS orgname,
            ci.c_doctypetarget_id,
            ( SELECT cd2.name
                   FROM c_doctype cd2
                  WHERE cd2.c_doctype_id = ci.c_doctypetarget_id) AS document_type,
            ci.isactive,
            ci.isapproved,
            ( SELECT arl.name
                   FROM ad_ref_list arl
                     JOIN ad_reference ar ON ar.ad_reference_id = arl.ad_reference_id
                  WHERE ar.name::text ~~ '_Document Status'::text AND arl.value::text = ci.docstatus::text) AS documentstatus,
            ci.dateinvoiced,
            mw.name AS warehouse,
            ci.documentno AS invoice_no,
            co.documentno AS do_no,
            mp.value AS doc_code,
            cb.value AS customer_code,
            cb.name AS customer_name,
            0 AS toleransi_ssales,
            0 AS toleransi_sfree,
            0 AS toleransi_dead,
            ci2.qtytolerance AS toleransi_sales,
            ci2.qtytolerance AS total_toleransi_plus_sample,
            ci2.qtyentered AS quantity,
            0 AS discount,
            0 AS freight_amount,
            ci2.taxamt AS ppn_amount,
            ci2.qtyinvoiced,
            ci2.discountamt,
            ci2.z_discount_tolerance,
            ( SELECT round(c_tax.z_dtp_rate * 0.01 * ci2.linenetamt) AS dtp_amount
                   FROM c_tax
                  WHERE c_tax.c_tax_id = ci2.c_tax_id) AS ppn_amount2,
            ci2.pricelist AS list_price,
            0 AS freight_out,
            ci2.priceentered AS price
           FROM c_invoice ci
             LEFT JOIN c_invoiceline ci2 ON ci2.c_invoice_id = ci.c_invoice_id
             LEFT JOIN m_product mp ON mp.m_product_id = ci2.m_product_id
             LEFT JOIN c_order co ON co.c_order_id = ci2.c_order_id
             LEFT JOIN c_bpartner cb ON cb.c_bpartner_id = ci.c_bpartner_id
             LEFT JOIN m_warehouse mw ON mw.m_warehouse_id = co.m_warehouse_id
          WHERE ci.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar
          ORDER BY ci.dateinvoiced, ci.documentno) a;


-- adempiere.ipa_pr_po_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.ipa_pr_po_reportview
AS SELECT cp.ad_org_id,
    cp.ad_client_id,
    cp.documentno AS ipa_no,
    cp.datestart,
    cp.c_project_id,
    a.datedoc,
    cpl.line AS ipa_line,
    ipa_prod.name AS ipa_item_name,
    cpl.plannedqty AS ipa_qty,
    ipa_uom.name AS ipa_uom,
    cpl.plannedprice,
    cpl.plannedamt AS ipa_amount,
    cp.datedoc AS cp_date,
    cp.docstatus AS ipa_approvalstatus,
    cp.z_expired_date AS ipa_expired_date,
    a.help,
    a.documentno AS req_no,
    b.line AS reqline,
        CASE
            WHEN (( SELECT (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id)) > 0 THEN (( SELECT "substring"(rl.description::text, (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1, (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)) AS "substring"
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id AND ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)) > 0))::numeric
            ELSE b.qty
        END AS req_qty,
    b.m_product_id,
    product.name AS pr_prod,
    product.value AS pr_prod_value,
    product.name,
    product.value,
    b.c_uom_id,
    e.documentno AS po_no,
    d.line AS orderline,
    d.qtyordered,
    e.dateordered,
    e.datepromised,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN e.documentno IS NULL THEN ''::text
                    WHEN (e.docstatus = ANY (ARRAY['CL'::bpchar, 'VO'::bpchar])) AND f.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    WHEN f.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                    ELSE ( SELECT
                            CASE
                                WHEN g.movementqty < d.qtyordered THEN 'Partial'::text
                                ELSE 'Complete'::text
                            END AS "case")
                END AS "case") AS po_status,
        CASE
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS po_approval_status,
    e.mm_incoterms_id,
    e.salesrep_id,
    f.documentno AS gr_no,
    g.line AS gr_line,
    f.movementdate,
    g.movementqty,
    h.name AS comp_name,
    k.city,
    l.name AS vend_name,
    n.address1,
    n.city AS vend_city,
    product.value AS material_no,
    product.name AS material_name,
    product_category.value AS purchase_group,
    b.priceactual AS pr_price,
        CASE
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS pr_approval_status,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    ELSE ( SELECT
                            CASE
                                WHEN e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                                ELSE 'Complete'::text
                            END AS "case")
                END AS "case") AS pr_status,
    d.pricelist AS price,
    uom.x12de355 AS uomsimbol,
    d.description,
        CASE
            WHEN (d.z_savings - d.pricelist) < 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE (d.z_savings - d.pricelist) * d.qtyordered
        END AS saving,
    a.createdby AS pr_created,
    "left"(a.datedoc::character varying::text, 4) AS tahun,
    "substring"(a.datedoc::character varying::text, 6, 2) AS bulan,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN to_char(e.dateordered - (( SELECT wfa.created
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                         JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                         JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                      WHERE wfp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric AND wfn.name::text ~~ '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                      ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                    ELSE to_char(e.dateordered - (( SELECT wfa.created
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                         JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                         JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                      WHERE wfp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric AND wfn.name::text ~~ '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                      ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text)
                END AS to_char) AS leadtime_popr,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN to_char(f.movementdate - (( SELECT wfa.created
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                         JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                         JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                      WHERE wfp.record_id = e.c_order_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 259::numeric AND wfn.name::text ~~ '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                      ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                    ELSE to_char(f.movementdate - (( SELECT wfa.created
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                         JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                         JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                      WHERE wfp.record_id = e.c_order_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 259::numeric AND wfn.name::text ~~ '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                      ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text)
                END AS to_char) AS leadtime_grpo,
    location.city AS plant,
    l.value AS vendor_id,
    l.c_bpartner_id AS vendor_id2,
    h.value AS bukrs,
    comp_agent.name AS zzpic,
    warehouse.name AS lgobe,
    ernam.emailuser AS ernam,
    e.createdby,
    currency.iso_code,
    conrate.multiplyrate AS rate,
    whadd.address1::text || ((( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN whadd.address2 IS NULL THEN ''::character varying
                    ELSE whadd.address2
                END AS address2))::text) AS warehouseaddress,
    round(d.linenetamt) AS linenetamt,
    round(d.linenetamt * ctax.rate / 100::numeric) AS tax,
    round(d.linenetamt + d.linenetamt * ctax.rate / 100::numeric) AS totalaftertax,
    lcfa.amt AS freight,
    lcia.amt::text AS insurance,
    COALESCE(lcoa.amt, 0::numeric) + COALESCE(lcinstaa.amt, 0::numeric) AS other_charge,
    pyt.name AS paymenterm,
    loc.address1::text || loc.address2::text AS invoiceadd,
    a.ad_user_id,
        CASE
            WHEN ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)) > 0 THEN ((( SELECT "substring"(rl.description::text, (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1, (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)) AS "substring"
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id))::numeric) * b.priceactual
            ELSE b.linenetamt
        END AS pr_amount,
    COALESCE(b.z_pic, comp_agent.name) AS z_pic,
    a.c_doctype_id,
    a.docstatus,
    a.iscapex,
    b.description AS req_line_desc,
    whpo.value AS warehouse_po_value,
        CASE
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) = d.qtyordered THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) >= d.qtyordered AND (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) <= (d.qtyordered + d.qtyordered * e.tolerance / 100::numeric) THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) <= d.qtyordered AND (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) IS NOT NULL THEN 'Partial'::text
            ELSE 'Outstanding'::text
        END AS potogrstatus,
    g.description AS grline_deskripsi,
    parentorg.name AS parent_org_name,
    b.isactive AS req_line_active,
    d.isactive AS po_line_active,
    cp.user1_id AS costcenter
   FROM c_project cp
     LEFT JOIN c_projectline cpl ON cp.c_project_id = cpl.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline b ON b.c_projectline_id = cpl.c_projectline_id AND cpl.ad_org_id = b.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN m_requisition a ON b.m_requisition_id = a.m_requisition_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user c ON a.ad_user_id = c.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_matchreq mreq ON b.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline d ON
        CASE
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NULL THEN b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND mreq.c_orderline_id IS NULL AND b.c_orderline_id IS NOT NULL THEN b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
            ELSE mreq.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
        END
     LEFT JOIN c_order e ON d.c_order_id = e.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location bloca ON e.c_bpartner_location_id = bloca.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location loc ON bloca.c_location_id = loc.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_paymentterm pyt ON e.c_paymentterm_id = pyt.c_paymentterm_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lcf ON e.c_order_id = lcf.c_order_id AND lcf.m_costelement_id = 1000060::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcfa ON lcf.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcfa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcfa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lci ON e.c_order_id = lci.c_order_id AND lci.m_costelement_id = 1000061::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcia ON lci.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcia.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcia.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lco ON e.c_order_id = lco.c_order_id AND lco.m_costelement_id = 1000062::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcoa ON lco.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcoa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcoa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lcinsta ON e.c_order_id = lcinsta.c_order_id AND lcinsta.m_costelement_id = 1000063::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcinstaa ON lcinsta.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcinstaa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcinstaa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse whpo ON e.m_warehouse_id = whpo.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN c_currency currency ON e.c_currency_id = currency.c_currency_id
     LEFT JOIN c_conversion_rate conrate ON currency.c_currency_id = conrate.c_currency_id AND conrate.c_currency_id_to = 303::numeric AND conrate.validfrom = a.created
     LEFT JOIN ad_user comp_agent ON e.salesrep_id = comp_agent.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse warehouse ON e.m_warehouse_id = warehouse.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location whadd ON warehouse.c_location_id = whadd.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user ernam ON e.createdby = ernam.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_matchpo mpo ON d.c_orderline_id = mpo.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inoutline g ON g.m_inoutline_id = mpo.m_inoutline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inout f ON f.m_inout_id = g.m_inout_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org h ON a.ad_org_id = h.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo i ON h.ad_org_id = i.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location k ON i.c_location_id = k.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner l ON e.c_bpartner_id = l.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location m ON e.c_bpartner_location_id = m.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location n ON m.c_location_id = n.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product product ON b.m_product_id = product.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category product_category ON product.m_product_category_id = product_category.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON b.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo plant ON a.ad_org_id = plant.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location location ON plant.c_location_id = location.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_tax ctax ON d.c_tax_id = ctax.c_tax_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org parentorg ON i.parent_org_id = parentorg.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product ipa_prod ON ipa_prod.m_product_id = cpl.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom ipa_uom ON ipa_uom.c_uom_id = cpl.c_uom_id
  WHERE
        CASE
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND (( SELECT count(m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id) AS count
               FROM m_matchreq
              WHERE m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id)) > 1 THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND (( SELECT count(m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id) AS count
               FROM m_matchreq
              WHERE m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id)) = 1 THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL OR e.documentno IS NULL
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NULL THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL OR e.documentno IS NULL
            ELSE NULL::boolean
        END
  ORDER BY cp.documentno, cpl.line, product.name;


-- adempiere.ipa_pr_po_reportview_v2 source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.ipa_pr_po_reportview_v2
AS SELECT cp.ad_org_id,
    cp.ad_client_id,
    cp.documentno AS ipa_no,
    cp.datestart,
    cp.c_project_id,
    a.datedoc,
    cpl.line AS ipa_line,
    ipa_prod.name AS ipa_item_name,
    cpl.plannedqty AS ipa_qty,
    ipa_uom.name AS ipa_uom,
    cpl.plannedprice,
    cpl.plannedamt AS ipa_amount,
    cp.datedoc AS cp_date,
    cp.docstatus AS ipa_approvalstatus,
    cp.z_expired_date AS ipa_expired_date,
    a.help,
    a.documentno AS req_no,
    b.line AS reqline,
        CASE
            WHEN (( SELECT (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id)) > 0 THEN (( SELECT "substring"(rl.description::text, (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1, (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)) AS "substring"
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id AND ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)) > 0))::numeric
            ELSE b.qty
        END AS req_qty,
    b.m_product_id,
    product.name AS pr_prod,
    product.value AS pr_prod_value,
    product.name,
    product.value,
    b.c_uom_id,
    e.documentno AS po_no,
    d.line AS orderline,
    d.qtyordered,
    e.dateordered,
    e.datepromised,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN e.documentno IS NULL THEN ''::text
                    WHEN (e.docstatus = ANY (ARRAY['CL'::bpchar, 'VO'::bpchar])) AND f.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    WHEN f.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                    ELSE ( SELECT
                            CASE
                                WHEN g.movementqty < d.qtyordered THEN 'Partial'::text
                                ELSE 'Complete'::text
                            END AS "case")
                END AS "case") AS po_status,
        CASE
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS po_approval_status,
    e.mm_incoterms_id,
    e.salesrep_id,
    f.documentno AS gr_no,
    g.line AS gr_line,
    f.movementdate,
    g.movementqty,
    h.name AS comp_name,
    k.city,
    l.name AS vend_name,
    n.address1,
    n.city AS vend_city,
    product.value AS material_no,
    product.name AS material_name,
    product_category.value AS purchase_group,
    b.priceactual AS pr_price,
        CASE
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS pr_approval_status,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    ELSE ( SELECT
                            CASE
                                WHEN e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                                ELSE 'Complete'::text
                            END AS "case")
                END AS "case") AS pr_status,
    d.pricelist AS price,
    uom.x12de355 AS uomsimbol,
    d.description,
        CASE
            WHEN (d.z_savings - d.pricelist) < 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE (d.z_savings - d.pricelist) * d.qtyordered
        END AS saving,
    a.createdby AS pr_created,
    "left"(a.datedoc::character varying::text, 4) AS tahun,
    "substring"(a.datedoc::character varying::text, 6, 2) AS bulan,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN to_char(e.dateordered - (( SELECT wfa.created
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                         JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                         JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                      WHERE wfp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric AND wfn.name::text ~~ '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                      ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                    ELSE to_char(e.dateordered - (( SELECT wfa.created
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                         JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                         JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                      WHERE wfp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric AND wfn.name::text ~~ '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                      ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text)
                END AS to_char) AS leadtime_popr,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN to_char(f.movementdate - (( SELECT wfa.created
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                         JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                         JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                      WHERE wfp.record_id = e.c_order_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 259::numeric AND wfn.name::text ~~ '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                      ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                    ELSE to_char(f.movementdate - (( SELECT wfa.created
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                         JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                         JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                      WHERE wfp.record_id = e.c_order_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 259::numeric AND wfn.name::text ~~ '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                      ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text)
                END AS to_char) AS leadtime_grpo,
    location.city AS plant,
    l.value AS vendor_value,
    l.c_bpartner_id AS vendor_id2,
    h.value AS bukrs,
    comp_agent.name AS zzpic,
    warehouse.name AS lgobe,
    ernam.emailuser AS ernam,
    e.createdby,
    currency.iso_code,
    conrate.multiplyrate AS rate,
    whadd.address1::text || ((( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN whadd.address2 IS NULL THEN ''::character varying
                    ELSE whadd.address2
                END AS address2))::text) AS warehouseaddress,
    round(d.linenetamt) AS linenetamt,
    round(d.linenetamt * ctax.rate / 100::numeric) AS tax,
    round(d.linenetamt + d.linenetamt * ctax.rate / 100::numeric) AS totalaftertax,
    lcfa.amt AS freight,
    lcia.amt::text AS insurance,
    COALESCE(lcoa.amt, 0::numeric) + COALESCE(lcinstaa.amt, 0::numeric) AS other_charge,
    pyt.name AS paymenterm,
    loc.address1::text || loc.address2::text AS invoiceadd,
    a.ad_user_id,
        CASE
            WHEN ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)) > 0 THEN ((( SELECT "substring"(rl.description::text, (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1, (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)) AS "substring"
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id))::numeric) * b.priceactual
            ELSE b.linenetamt
        END AS pr_amount,
    COALESCE(b.z_pic, comp_agent.name) AS z_pic,
    a.c_doctype_id,
    a.docstatus,
    a.iscapex,
    b.description AS req_line_desc,
    whpo.value AS warehouse_po_value,
        CASE
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) = d.qtyordered THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) >= d.qtyordered AND (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) <= (d.qtyordered + d.qtyordered * e.tolerance / 100::numeric) THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) <= d.qtyordered AND (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) IS NOT NULL THEN 'Partial'::text
            ELSE 'Outstanding'::text
        END AS potogrstatus,
    g.description AS grline_deskripsi,
    parentorg.name AS parent_org_name,
    b.isactive AS req_line_active,
    d.isactive AS po_line_active,
    cp.user1_id AS costcenter
   FROM c_project cp
     LEFT JOIN c_projectline cpl ON cp.c_project_id = cpl.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline b ON b.c_projectline_id = cpl.c_projectline_id AND cpl.ad_org_id = b.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN m_requisition a ON b.m_requisition_id = a.m_requisition_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user c ON a.ad_user_id = c.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_matchreq mreq ON b.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline d ON
        CASE
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NULL THEN b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND mreq.c_orderline_id IS NULL AND b.c_orderline_id IS NOT NULL THEN b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
            ELSE mreq.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
        END
     LEFT JOIN c_order e ON d.c_order_id = e.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location bloca ON e.c_bpartner_location_id = bloca.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location loc ON bloca.c_location_id = loc.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_paymentterm pyt ON e.c_paymentterm_id = pyt.c_paymentterm_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lcf ON e.c_order_id = lcf.c_order_id AND lcf.m_costelement_id = 1000060::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcfa ON lcf.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcfa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcfa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lci ON e.c_order_id = lci.c_order_id AND lci.m_costelement_id = 1000061::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcia ON lci.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcia.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcia.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lco ON e.c_order_id = lco.c_order_id AND lco.m_costelement_id = 1000062::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcoa ON lco.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcoa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcoa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lcinsta ON e.c_order_id = lcinsta.c_order_id AND lcinsta.m_costelement_id = 1000063::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcinstaa ON lcinsta.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcinstaa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcinstaa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse whpo ON e.m_warehouse_id = whpo.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN c_currency currency ON e.c_currency_id = currency.c_currency_id
     LEFT JOIN c_conversion_rate conrate ON currency.c_currency_id = conrate.c_currency_id AND conrate.c_currency_id_to = 303::numeric AND conrate.validfrom = a.created
     LEFT JOIN ad_user comp_agent ON e.salesrep_id = comp_agent.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse warehouse ON e.m_warehouse_id = warehouse.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location whadd ON warehouse.c_location_id = whadd.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user ernam ON e.createdby = ernam.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_matchpo mpo ON d.c_orderline_id = mpo.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inoutline g ON g.m_inoutline_id = mpo.m_inoutline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inout f ON f.m_inout_id = g.m_inout_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org h ON a.ad_org_id = h.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo i ON h.ad_org_id = i.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location k ON i.c_location_id = k.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner l ON e.c_bpartner_id = l.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location m ON e.c_bpartner_location_id = m.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location n ON m.c_location_id = n.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product product ON b.m_product_id = product.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category product_category ON product.m_product_category_id = product_category.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON b.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo plant ON a.ad_org_id = plant.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location location ON plant.c_location_id = location.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_tax ctax ON d.c_tax_id = ctax.c_tax_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org parentorg ON i.parent_org_id = parentorg.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product ipa_prod ON ipa_prod.m_product_id = cpl.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom ipa_uom ON ipa_uom.c_uom_id = cpl.c_uom_id
  WHERE
        CASE
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND (( SELECT count(m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id) AS count
               FROM m_matchreq
              WHERE m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id)) > 1 THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND (( SELECT count(m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id) AS count
               FROM m_matchreq
              WHERE m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id)) = 1 THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL OR e.documentno IS NULL
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NULL THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL OR e.documentno IS NULL
            ELSE NULL::boolean
        END
  ORDER BY cp.documentno, cpl.line, product.name;



-- adempiere.open_requisition_v2 source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.open_requisition_v2
AS SELECT DISTINCT r.m_requisition_id,
    r.ad_client_id,
    r.ad_org_id,
    r.documentno,
    ( SELECT wfa.created
           FROM ad_wf_process wfp
             JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
             JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
          WHERE wfp.record_id = r.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_client_id = r.ad_client_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric AND wfn.name::text = '(DocComplete)'::text
          ORDER BY wfa.created DESC
         LIMIT 1) AS daterequired,
    r.docstatus,
    r.ad_user_id,
    rl.z_pic,
    rl.qty,
    COALESCE(o.documentno, ordr.documentno) AS a,
    pro.m_product_category_id,
    pro.name,
    pro.value,
    pro.sku,
    rl.c_uom_id,
    r.c_doctype_id,
    rl.line,
    rl.priceactual,
    rl.isactive AS req_line_active,
    mp.name AS pr_pricelist,
    rl.qty - COALESCE(ol.qtyentered, 0::numeric) AS remaining,
    (((COALESCE(r.description, ''::character varying)::text || COALESCE(( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN adwa.textmsg::text ~~ '%isITVerification=Y%'::text THEN ' Verification Approval US Area = Yes '::text
                    WHEN adwa.textmsg::text ~~ '%isITVerification=N%'::text THEN ' Verification Approval US Area = No '::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS "case"
           FROM ad_wf_process adwp
             JOIN ad_wf_activity adwa ON adwp.ad_wf_process_id = adwa.ad_wf_process_id
             JOIN ad_wf_node adwn ON adwa.ad_wf_node_id = adwn.ad_wf_node_id
          WHERE adwp.record_id = r.m_requisition_id AND adwp.created = (( SELECT adwpt.created
                   FROM ad_wf_process adwpt
                  WHERE adwpt.record_id = r.m_requisition_id AND adwpt.ad_table_id = 702::numeric
                  ORDER BY adwpt.created DESC
                 LIMIT 1)) AND adwp.ad_client_id = r.ad_client_id AND adwp.ad_org_id = r.ad_org_id AND adwn.name::text ~~ '%Verification US Area%'::text
          ORDER BY adwp.created DESC
         LIMIT 1), ''::text)) || COALESCE(( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN adwa.textmsg::text ~~ '%isITVerification=Y%'::text THEN ('('::text || "substring"(adwa.textmsg::text, "position"(adwa.textmsg::text, 'isITVerification=Y - '::text) + 21, length(adwa.textmsg::text))) || ') '::text
                    WHEN adwa.textmsg::text ~~ '%isITVerification=N%'::text THEN ('('::text || "substring"(adwa.textmsg::text, "position"(adwa.textmsg::text, 'isITVerification=N - '::text) + 21, length(adwa.textmsg::text))) || ')'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS "case"
           FROM ad_wf_process adwp
             JOIN ad_wf_activity adwa ON adwp.ad_wf_process_id = adwa.ad_wf_process_id
             JOIN ad_wf_node adwn ON adwa.ad_wf_node_id = adwn.ad_wf_node_id
          WHERE adwp.record_id = r.m_requisition_id AND adwp.created = (( SELECT adwpt.created
                   FROM ad_wf_process adwpt
                  WHERE adwpt.record_id = r.m_requisition_id AND adwpt.ad_table_id = 702::numeric
                  ORDER BY adwpt.created DESC
                 LIMIT 1)) AND adwp.ad_client_id = r.ad_client_id AND adwp.ad_org_id = r.ad_org_id AND adwn.name::text ~~ '%Verification US Area%'::text
          ORDER BY adwp.created DESC
         LIMIT 1), ''::text)) || COALESCE(( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN adwa.textmsg::text ~~ '%isITVerification=Y%'::text THEN ', Verification Approval Head of US = Yes '::text
                    WHEN adwa.textmsg::text ~~ '%isITVerification=N%'::text THEN ', Verification Approval Head of US = No '::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS "case"
           FROM ad_wf_process adwp
             JOIN ad_wf_activity adwa ON adwp.ad_wf_process_id = adwa.ad_wf_process_id
             JOIN ad_wf_node adwn ON adwa.ad_wf_node_id = adwn.ad_wf_node_id
          WHERE adwp.record_id = r.m_requisition_id AND adwp.created = (( SELECT adwpt.created
                   FROM ad_wf_process adwpt
                  WHERE adwpt.record_id = r.m_requisition_id AND adwpt.ad_table_id = 702::numeric
                  ORDER BY adwpt.created DESC
                 LIMIT 1)) AND adwp.ad_client_id = r.ad_client_id AND adwp.ad_org_id = r.ad_org_id AND adwn.name::text ~~ '%Verification Head of US%'::text
          ORDER BY adwp.created DESC
         LIMIT 1), ''::text)) || COALESCE(( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN adwa.textmsg::text ~~ '%isITVerification=Y%'::text THEN ('('::text || "substring"(adwa.textmsg::text, "position"(adwa.textmsg::text, 'isITVerification=Y - '::text) + 21, length(adwa.textmsg::text))) || ')'::text
                    WHEN adwa.textmsg::text ~~ '%isITVerification=N%'::text THEN ('('::text || "substring"(adwa.textmsg::text, "position"(adwa.textmsg::text, 'isITVerification=N - '::text) + 21, length(adwa.textmsg::text))) || ')'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS "case"
           FROM ad_wf_process adwp
             JOIN ad_wf_activity adwa ON adwp.ad_wf_process_id = adwa.ad_wf_process_id
             JOIN ad_wf_node adwn ON adwa.ad_wf_node_id = adwn.ad_wf_node_id
          WHERE adwp.record_id = r.m_requisition_id AND adwp.created = (( SELECT adwpt.created
                   FROM ad_wf_process adwpt
                  WHERE adwpt.record_id = r.m_requisition_id AND adwpt.ad_table_id = 702::numeric
                  ORDER BY adwpt.created DESC
                 LIMIT 1)) AND adwp.ad_client_id = r.ad_client_id AND adwp.ad_org_id = r.ad_org_id AND adwn.name::text ~~ '%Verification Head of US%'::text
          ORDER BY adwp.created DESC
         LIMIT 1), ''::text) AS remarks,
    ce.c_elementvalue_id
   FROM m_requisition r
     LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline rl ON r.m_requisition_id = rl.m_requisition_id
     LEFT JOIN m_matchreq mreq ON rl.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline ol ON mreq.c_orderline_id = ol.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product pro ON rl.m_product_id = pro.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN c_order o ON ol.c_order_id = o.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline orderline ON rl.c_orderline_id = orderline.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_order ordr ON orderline.c_order_id = ordr.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_elementvalue ce ON r.user1_id = ce.c_elementvalue_id
     LEFT JOIN ( SELECT sum(orl.qtyentered) AS qty,
            mareq.m_requisitionline_id AS reqlineid
           FROM m_matchreq mareq
             JOIN c_orderline orl ON mareq.c_orderline_id = orl.c_orderline_id
          GROUP BY mareq.m_requisitionline_id) tabel ON tabel.reqlineid = rl.m_requisitionline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_pricelist mp ON r.m_pricelist_id = mp.m_pricelist_id
  WHERE (r.c_doctype_id IN ( SELECT dt.c_doctype_id
           FROM c_doctype dt
          WHERE dt.name::text ~~ 'PR COM'::text)) AND (tabel.qty < rl.qty OR rl.c_orderline_id IS NULL)
  ORDER BY (COALESCE(o.documentno, ordr.documentno)) DESC, rl.line;


-- adempiere.po_gr_invoice_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.po_gr_invoice_reportview
AS SELECT co.ad_client_id,
    co.ad_org_id,
    co.documentno AS po_no,
    doc.name AS doctype_name,
    ce.name AS cost_center,
    col.line AS po_line,
    co.poreference,
    mp.value AS material_no,
    mp.name AS material_name,
    cu.name AS po_uom,
    COALESCE(col.qtyordered, 0::numeric) AS qtyordered,
    COALESCE(col.priceentered, 0::numeric) AS priceentered,
    (COALESCE(col.priceentered, 0::numeric) - COALESCE(col.ppnincluded, 0::numeric)) * COALESCE(col.qtyordered, 0::numeric) AS totalbeforetax,
    COALESCE(col.ppnincluded, 0::numeric) AS tax,
    COALESCE(col.linenetamt, 0::numeric) AS totalaftertax,
    COALESCE(lcfa.amt, 0::numeric) AS freight,
    COALESCE(lcia.amt, 0::numeric) AS insurance,
    COALESCE(lcoa.amt, 0::numeric) + COALESCE(lcinstaa.amt, 0::numeric) AS other_charge,
    cc.iso_code AS currency,
    COALESCE(conrate.multiplyrate, 0::numeric) AS rate,
    product_category.value AS purchase_group,
    co.dateordered,
    co.datepromised,
        CASE
            WHEN co.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN co.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN co.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN co.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN co.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN co.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS po_approval_status,
        CASE
            WHEN col.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar THEN NULL::text
            ELSE 'Closed'::text
        END AS po_line_active_new,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN co.documentno IS NULL THEN ''::text
                    WHEN (co.docstatus = ANY (ARRAY['CL'::bpchar, 'VO'::bpchar])) AND mi.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    WHEN mi.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                    ELSE ( SELECT
                            CASE
                                WHEN g.movementqty < col.qtyordered THEN 'Partial'::text
                                ELSE 'Complete'::text
                            END AS "case")
                END AS "case") AS po_status,
        CASE
            WHEN col.isactive = 'N'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = col.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) = col.qtyordered THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = col.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) >= col.qtyordered AND (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = col.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) <= (col.qtyordered + col.qtyordered * co.tolerance / 100::numeric) THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = col.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) <= col.qtyordered AND (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = col.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) IS NOT NULL THEN 'Partial'::text
            ELSE 'Outstanding'::text
        END AS potogrstatus,
        CASE
            WHEN (col.z_savings - col.pricelist) < 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE (col.z_savings - col.pricelist) * col.qtyordered
        END AS saving,
    co.mm_incoterms_id,
    co.salesrep_id,
    loc.address1::text || loc.address2::text AS invoiceadd,
    pyt.name AS paymenterm,
    mi.documentno AS gr_no,
    g.line AS gr_line,
    COALESCE(g.qtyentered, 0::numeric) AS gr_qty,
    COALESCE(col.priceentered, 0::numeric) AS gr_price,
    COALESCE((COALESCE(col.priceentered, 0::numeric) - COALESCE(col.ppnincluded, 0::numeric)) * g.qtyentered, 0::numeric) AS gr_totalbeforetax,
    COALESCE(col.ppnincluded, 0::numeric) AS gr_tax,
    COALESCE(g.qtyentered, 0::numeric) * COALESCE(col.priceentered, 0::numeric) AS gr_totalaftertax,
    g.description AS gr_description,
    mi.movementdate,
    whadd.address1::text || ((( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN whadd.address2 IS NULL THEN ''::character varying
                    ELSE whadd.address2
                END AS address2))::text) AS warehouseaddress,
    warehouse.name AS lgobe,
    "left"(co.dateordered::character varying::text, 4) AS tahun,
    "substring"(co.dateordered::character varying::text, 6, 2) AS bulan,
    l.value AS vendor_id,
    l.name AS vend_name,
    bloca.name AS vend_location_name,
    n.city AS vend_city,
    ci.documentno AS invoice_number,
    ci.dateinvoiced AS invoice_date,
    ci.grandtotal AS invoice_amount,
        CASE
            WHEN ci.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN ci.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN ci.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN ci.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN ci.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN ci.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS invoice_doc_status,
    ce.c_elementvalue_id,
    co.c_doctypetarget_id AS c_doctype_id,
    mi.z_sj_no AS nosj,
    mi.freightamt,
    mi.poreference AS gr_po_ref
   FROM c_order co
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype doc ON co.c_doctypetarget_id = doc.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline col ON co.c_order_id = col.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product mp ON mp.m_product_id = col.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom cu ON cu.c_uom_id = col.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN c_elementvalue ce ON ce.c_elementvalue_id = co.user1_id
     LEFT JOIN m_matchpo mpo ON col.c_orderline_id = mpo.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inoutline g ON g.m_inoutline_id = mpo.m_inoutline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inout mi ON mi.m_inout_id = g.m_inout_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lcf ON co.c_order_id = lcf.c_order_id AND lcf.m_costelement_id = (( SELECT mc.m_costelement_id
           FROM m_costelement mc
          WHERE mc.name::text = 'Freight'::text AND mc.ad_client_id = 1000017::numeric))
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcfa ON lcf.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcfa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcfa.c_orderline_id = col.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lci ON co.c_order_id = lci.c_order_id AND lci.m_costelement_id = (( SELECT mc.m_costelement_id
           FROM m_costelement mc
          WHERE mc.name::text = 'Insurance'::text AND mc.ad_client_id = 1000017::numeric))
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcia ON lci.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcia.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcia.c_orderline_id = col.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lco ON co.c_order_id = lco.c_order_id AND lco.m_costelement_id = (( SELECT mc.m_costelement_id
           FROM m_costelement mc
          WHERE mc.name::text = 'Other Charges'::text AND mc.ad_client_id = 1000017::numeric))
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcoa ON lco.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcoa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcoa.c_orderline_id = col.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lcinsta ON co.c_order_id = lcinsta.c_order_id AND lcinsta.m_costelement_id = (( SELECT mc.m_costelement_id
           FROM m_costelement mc
          WHERE mc.name::text = 'Installation'::text AND mc.ad_client_id = 1000017::numeric))
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcinstaa ON lcinsta.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcinstaa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcinstaa.c_orderline_id = col.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_currency cc ON cc.c_currency_id = co.c_currency_id
     LEFT JOIN c_conversion_rate conrate ON cc.c_currency_id = conrate.c_currency_id AND conrate.c_currency_id_to = 303::numeric AND conrate.validfrom = to_char(co.created, 'yyyy-mm-dd 00:00:00'::text)::timestamp without time zone
     LEFT JOIN m_product product ON col.m_product_id = product.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category product_category ON product.m_product_category_id = product_category.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location bloca ON co.c_bpartner_location_id = bloca.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location loc ON bloca.c_location_id = loc.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_paymentterm pyt ON co.c_paymentterm_id = pyt.c_paymentterm_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse warehouse ON co.m_warehouse_id = warehouse.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location whadd ON warehouse.c_location_id = whadd.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner l ON co.c_bpartner_id = l.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location m ON co.c_bpartner_location_id = m.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location n ON m.c_location_id = n.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_invoiceline cil ON cil.m_inoutline_id = g.m_inoutline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_invoice ci ON ci.c_invoice_id = cil.c_invoice_id
  WHERE (co.c_doctypetarget_id IN ( SELECT cd.c_doctype_id
           FROM c_doctype cd
          WHERE cd.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['PO AFF'::character varying::text, 'STO FEED ADDITIONAL'::character varying::text, 'PO FEED ADDITIONAL'::character varying::text, 'PO HE'::character varying::text, 'STO AFF'::character varying::text, 'STO HE'::character varying::text, 'PO BIRD'::character varying::text])))
  ORDER BY co.documentno, col.line;


-- adempiere.po_gudang_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.po_gudang_reportview
AS SELECT ord.ad_client_id,
    ord.ad_org_id,
    org.value AS plant,
    org.description AS companyname,
    loc1.city AS companycity,
    org.name AS location,
    ord.c_order_id AS nopo,
    ordline.line,
    product.value AS nosku,
    product.name AS namasku,
    ordline.qtyordered AS poqty,
    minoutline.qtyentered AS grqty,
    uom.name AS uom,
    ord.dateordered AS tglpo,
    ord.datepromised AS eta,
    aduser.ad_user_id AS buyer1,
    aduser.name AS buyer,
    minco.name AS incoterms,
    warehouse.m_warehouse_id AS warehousename1,
    warehouse.name AS warehousename,
    loc2.address1 AS deliveryaddress,
    bpartner.c_bpartner_id AS suppliername1,
    bpartner.name AS suppliername,
    loc3.address1 AS supplieraddress,
    loc3.city AS suppliercity,
    req.documentno AS pr_no,
    req.datedoc AS pr_date,
    payterm.name AS paymentterm,
    ord.docstatus,
    req.created,
    req.createdby,
    dtyp.name,
    dtyp.c_doctype_id,
    ev.c_elementvalue_id
   FROM c_order ord
     LEFT JOIN ad_org org ON ord.ad_org_id = org.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo orginfo ON org.ad_org_id = orginfo.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location loc1 ON orginfo.c_location_id = loc1.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline ordline ON ord.c_order_id = ordline.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product product ON ordline.m_product_id = product.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inoutline minoutline ON ordline.c_orderline_id = minoutline.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON ordline.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user aduser ON ord.salesrep_id = aduser.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN mm_incoterms minco ON ord.mm_incoterms_id = minco.mm_incoterms_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse warehouse ON ord.m_warehouse_id = warehouse.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location loc2 ON loc2.c_location_id = warehouse.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bpartner ON ord.c_bpartner_id = bpartner.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location bpartnerloc ON ord.c_bpartner_location_id = bpartnerloc.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location loc3 ON bpartnerloc.c_location_id = loc3.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline reqline ON ordline.c_orderline_id = reqline.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_requisition req ON reqline.m_requisition_id = req.m_requisition_id
     LEFT JOIN c_paymentterm payterm ON payterm.c_paymentterm_id = ord.c_paymentterm_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype dtyp ON dtyp.c_doctype_id = ord.c_doctypetarget_id
     LEFT JOIN c_elementvalue ev ON ord.user1_id = ev.c_elementvalue_id
  WHERE dtyp.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['PO AFF'::text, 'PO BIRD'::text, 'PO COM'::text, 'PO DOC FS'::text, 'PO DOC GP'::text, 'PO DOC PS'::text, 'PO FEED'::text, 'PO FEED ADDITIONAL'::text, 'PO HE'::text, 'PO PE'::text, 'PO VMD'::text, 'STO AFF'::text, 'STOF DOC FS'::text, 'STO DOC PS'::text, 'STO FEED'::text, 'STO FEED ADDITIONAL'::text, 'STO HE'::text, 'STO PE'::text])
  ORDER BY ord.documentno, ordline.line;

-- adempiere.pr_approval source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.pr_approval
AS SELECT a.ad_client_id,
    a.ad_org_id,
    a.m_requisition_id,
    a.help,
    a.documentno AS req_no,
    e.documentno AS po_no,
    mio.documentno AS gr_no,
    b.line AS reqline,
    b.m_product_id,
    prod.value AS prodvalue,
    prod.name AS prodname,
    b.qty AS req_qty,
    uom.x12de355 AS uomsimbol,
    b.priceactual AS pr_price,
    b.linenetamt AS pr_amount,
    a.datedoc,
    a.createdby AS pr_created,
    a.z_pic,
        CASE
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS pr_approval_status,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    ELSE ( SELECT
                            CASE
                                WHEN e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                                ELSE 'Complete'::text
                            END AS "case")
                END AS "case") AS pr_status,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN to_char(e.dateordered - a.datedoc, 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                    ELSE to_char(e.dateordered - a.datedoc, 'dd'::text)
                END AS to_char) AS leadtime_popr,
    adwa2.created,
    doctyp.name,
    e.c_paymentterm_id,
    adwn.ad_wf_node_id AS approval
   FROM m_requisition a
     LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline b ON b.m_requisition_id = a.m_requisition_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON b.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user c ON a.ad_user_id = c.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline d ON b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_order e ON d.c_order_id = e.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON b.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inoutline miol ON miol.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inout mio ON mio.m_inout_id = miol.m_inout_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype doctyp ON a.c_doctype_id = doctyp.c_doctype_id
     JOIN ad_wf_process adwp ON adwp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND a.ad_client_id = adwp.ad_client_id AND a.ad_org_id = adwp.ad_org_id
     JOIN ad_wf_activity adwa ON adwa.ad_wf_process_id = adwp.ad_wf_process_id
     JOIN ad_wf_node adwn ON adwa.ad_wf_node_id = adwn.ad_wf_node_id
     JOIN ad_wf_activity adwa2 ON adwa2.ad_wf_activity_id = (( SELECT adwax.ad_wf_activity_id
           FROM ad_wf_activity adwax
          WHERE adwax.ad_wf_process_id = adwp.ad_wf_process_id AND adwax.created > adwa.created
          ORDER BY adwax.created
         LIMIT 1))
UNION
 SELECT a.ad_client_id,
    a.ad_org_id,
    a.m_requisition_id,
    a.help,
    a.documentno AS req_no,
    e.documentno AS po_no,
    mio.documentno AS gr_no,
    b.line AS reqline,
    b.m_product_id,
    prod.value AS prodvalue,
    prod.name AS prodname,
    b.qty AS req_qty,
    uom.x12de355 AS uomsimbol,
    b.priceactual AS pr_price,
    b.linenetamt AS pr_amount,
    a.datedoc,
    a.createdby AS pr_created,
    a.z_pic,
        CASE
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS pr_approval_status,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    ELSE ( SELECT
                            CASE
                                WHEN e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                                ELSE 'Complete'::text
                            END AS "case")
                END AS "case") AS pr_status,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN to_char(e.dateordered - a.datedoc, 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                    ELSE to_char(e.dateordered - a.datedoc, 'dd'::text)
                END AS to_char) AS leadtime_popr,
    NULL::timestamp without time zone AS created,
    doctyp.name,
    e.c_paymentterm_id,
    adwn.ad_wf_node_id AS approval
   FROM m_requisition a
     LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline b ON b.m_requisition_id = a.m_requisition_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON b.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user c ON a.ad_user_id = c.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline d ON b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_order e ON d.c_order_id = e.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON b.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inoutline miol ON miol.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inout mio ON mio.m_inout_id = miol.m_inout_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype doctyp ON a.c_doctype_id = doctyp.c_doctype_id
     JOIN ad_wf_process adwp ON adwp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND a.ad_client_id = adwp.ad_client_id AND a.ad_org_id = adwp.ad_org_id
     JOIN ad_wf_activity adwa ON adwa.ad_wf_process_id = adwp.ad_wf_process_id AND adwa.wfstate = 'OS'::bpchar
     JOIN ad_wf_node adwn ON adwa.ad_wf_node_id = adwn.ad_wf_node_id;


-- adempiere.pr_approval_foac source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.pr_approval_foac
AS SELECT a.ad_client_id,
    a.ad_org_id,
    a.m_requisition_id,
    a.help,
    a.documentno AS req_no,
    e.documentno AS po_no,
    mio.documentno AS gr_no,
    b.line AS reqline,
    d.m_product_id,
    prod.value AS prodvalue,
    prod.name AS prodname,
        CASE
            WHEN d.isactive = 'N'::bpchar THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE b.qty
        END AS req_qty,
    uom.x12de355 AS uomsimbol,
    b.priceactual AS pr_price,
        CASE
            WHEN d.isactive = 'N'::bpchar THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE b.linenetamt
        END AS pr_amount,
    a.datedoc,
    a.createdby AS pr_created,
    b.z_pic,
        CASE
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS pr_approval_status,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    ELSE ( SELECT
                            CASE
                                WHEN e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                                ELSE 'Complete'::text
                            END AS "case")
                END AS "case") AS pr_status,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN to_char(e.dateordered - a.datedoc, 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                    ELSE to_char(e.dateordered - a.datedoc, 'dd'::text)
                END AS to_char) AS leadtime_popr,
    adwa2.created,
    doctyp.name,
    e.c_paymentterm_id,
    a.iscapex,
    curr.iso_code AS currency,
    b.description AS deskripsi_prline,
    eval.name AS deptname,
    eval.description AS kodedep,
    eval.c_elementvalue_id,
    d.isactive,
        CASE
            WHEN d.isactive = 'N'::bpchar THEN d.isactive
            ELSE b.isactive
        END AS req_active,
        CASE
            WHEN d.isactive = 'N'::bpchar THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE d.qtyentered
        END AS po_qty,
    d.priceactual AS price_po,
        CASE
            WHEN d.isactive = 'N'::bpchar THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE d.linenetamt
        END AS amt_po,
    mio.movementdate,
    miol.qtyentered
   FROM m_requisition a
     LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline b ON b.m_requisition_id = a.m_requisition_id
     LEFT JOIN c_elementvalue eval ON a.user1_id = eval.c_elementvalue_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON b.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user c ON a.ad_user_id = c.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline d ON b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_order e ON d.c_order_id = e.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON b.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inoutline miol ON miol.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inout mio ON mio.m_inout_id = miol.m_inout_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype doctyp ON a.c_doctype_id = doctyp.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN m_pricelist mpr ON a.m_pricelist_id = mpr.m_pricelist_id
     LEFT JOIN c_currency curr ON mpr.c_currency_id = curr.c_currency_id
     JOIN ad_wf_process adwp ON adwp.ad_wf_process_id =
        CASE
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN ( SELECT wfp.ad_wf_process_id
               FROM ad_wf_process wfp
              WHERE wfp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric AND NOT (wfp.ad_wf_process_id IN ( SELECT wfpt.ad_wf_process_id
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfpt
                      WHERE wfpt.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfpt.ad_table_id = 702::numeric
                      ORDER BY wfpt.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1))
              ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
             LIMIT 1)
            ELSE ( SELECT wfp.ad_wf_process_id
               FROM ad_wf_process wfp
              WHERE wfp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric
              ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
             LIMIT 1)
        END
     JOIN ad_wf_activity adwa ON adwa.ad_wf_process_id = adwp.ad_wf_process_id
     JOIN ad_wf_node adwn ON adwa.ad_wf_node_id = adwn.ad_wf_node_id AND adwn.name::text ~~ '%FOAC%'::text
     JOIN ad_wf_activity adwa2 ON adwa2.ad_wf_activity_id = (( SELECT adwax.ad_wf_activity_id
           FROM ad_wf_activity adwax
          WHERE adwax.ad_wf_process_id = adwp.ad_wf_process_id AND adwax.created > adwa.created
          ORDER BY adwax.created
         LIMIT 1))
UNION

    a.ad_org_id,
    a.m_requisition_id,
    a.help,

     LEFT JOIN m_inout mio ON mio.m_inout_id = miol.m_inout_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype doctyp ON a.c_doctype_id = doctyp.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN m_pricelist mpr ON a.m_pricelist_id = mpr.m_pricelist_id
     LEFT JOIN c_currency curr ON mpr.c_currency_id = curr.c_currency_id
     JOIN ad_wf_process adwp ON adwp.ad_wf_process_id =
        CASE
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN ( SELECT wfp.ad_wf_process_id

                      WHERE wfpt.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfpt.ad_table_id = 702::numeric

                     LIMIT 1))
