              WHERE wfp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric
              ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
             LIMIT 1)
        END
     JOIN ad_wf_activity adwa ON adwa.ad_wf_process_id = adwp.ad_wf_process_id AND adwa.wfstate = 'OS'::bpchar
     JOIN ad_wf_node adwn ON adwa.ad_wf_node_id = adwn.ad_wf_node_id AND adwn.name::text ~~ '%FOAC%'::text;



-- adempiere.pr_po_invoice_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.pr_po_invoice_reportview
AS SELECT ad_client_id,
    ad_org_id,
    help,
    req_no,
    reqline,
    req_qty,
    m_product_id,
    pr_prod,
    pr_prod_value,
    name,
    value,
    c_uom_id,
    datedoc,
    po_no,
    orderline,
    qtyordered,
    dateordered,
    datepromised,
    po_status,
    po_approval_status,
    mm_incoterms_id,
    salesrep_id,
    gr_no,
    gr_line,
    movementdate,
    movementqty,
    comp_name,
    city,
    vend_name,
    address1,
    vend_city,
    material_no,
    material_name,
    purchase_group,
    pr_price,
    pr_approval_status,
    pr_status,
    price,
    uomsimbol,
    description,
    saving,
    pr_created,
    tahun,
    bulan,
    leadtime_popr,
    leadtime_grpo,
    plant,
    vendor_id AS vendor_searchkey,
    vendor_id2 AS c_bpartner_id,
    bukrs,
    zzpic,
    lgobe,
    ernam,
    createdby,
    iso_code,
    rate,
    warehouseaddress,
    linenetamt,
    tax,
    totalaftertax,
    freight,
    insurance,
    other_charge,
    paymenterm,
    invoiceadd,
    ad_user_id,
    pr_amount,
    z_pic,
    c_doctype_id,
    docstatus,
    iscapex,
    req_line_desc,
    warehouse_po_value,
    potogrstatus,
    grline_deskripsi,
    parent_org_name,
    req_line_active,
    po_line_active,
    po_line_active_new,
    round(movementqty * price) AS gr_amount,
        CASE
            WHEN tax > 0::numeric THEN round(round(movementqty * price) * 0.1)
            WHEN tax = 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE NULL::numeric
        END AS gr_tax_amount,
    round(
        CASE
            WHEN tax > 0::numeric THEN round(round(movementqty * price) * 0.1)
            WHEN tax = 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE NULL::numeric
        END + round(movementqty * price)) AS gr_amount_after_tax,
    c_elementvalue_id,
    glaccount,
    gl_account_id,
    invoice_number,
    invoice_date,
    invoice_amount,
    poreference,
    invoice_doc_status,
    nosj,
    freightamt,
    journal_amt,
    journal_docno,
    journal_datedoc,
    journal_docstatus
   FROM ( SELECT pr_po_reportview_mv.ad_client_id,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.ad_org_id,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.help,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.req_no,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.reqline,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.req_qty,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.m_product_id,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.pr_prod,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.pr_prod_value,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.name,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.value,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.c_uom_id,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.datedoc,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.po_no,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.orderline,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.qtyordered,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.dateordered,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.datepromised,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.po_status,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.po_approval_status,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.mm_incoterms_id,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.salesrep_id,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.gr_no,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.gr_line,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.movementdate,
                CASE
                    WHEN io.docstatus = 'RE'::bpchar THEN pr_po_reportview_mv.movementqty * '-1'::integer::numeric
                    ELSE pr_po_reportview_mv.movementqty
                END AS movementqty,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.comp_name,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.city,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.vend_name,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.address1,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.vend_city,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.material_no,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.material_name,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.purchase_group,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.pr_price,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.pr_approval_status,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.pr_status,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.price,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.uomsimbol,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.description,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.saving,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.pr_created,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.tahun,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.bulan,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.leadtime_popr,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.leadtime_grpo,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.plant,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.vendor_id,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.vendor_id2,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.bukrs,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.zzpic,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.lgobe,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.ernam,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.createdby,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.iso_code,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.rate,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.warehouseaddress,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.linenetamt,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.tax,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.totalaftertax,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.freight,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.insurance,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.other_charge,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.paymenterm,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.invoiceadd,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.ad_user_id,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.pr_amount,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.z_pic,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.c_doctype_id,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.docstatus,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.iscapex,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.req_line_desc,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.warehouse_po_value,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.potogrstatus,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.grline_deskripsi,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.parent_org_name,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.req_line_active,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.po_line_active,
            pr_po_reportview_mv.po_line_active_new,
            ce.c_elementvalue_id,
            (ce2.value::text || '-'::text) || ce2.name::text AS glaccount,
            mr.gl_account_id,
            ci.documentno AS invoice_number,
            ci.dateinvoiced AS invoice_date,
            ci.grandtotal AS invoice_amount,
            co.poreference,
                CASE
                    WHEN ci.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
                    WHEN ci.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
                    WHEN ci.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
                    WHEN ci.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
                    WHEN ci.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
                    WHEN ci.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS invoice_doc_status,
            io.z_sj_no AS nosj,
            io.freightamt,
            gj.totalcr AS journal_amt,
            gj.documentno AS journal_docno,
            gj.datedoc AS journal_datedoc,
                CASE
                    WHEN gj.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
                    WHEN gj.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
                    WHEN gj.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
                    WHEN gj.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
                    WHEN gj.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
                    WHEN gj.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS journal_docstatus
           FROM pr_po_reportview_mv
             LEFT JOIN m_inout io ON io.documentno::text = pr_po_reportview_mv.gr_no
             LEFT JOIN m_requisition mreq ON mreq.documentno::text = pr_po_reportview_mv.req_no AND mreq.ad_client_id = pr_po_reportview_mv.ad_client_id::numeric
             LEFT JOIN c_elementvalue ce ON mreq.user1_id = ce.c_elementvalue_id
             LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline mr ON mreq.m_requisition_id = mr.m_requisition_id AND mr.line = pr_po_reportview_mv.reqline::numeric
             LEFT JOIN c_elementvalue ce2 ON mr.gl_account_id = ce2.c_elementvalue_id
             LEFT JOIN c_order co ON co.documentno::text = pr_po_reportview_mv.po_no
             LEFT JOIN m_inoutline iol ON iol.m_inout_id = io.m_inout_id
             LEFT JOIN c_invoiceline cil ON cil.m_inoutline_id = iol.m_inoutline_id
             LEFT JOIN c_invoice ci ON ci.c_invoice_id = cil.c_invoice_id
             LEFT JOIN z_matchmrars zm ON zm.m_inout_id = io.m_inout_id
             LEFT JOIN gl_journal gj ON gj.gl_journal_id = zm.gl_journal_id
        UNION ALL
        ( SELECT a_1.ad_client_id,
            a_1.ad_org_id,
            a_1.help,
            a_1.documentno AS req_no,
            b.line AS reqline,
                CASE
                    WHEN (( SELECT (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                                 JOIN m_requisition requix_1 ON rl_1.m_requisition_id = requix_1.m_requisition_id
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id AND requix_1.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                                 JOIN m_requisition requix_1 ON rl_1.m_requisition_id = requix_1.m_requisition_id
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id AND requix_1.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar)) + 1)
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl
                         JOIN m_requisition requix ON rl.m_requisition_id = requix.m_requisition_id
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id AND requix.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar)) > 0 AND (( SELECT (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                                 JOIN m_requisition requix_1 ON rl_1.m_requisition_id = requix_1.m_requisition_id
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id AND requix_1.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                                 JOIN m_requisition requix_1 ON rl_1.m_requisition_id = requix_1.m_requisition_id
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id AND requix_1.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar)) + 1)
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl
                         JOIN m_requisition requix ON rl.m_requisition_id = requix.m_requisition_id
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id AND requix.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar)) IS NOT NULL THEN (( SELECT "substring"(rl.description::text, (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1, (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)) AS "substring"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id AND ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)) > 0))::numeric
                    ELSE b.qty
                END AS req_qty,
            b.m_product_id,
            product.name AS pr_prod,
            product.value AS pr_prod_value,
            product.name,
            product.value,
            b.c_uom_id,
            a_1.datedoc,
            e.documentno AS po_no,
            d.line AS orderline,
            d.qtyordered,
            e.dateordered,
            e.datepromised,
            ( SELECT
                        CASE
                            WHEN e.documentno IS NULL THEN ''::text
                            WHEN (e.docstatus = ANY (ARRAY['CL'::bpchar, 'VO'::bpchar])) AND f.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                            WHEN f.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                            ELSE ( SELECT
                                    CASE
WHEN g.movementqty < d.qtyordered THEN 'Partial'::text
ELSE 'Complete'::text
                                    END AS "case")
                        END AS "case") AS po_status,
                CASE
                    WHEN e.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
                    WHEN e.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
                    WHEN e.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
                    WHEN e.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
                    WHEN e.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
                    WHEN e.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS po_approval_status,
            e.mm_incoterms_id,
            e.salesrep_id,
            f.documentno AS gr_no,
            g.line AS gr_line,
            f.movementdate,
                CASE
                    WHEN f.docstatus = 'RE'::bpchar THEN g.movementqty * '-1'::integer::numeric
                    ELSE g.movementqty
                END AS movementqty,
            h.name AS comp_name,
            k.city,
            l.name AS vend_name,
            n.address1,
            n.city AS vend_city,
            product.value AS material_no,
            product.name AS material_name,
            product_category.value AS purchase_group,
            b.priceactual AS pr_price,
                CASE
                    WHEN a_1.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
                    WHEN a_1.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
                    WHEN a_1.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
                    WHEN a_1.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
                    WHEN a_1.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
                    WHEN a_1.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS pr_approval_status,
            ( SELECT
                        CASE
                            WHEN a_1.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                            WHEN a_1.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                            ELSE ( SELECT
                                    CASE
WHEN e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
ELSE 'Complete'::text
                                    END AS "case")
                        END AS "case") AS pr_status,
            d.pricelist AS price,
            uom.x12de355 AS uomsimbol,
            d.description,
                CASE
                    WHEN (d.z_savings - d.pricelist) < 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
                    ELSE (d.z_savings - d.pricelist) * d.qtyordered
                END AS saving,
            a_1.createdby AS pr_created,
            "left"(a_1.datedoc::character varying::text, 4) AS tahun,
            "substring"(a_1.datedoc::character varying::text, 6, 2) AS bulan,
            ( SELECT
                        CASE
                            WHEN to_char(e.dateordered - (( SELECT wfa.created
                               FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                                 JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                                 JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                              WHERE wfp.record_id = a_1.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric AND wfn.name::text = '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                              ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                             LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                            ELSE to_char(e.dateordered - (( SELECT wfa.created
                               FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                                 JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                                 JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                              WHERE wfp.record_id = a_1.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric AND wfn.name::text = '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                              ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                             LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text)
                        END AS to_char) AS leadtime_popr,
            ( SELECT
                        CASE
                            WHEN to_char(f.movementdate - (( SELECT wfa.created
                               FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                                 JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                                 JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                              WHERE wfp.record_id = e.c_order_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 259::numeric AND wfn.name::text = '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                              ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                             LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                            ELSE to_char(f.movementdate - (( SELECT wfa.created
                               FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                                 JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                                 JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                              WHERE wfp.record_id = e.c_order_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 259::numeric AND wfn.name::text = '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                              ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                             LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text)
                        END AS to_char) AS leadtime_grpo,
            location.city AS plant,
            l.value AS vendor_id,
            l.c_bpartner_id AS vendor_id2,
            h.value AS bukrs,
            comp_agent.name AS zzpic,
            warehouse.name AS lgobe,
            ernam.emailuser AS ernam,
            e.createdby,
            currency.iso_code,
            conrate.multiplyrate AS rate,
            whadd.address1::text || ((( SELECT
                        CASE
                            WHEN whadd.address2 IS NULL THEN ''::character varying
                            ELSE whadd.address2
                        END AS address2))::text) AS warehouseaddress,
            round(d.linenetamt) AS linenetamt,
            round(d.linenetamt * ctax.rate / 100::numeric) AS tax,
            round(d.linenetamt + d.linenetamt * ctax.rate / 100::numeric) AS totalaftertax,
            lcfa.amt AS freight,
            lcia.amt::text AS insurance,
            COALESCE(lcoa.amt, 0::numeric) + COALESCE(lcinstaa.amt, 0::numeric) AS other_charge,
            pyt.name AS paymenterm,
            loc.address1::text || loc.address2::text AS invoiceadd,
            a_1.ad_user_id,
                CASE
                    WHEN (( SELECT (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                                 JOIN m_requisition requix_1 ON rl_1.m_requisition_id = requix_1.m_requisition_id
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id AND requix_1.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                                 JOIN m_requisition requix_1 ON rl_1.m_requisition_id = requix_1.m_requisition_id
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id AND requix_1.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar)) + 1)
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl
                         JOIN m_requisition requix ON rl.m_requisition_id = requix.m_requisition_id
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id AND requix.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar)) > 0 AND (( SELECT (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                                 JOIN m_requisition requix_1 ON rl_1.m_requisition_id = requix_1.m_requisition_id
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id AND requix_1.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                                 JOIN m_requisition requix_1 ON rl_1.m_requisition_id = requix_1.m_requisition_id
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id AND requix_1.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar)) + 1)
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl
                         JOIN m_requisition requix ON rl.m_requisition_id = requix.m_requisition_id
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id AND requix.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar)) IS NOT NULL THEN ((( SELECT "substring"(rl.description::text, (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1, (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                               FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)) AS "substring"
                       FROM m_requisitionline rl
                      WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id))::numeric) * b.priceactual
                    ELSE b.linenetamt
                END AS pr_amount,
            COALESCE(b.z_pic, comp_agent.name) AS z_pic,
            a_1.c_doctype_id,
            a_1.docstatus,
            a_1.iscapex,
            b.description AS req_line_desc,
            whpo.value AS warehouse_po_value,
                CASE
                    WHEN d.isactive = 'N'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
                    WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
                       FROM m_inoutline mioline
                         LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                         LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                         LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
                      WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) = d.qtyordered THEN 'Complete'::text
                    WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
                       FROM m_inoutline mioline
                         LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                         LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                         LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
                      WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) >= d.qtyordered AND (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
                       FROM m_inoutline mioline
                         LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                         LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                         LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
                      WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) <= (d.qtyordered + d.qtyordered * e.tolerance / 100::numeric) THEN 'Complete'::text
                    WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
                       FROM m_inoutline mioline
                         LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                         LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                         LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
                      WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) <= d.qtyordered AND (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
                       FROM m_inoutline mioline
                         LEFT JOIN m_inout mino ON mino.m_inout_id = mioline.m_inout_id
                         LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                         LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
                      WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id AND mino.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar)) IS NOT NULL THEN 'Partial'::text
                    ELSE 'Outstanding'::text
                END AS potogrstatus,
            g.description AS grline_deskripsi,
            parentorg.name AS parent_org_name,
            b.isactive AS req_line_active,
            d.isactive AS po_line_active,
                CASE
                    WHEN d.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar THEN NULL::text
                    ELSE 'Closed'::text
                END AS po_line_active_new,
            ce.c_elementvalue_id,
            (ce2.value::text || '-'::text) || ce2.name::text AS glaccount,
            b.gl_account_id,
            ci.documentno AS invoice_number,
            ci.dateinvoiced AS invoice_date,
            ci.grandtotal AS invoice_amount,
            e.poreference,
                CASE
                    WHEN ci.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
                    WHEN ci.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
                    WHEN ci.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
                    WHEN ci.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
                    WHEN ci.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
                    WHEN ci.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS invoice_doc_status,
            f.z_sj_no AS nosj,
            f.freightamt,
            gj.totalcr AS journal_amt,
            gj.documentno AS journal_docno,
            gj.datedoc AS journal_datedoc,
                CASE
                    WHEN gj.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
                    WHEN gj.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
                    WHEN gj.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
                    WHEN gj.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
                    WHEN gj.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
                    WHEN gj.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS journal_docstatus
           FROM m_requisition a_1
             LEFT JOIN c_elementvalue ce ON a_1.user1_id = ce.c_elementvalue_id
             LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline b ON b.m_requisition_id = a_1.m_requisition_id
             LEFT JOIN c_elementvalue ce2 ON b.gl_account_id = ce2.c_elementvalue_id
             LEFT JOIN ad_user c ON a_1.ad_user_id = c.ad_user_id
             LEFT JOIN m_matchreq mreq ON b.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id
             LEFT JOIN c_orderline d ON
                CASE
                    WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NULL THEN b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
                    WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND mreq.c_orderline_id IS NULL AND b.c_orderline_id IS NOT NULL THEN b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
                    ELSE mreq.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
                END
             LEFT JOIN c_order e ON d.c_order_id = e.c_order_id
             LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location bloca ON e.c_bpartner_location_id = bloca.c_bpartner_location_id
             LEFT JOIN c_location loc ON bloca.c_location_id = loc.c_location_id
             LEFT JOIN c_paymentterm pyt ON e.c_paymentterm_id = pyt.c_paymentterm_id
             LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lcf ON e.c_order_id = lcf.c_order_id AND lcf.m_costelement_id = 1000003::numeric
             LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcfa ON lcf.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcfa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcfa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
             LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lci ON e.c_order_id = lci.c_order_id AND lci.m_costelement_id = 1000016::numeric
             LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcia ON lci.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcia.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcia.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
             LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lco ON e.c_order_id = lco.c_order_id AND lco.m_costelement_id = 1000004::numeric
             LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcoa ON lco.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcoa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcoa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
             LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lcinsta ON e.c_order_id = lcinsta.c_order_id AND lcinsta.m_costelement_id = 1000026::numeric
             LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcinstaa ON lcinsta.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcinstaa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcinstaa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
             LEFT JOIN m_warehouse whpo ON e.m_warehouse_id = whpo.m_warehouse_id
             LEFT JOIN c_currency currency ON e.c_currency_id = currency.c_currency_id
             LEFT JOIN c_conversion_rate conrate ON currency.c_currency_id = conrate.c_currency_id AND conrate.c_currency_id_to = 303::numeric AND conrate.validfrom = a_1.created
             LEFT JOIN ad_user comp_agent ON e.salesrep_id = comp_agent.ad_user_id
             LEFT JOIN m_warehouse warehouse ON e.m_warehouse_id = warehouse.m_warehouse_id
             LEFT JOIN c_location whadd ON warehouse.c_location_id = whadd.c_location_id
             LEFT JOIN ad_user ernam ON e.createdby = ernam.ad_user_id
             LEFT JOIN m_matchpo mpo ON d.c_orderline_id = mpo.c_orderline_id
             LEFT JOIN m_inoutline g ON g.m_inoutline_id = mpo.m_inoutline_id
             LEFT JOIN m_inout f ON f.m_inout_id = g.m_inout_id
             LEFT JOIN ad_org h ON a_1.ad_org_id = h.ad_org_id
             LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo i ON h.ad_org_id = i.ad_org_id
             LEFT JOIN c_location k ON i.c_location_id = k.c_location_id
             LEFT JOIN c_bpartner l ON e.c_bpartner_id = l.c_bpartner_id
             LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location m ON e.c_bpartner_location_id = m.c_bpartner_location_id
             LEFT JOIN c_location n ON m.c_location_id = n.c_location_id
             LEFT JOIN m_product product ON b.m_product_id = product.m_product_id
             LEFT JOIN m_product_category product_category ON product.m_product_category_id = product_category.m_product_category_id
             LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON b.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
             LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo plant ON a_1.ad_org_id = plant.ad_org_id
             LEFT JOIN c_location location ON plant.c_location_id = location.c_location_id
             LEFT JOIN c_tax ctax ON d.c_tax_id = ctax.c_tax_id
             LEFT JOIN ad_org parentorg ON i.parent_org_id = parentorg.ad_org_id
             LEFT JOIN c_invoice ci ON ci.c_order_id = e.c_order_id
             LEFT JOIN z_matchmrars zm ON zm.m_inout_id = f.m_inout_id
             LEFT JOIN gl_journal gj ON gj.gl_journal_id = zm.gl_journal_id
          WHERE
                CASE
                    WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND (( SELECT count(m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id) AS count
                       FROM m_matchreq
                      WHERE m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id)) > 1 THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL
                    WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND (( SELECT count(m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id) AS count
                       FROM m_matchreq
                      WHERE m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id)) = 1 THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL OR e.documentno IS NULL
                    WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NULL THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL OR e.documentno IS NULL
                    ELSE NULL::boolean
                END AND a_1.datedoc::date = CURRENT_DATE
          ORDER BY a_1.documentno, b.line)) a
  GROUP BY ad_client_id, ad_org_id, help, req_no, reqline, req_qty, m_product_id, pr_prod, pr_prod_value, name, value, c_uom_id, datedoc, po_no, orderline, qtyordered, dateordered, datepromised, po_status, po_approval_status, mm_incoterms_id, salesrep_id, gr_no, gr_line, movementdate, movementqty, comp_name, city, vend_name, address1, vend_city, material_no, material_name, purchase_group, pr_price, pr_approval_status, pr_status, price, uomsimbol, description, saving, pr_created, tahun, bulan, leadtime_popr, leadtime_grpo, plant, vendor_id, vendor_id2, bukrs, zzpic, lgobe, ernam, createdby, iso_code, rate, warehouseaddress, linenetamt, tax, totalaftertax, freight, insurance, other_charge, paymenterm, invoiceadd, ad_user_id, pr_amount, z_pic, c_doctype_id, docstatus, iscapex, req_line_desc, warehouse_po_value, potogrstatus, grline_deskripsi, parent_org_name, req_line_active, po_line_active, po_line_active_new, (round(movementqty * price)), (
        CASE
            WHEN tax > 0::numeric THEN round(round(movementqty * price) * 0.1)
            WHEN tax = 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE NULL::numeric
        END), (round(
        CASE
            WHEN tax > 0::numeric THEN round(round(movementqty * price) * 0.1)
            WHEN tax = 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE NULL::numeric
        END + round(movementqty * price))), c_elementvalue_id, glaccount, gl_account_id, invoice_number, invoice_date, invoice_amount, poreference, invoice_doc_status, nosj, freightamt, journal_amt, journal_docno, journal_datedoc, journal_docstatus;


-- adempiere.pr_po_reportview_v3_mv source

CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW adempiere.pr_po_reportview_v3_mv
TABLESPACE pg_default
AS SELECT a.ad_client_id,
    a.ad_org_id,
    a.help,
    a.documentno AS req_no,
    b.line AS reqline,
        CASE
            WHEN mreq.c_rfqlineqty_id IS NOT NULL AND a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND b.c_orderline_id IS NULL OR mreq.m_matchreq_id IS NULL AND a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND b.c_orderline_id IS NULL THEN (( SELECT "substring"(rl.description::text,
                    CASE
                        WHEN (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) = 0 THEN NULL::integer
                        ELSE (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1
                    END,
                    CASE
                        WHEN (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) = 0 THEN NULL::integer
                        ELSE (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)
                    END) AS "substring"
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id))::numeric
            ELSE ( SELECT b.qty
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id)
        END AS req_qty,
    b.m_product_id,
    product.name AS pr_prod,
    product.value AS pr_prod_value,
    product.name,
    product.value,
    b.c_uom_id,
    a.datedoc,
    e.documentno AS po_no,
    d.line AS orderline,
    d.qtyordered,
    e.dateordered,
    e.datepromised,
        CASE
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS po_approval_status,
    e.mm_incoterms_id,
    e.salesrep_id,
    h.name AS comp_name,
    k.city,
    l.name AS vend_name,
    n.address1,
    n.city AS vend_city,
    product.value AS material_no,
    product.name AS material_name,
    product_category.value AS purchase_group,
    b.priceactual AS pr_price,
        CASE
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS pr_approval_status,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    ELSE ( SELECT
                            CASE
                                WHEN e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                                ELSE 'Complete'::text
                            END AS "case")
                END AS "case") AS pr_status,
    d.pricelist AS price,
    uom.x12de355 AS uomsimbol,
    d.description,
        CASE
            WHEN (d.z_savings - d.pricelist) < 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE (d.z_savings - d.pricelist) * d.qtyordered
        END AS saving,
    a.createdby AS pr_created,
    "left"(a.datedoc::character varying::text, 4) AS tahun,
    "substring"(a.datedoc::character varying::text, 6, 2) AS bulan,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN to_char(e.dateordered - (( SELECT wfa.created
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                         JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                         JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                      WHERE wfp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric AND wfn.name::text ~~ '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                      ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                    ELSE to_char(e.dateordered - (( SELECT wfa.created
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                         JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                         JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                      WHERE wfp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric AND wfn.name::text ~~ '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                      ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text)
                END AS to_char) AS leadtime_popr,
    location.city AS plant,
    l.value AS vendor_id,
    l.c_bpartner_id AS vendor_id2,
    h.value AS bukrs,
    comp_agent.name AS zzpic,
    warehouse.name AS lgobe,
    ernam.emailuser AS ernam,
    e.createdby,
    currency.iso_code,
    conrate.multiplyrate AS rate,
    whadd.address1::text || ((( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN whadd.address2 IS NULL THEN ''::character varying
                    ELSE whadd.address2
                END AS address2))::text) AS warehouseaddress,
    round(d.linenetamt) AS linenetamt,
    round(d.linenetamt * ctax.rate / 100::numeric) AS tax,
    round(d.linenetamt + d.linenetamt * ctax.rate / 100::numeric) AS totalaftertax,
    lcfa.amt AS freight,
    lcia.amt AS insurance,
    COALESCE(lcoa.amt, 0::numeric) + COALESCE(lcinstaa.amt, 0::numeric) AS other_charge,
    pyt.name AS paymenterm,
    loc.address1::text || loc.address2::text AS invoiceadd,
    a.ad_user_id,
        CASE
            WHEN mreq.c_rfqlineqty_id IS NOT NULL AND a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND b.c_orderline_id IS NULL OR mreq.m_matchreq_id IS NULL AND a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND b.c_orderline_id IS NULL THEN ((( SELECT "substring"(rl.description::text,
                    CASE
                        WHEN (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) = 0 THEN NULL::integer
                        ELSE (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1
                    END,
                    CASE
                        WHEN (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) = 0 THEN NULL::integer
                        ELSE (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)
                    END) AS "substring"
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id))::numeric) * b.priceactual
            ELSE ( SELECT b.linenetamt
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id)
        END AS pr_amount,
    COALESCE(b.z_pic, comp_agent.name) AS z_pic,
    a.c_doctype_id,
    a.docstatus,
    a.iscapex,
    b.description AS req_line_desc,
    whpo.value AS warehouse_po_value,
        CASE
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id)) = d.qtyordered THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id)) <= d.qtyordered AND (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id)) IS NOT NULL THEN 'Partial'::text
            ELSE 'Outstanding'::text
        END AS potogrstatus,
    ( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
           FROM m_inoutline mioline
             LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
             LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
          WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id) AS totalbpb,
    ( SELECT count(DISTINCT mio.m_inout_id) AS count
           FROM m_inoutline mioline
             LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
             LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
             LEFT JOIN m_inout mio ON mioline.m_inout_id = mio.m_inout_id
          WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id) AS jumlahbpb,
    NULL::text AS postatus,
    b.isactive AS req_line_active,
    d.isactive AS po_line_active
   FROM m_requisition a
     LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline b ON b.m_requisition_id = a.m_requisition_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user c ON a.ad_user_id = c.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_matchreq mreq ON b.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline d ON
        CASE
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NULL THEN b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND mreq.c_orderline_id IS NULL AND b.c_orderline_id IS NOT NULL THEN b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
            ELSE mreq.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
        END
     LEFT JOIN c_order e ON d.c_order_id = e.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location bloca ON e.c_bpartner_location_id = bloca.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location loc ON bloca.c_location_id = loc.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_paymentterm pyt ON e.c_paymentterm_id = pyt.c_paymentterm_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lcf ON e.c_order_id = lcf.c_order_id AND lcf.m_costelement_id = 1000003::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcfa ON lcf.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcfa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcfa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lci ON e.c_order_id = lci.c_order_id AND lci.m_costelement_id = 1000016::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcia ON lci.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcia.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcia.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lco ON e.c_order_id = lco.c_order_id AND lco.m_costelement_id = 1000004::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcoa ON lco.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcoa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcoa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lcinsta ON e.c_order_id = lcinsta.c_order_id AND lcinsta.m_costelement_id = 1000026::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcinstaa ON lcinsta.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcinstaa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcinstaa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse whpo ON e.m_warehouse_id = whpo.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN c_currency currency ON e.c_currency_id = currency.c_currency_id
     LEFT JOIN c_conversion_rate conrate ON currency.c_currency_id = conrate.c_currency_id AND conrate.c_currency_id_to = 303::numeric AND conrate.validfrom = a.created
     LEFT JOIN ad_user comp_agent ON e.salesrep_id = comp_agent.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse warehouse ON e.m_warehouse_id = warehouse.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location whadd ON warehouse.c_location_id = whadd.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user ernam ON e.createdby = ernam.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_matchpo mpo ON d.c_orderline_id = mpo.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org h ON a.ad_org_id = h.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo i ON h.ad_org_id = i.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location k ON i.c_location_id = k.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner l ON e.c_bpartner_id = l.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location m ON e.c_bpartner_location_id = m.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location n ON m.c_location_id = n.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product product ON b.m_product_id = product.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category product_category ON product.m_product_category_id = product_category.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON b.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo plant ON a.ad_org_id = plant.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location location ON plant.c_location_id = location.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_tax ctax ON d.c_tax_id = ctax.c_tax_id
  WHERE
        CASE
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND (( SELECT count(m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id) AS count
               FROM m_matchreq
              WHERE m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id)) > 1 THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND (( SELECT count(m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id) AS count
               FROM m_matchreq
              WHERE m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id)) = 1 THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL OR e.documentno IS NULL
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NULL THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL OR e.documentno IS NULL
            ELSE NULL::boolean
        END
  ORDER BY a.documentno, b.line
WITH NO DATA;


-- adempiere.pr_po_reportview_v3 source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.pr_po_reportview_v3
AS SELECT pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.ad_client_id,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.ad_org_id,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.help,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.req_no,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.reqline,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.req_qty,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.m_product_id,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.pr_prod,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.pr_prod_value,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.name,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.value,

    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.mm_incoterms_id,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.salesrep_id,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.comp_name,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.city,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.vend_name,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.address1,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.vend_city,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.material_no,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.material_name,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.purchase_group,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.pr_price,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.pr_approval_status,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.pr_status,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.price,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.uomsimbol,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.description,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.saving,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.pr_created,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.tahun,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.bulan,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.leadtime_popr,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.plant,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.vendor_id,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.vendor_id2,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.bukrs,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.zzpic,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.lgobe,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.ernam,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.createdby,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.iso_code,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.rate,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.warehouseaddress,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.linenetamt,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.tax,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.totalaftertax,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.freight,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.insurance,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.other_charge,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.paymenterm,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.invoiceadd,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.ad_user_id,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.pr_amount,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.z_pic,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.c_doctype_id,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.docstatus,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.iscapex,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.req_line_desc,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.warehouse_po_value,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.potogrstatus,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.totalbpb,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.jumlahbpb,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.postatus,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.req_line_active,
    pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.po_line_active,
    ce.c_elementvalue_id
   FROM pr_po_reportview_v3_mv
     LEFT JOIN m_requisition mreq ON mreq.documentno::text = pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.req_no::text AND mreq.ad_client_id = pr_po_reportview_v3_mv.ad_client_id
     LEFT JOIN c_elementvalue ce ON mreq.user1_id = ce.c_elementvalue_id
UNION
( SELECT a.ad_client_id,
    a.ad_org_id,
    a.help,
    a.documentno AS req_no,
    b.line AS reqline,
        CASE
            WHEN mreq.c_rfqlineqty_id IS NOT NULL AND a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND b.c_orderline_id IS NULL OR mreq.m_matchreq_id IS NULL AND a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND b.c_orderline_id IS NULL THEN (( SELECT "substring"(rl.description::text,
                    CASE
                        WHEN (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) = 0 THEN NULL::integer
                        ELSE (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1
                    END,
                    CASE
                        WHEN (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) = 0 THEN NULL::integer
                        ELSE (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)
                    END) AS "substring"
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id))::numeric
            ELSE ( SELECT b.qty
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id)
        END AS req_qty,
    b.m_product_id,
    product.name AS pr_prod,
    product.value AS pr_prod_value,
    product.name,
    product.value,
    b.c_uom_id,
    a.datedoc,
    e.documentno AS po_no,
    d.line AS orderline,
    d.qtyordered,
    e.dateordered,
    e.datepromised,
        CASE
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN e.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS po_approval_status,
    e.mm_incoterms_id,
    e.salesrep_id,
    h.name AS comp_name,
    k.city,
    l.name AS vend_name,
    n.address1,
    n.city AS vend_city,
    product.value AS material_no,
    product.name AS material_name,
    product_category.value AS purchase_group,
    b.priceactual AS pr_price,
        CASE
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar THEN 'Draft'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN 'In Progress'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'AP'::bpchar THEN 'Approved'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar THEN 'Closed'::text
            WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            ELSE NULL::text
        END AS pr_approval_status,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    WHEN a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Cancelled'::text
                    ELSE ( SELECT
                            CASE
                                WHEN e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                                ELSE 'Complete'::text
                            END AS "case")
                END AS "case") AS pr_status,
    d.pricelist AS price,
    uom.x12de355 AS uomsimbol,
    d.description,
        CASE
            WHEN (d.z_savings - d.pricelist) < 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE (d.z_savings - d.pricelist) * d.qtyordered
        END AS saving,
    a.createdby AS pr_created,
    "left"(a.datedoc::character varying::text, 4) AS tahun,
    "substring"(a.datedoc::character varying::text, 6, 2) AS bulan,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN to_char(e.dateordered - (( SELECT wfa.created
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                         JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                         JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                      WHERE wfp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric AND wfn.name::text ~~ '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                      ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                    ELSE to_char(e.dateordered - (( SELECT wfa.created
                       FROM ad_wf_process wfp
                         JOIN ad_wf_activity wfa ON wfp.ad_wf_process_id = wfa.ad_wf_process_id
                         JOIN ad_wf_node wfn ON wfa.ad_wf_node_id = wfn.ad_wf_node_id
                      WHERE wfp.record_id = a.m_requisition_id AND wfp.ad_table_id = 702::numeric AND wfn.name::text ~~ '(DocComplete)'::text AND wfp.wfstate = 'CC'::bpchar
                      ORDER BY wfp.created DESC
                     LIMIT 1)), 'dd'::text)
                END AS to_char) AS leadtime_popr,
    location.city AS plant,
    l.value AS vendor_id,
    l.c_bpartner_id AS vendor_id2,
    h.value AS bukrs,
    comp_agent.name AS zzpic,
    warehouse.name AS lgobe,
    ernam.emailuser AS ernam,
    e.createdby,
    currency.iso_code,
    conrate.multiplyrate AS rate,
    whadd.address1::text || ((( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN whadd.address2 IS NULL THEN ''::character varying
                    ELSE whadd.address2
                END AS address2))::text) AS warehouseaddress,
    round(d.linenetamt) AS linenetamt,
    round(d.linenetamt * ctax.rate / 100::numeric) AS tax,
    round(d.linenetamt + d.linenetamt * ctax.rate / 100::numeric) AS totalaftertax,
    lcfa.amt AS freight,
    lcia.amt AS insurance,
    COALESCE(lcoa.amt, 0::numeric) + COALESCE(lcinstaa.amt, 0::numeric) AS other_charge,
    pyt.name AS paymenterm,
    loc.address1::text || loc.address2::text AS invoiceadd,
    a.ad_user_id,
        CASE
            WHEN mreq.c_rfqlineqty_id IS NOT NULL AND a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND b.c_orderline_id IS NULL OR mreq.m_matchreq_id IS NULL AND a.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND b.c_orderline_id IS NULL THEN ((( SELECT "substring"(rl.description::text,
                    CASE
                        WHEN (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) = 0 THEN NULL::integer
                        ELSE (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1
                    END,
                    CASE
                        WHEN (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) = 0 THEN NULL::integer
                        ELSE (( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, ']'::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) - ((( SELECT "position"(rl_1.description::text, '['::text) AS "position"
                           FROM m_requisitionline rl_1
                          WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl_1.m_requisitionline_id)) + 1)
                    END) AS "substring"
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id))::numeric) * b.priceactual
            ELSE ( SELECT b.linenetamt
               FROM m_requisitionline rl
              WHERE b.m_requisitionline_id = rl.m_requisitionline_id)
        END AS pr_amount,
    COALESCE(b.z_pic, comp_agent.name) AS z_pic,
    a.c_doctype_id,
    a.docstatus,
    a.iscapex,
    b.description AS req_line_desc,
    whpo.value AS warehouse_po_value,
        CASE
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id)) = d.qtyordered THEN 'Complete'::text
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id)) <= d.qtyordered AND (( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
               FROM m_inoutline mioline
                 LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
                 LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
              WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id)) IS NOT NULL THEN 'Partial'::text
            ELSE 'Outstanding'::text
        END AS potogrstatus,
    ( SELECT sum(mioline.movementqty) AS sum
           FROM m_inoutline mioline
             LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
             LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
          WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id) AS totalbpb,
    ( SELECT count(DISTINCT mio.m_inout_id) AS count
           FROM m_inoutline mioline
             LEFT JOIN m_matchpo matpo ON mioline.m_inoutline_id = matpo.m_inoutline_id
             LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON orlin.c_orderline_id = matpo.c_orderline_id
             LEFT JOIN m_inout mio ON mioline.m_inout_id = mio.m_inout_id
          WHERE orlin.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id) AS jumlahbpb,
    NULL::text AS postatus,
    b.isactive AS req_line_active,
    d.isactive AS po_line_active,
    ce.c_elementvalue_id
   FROM m_requisition a
     LEFT JOIN c_elementvalue ce ON a.user1_id = ce.c_elementvalue_id
     LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline b ON b.m_requisition_id = a.m_requisition_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user c ON a.ad_user_id = c.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_matchreq mreq ON b.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline d ON
        CASE
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NULL THEN b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND mreq.c_orderline_id IS NULL AND b.c_orderline_id IS NOT NULL THEN b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
            ELSE mreq.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
        END
     LEFT JOIN c_order e ON d.c_order_id = e.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location bloca ON e.c_bpartner_location_id = bloca.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location loc ON bloca.c_location_id = loc.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_paymentterm pyt ON e.c_paymentterm_id = pyt.c_paymentterm_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lcf ON e.c_order_id = lcf.c_order_id AND lcf.m_costelement_id = 1000003::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcfa ON lcf.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcfa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcfa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lci ON e.c_order_id = lci.c_order_id AND lci.m_costelement_id = 1000016::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcia ON lci.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcia.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcia.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lco ON e.c_order_id = lco.c_order_id AND lco.m_costelement_id = 1000004::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcoa ON lco.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcoa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcoa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcost lcinsta ON e.c_order_id = lcinsta.c_order_id AND lcinsta.m_costelement_id = 1000026::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_orderlandedcostallocation lcinstaa ON lcinsta.c_orderlandedcost_id = lcinstaa.c_orderlandedcost_id AND lcinstaa.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse whpo ON e.m_warehouse_id = whpo.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN c_currency currency ON e.c_currency_id = currency.c_currency_id
     LEFT JOIN c_conversion_rate conrate ON currency.c_currency_id = conrate.c_currency_id AND conrate.c_currency_id_to = 303::numeric AND conrate.validfrom = a.created
     LEFT JOIN ad_user comp_agent ON e.salesrep_id = comp_agent.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse warehouse ON e.m_warehouse_id = warehouse.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location whadd ON warehouse.c_location_id = whadd.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user ernam ON e.createdby = ernam.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_matchpo mpo ON d.c_orderline_id = mpo.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org h ON a.ad_org_id = h.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo i ON h.ad_org_id = i.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location k ON i.c_location_id = k.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner l ON e.c_bpartner_id = l.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location m ON e.c_bpartner_location_id = m.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location n ON m.c_location_id = n.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product product ON b.m_product_id = product.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category product_category ON product.m_product_category_id = product_category.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON b.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo plant ON a.ad_org_id = plant.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location location ON plant.c_location_id = location.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_tax ctax ON d.c_tax_id = ctax.c_tax_id
  WHERE
        CASE
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND (( SELECT count(m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id) AS count
               FROM m_matchreq
              WHERE m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id)) > 1 THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NOT NULL AND (( SELECT count(m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id) AS count
               FROM m_matchreq
              WHERE m_matchreq.m_requisitionline_id = mreq.m_requisitionline_id)) = 1 THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL OR e.documentno IS NULL
            WHEN mreq.m_requisitionline_id IS NULL THEN e.documentno IS NOT NULL OR e.documentno IS NULL
            ELSE NULL::boolean
        END AND a.datedoc::date = CURRENT_DATE
  ORDER BY a.documentno, b.line);


-- adempiere.pr_po_reportbaru source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.pr_po_reportbaru
AS SELECT a.ad_client_id,
    a.ad_org_id,
    a.help,
    a.documentno AS req_no,
    b.line AS reqline,
    b.qty AS req_qty,
    b.m_product_id,
    b.c_uom_id,
    a.datedoc,
    e.documentno AS po_no,
    d.line AS orderline,
    d.qtyordered,
    e.dateordered,
    e.datepromised,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN f.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                    ELSE 'Complete'::text
                END AS "case") AS po_status,
    b.priceactual,
    e.mm_incoterms_id,
    e.salesrep_id,
    f.documentno AS gr_no,
    g.line AS gr_line,
    f.movementdate,
    g.movementqty,
    h.name AS comp_name,
    k.city,
    l.name AS vend_name,
    n.address1,
    n.city AS vend_city,
    product.value AS material_no,
    product.name AS material_name,
    product_category.value AS purchase_group,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN e.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                    ELSE 'Complete'::text
                END AS "case") AS pr_status,
    d.pricelist AS price,
    uom.x12de355 AS uomsimbol,
    d.description,
    (d.z_savings - d.pricelist) * d.qtyordered AS saving,
    a.createdby AS pr_created,
    "left"(a.datedoc::character varying::text, 4) AS tahun,
    "substring"(a.datedoc::character varying::text, 6, 2) AS bulan,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN to_char(e.dateordered - a.datedoc, 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                    ELSE to_char(e.dateordered - a.datedoc, 'dd'::text)
                END AS to_char) AS leadtime_popr,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN to_char(f.movementdate - e.dateordered, 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                    ELSE to_char(f.movementdate - e.dateordered, 'dd'::text)
                END AS to_char) AS leadtime_grpo,
    location.city AS plant,
    l.value AS vendor_id,
    h.value AS bukrs,
    comp_agent.name AS zzpic,
    warehouse.name AS lgobe
   FROM m_requisition a
     LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline b ON b.m_requisition_id = a.m_requisition_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user c ON a.ad_user_id = c.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline d ON b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_order e ON d.c_order_id = e.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user comp_agent ON e.salesrep_id = comp_agent.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse warehouse ON e.m_warehouse_id = warehouse.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inout f ON f.c_order_id = e.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inoutline g ON g.m_inout_id = f.m_inout_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org h ON e.ad_org_id = h.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo i ON h.ad_org_id = i.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location k ON i.c_location_id = k.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner l ON e.c_bpartner_id = l.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location m ON e.c_bpartner_location_id = m.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location n ON m.c_location_id = n.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product product ON b.m_product_id = product.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category product_category ON product.m_product_category_id = product_category.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON b.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo plant ON e.ad_org_id = plant.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location location ON plant.c_location_id = location.c_location_id;







-- adempiere.report_klaim_kematian_doc source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.report_klaim_kematian_doc
AS SELECT ci.ad_client_id,
    ci.ad_org_id,
    ci.documentno AS cn_no,
    ci.dateacct AS cn_date,
    exci.documentno AS invoice_no,
    np.documentno AS np_no,
    cb.value AS bp_code,
    cb.name AS bp_name,
    mp.value AS product_code,
    mp.name AS product_name,
    cil.qtyentered AS qty_ekor,
    cil.priceentered AS price,
    cil.linenetamt AS amount,
    mw.name AS warehouse_from_hatchery,
    cb2.name AS ship_to
   FROM c_invoice ci
     JOIN c_invoiceline cil ON cil.c_invoice_id = ci.c_invoice_id
     JOIN c_order co ON co.c_order_id = ci.c_order_id
     JOIN c_invoice exci ON exci.c_invoice_id = ci.z_ex_invoice_id
     JOIN z_tbl_np np ON np.z_tbl_np_id = ci.z_tbl_np_id
     JOIN c_bpartner cb ON cb.c_bpartner_id = ci.c_bpartner_id
     JOIN m_product mp ON mp.m_product_id = cil.m_product_id
     JOIN m_warehouse mw ON mw.m_warehouse_id = co.m_warehouse_id
     JOIN c_bpartner cb2 ON cb2.c_bpartner_id = co.bill_bpartner_id
     JOIN ad_orginfo aoi ON aoi.ad_org_id = ci.ad_org_id
     JOIN ad_orgtype aot ON aot.ad_orgtype_id = aoi.ad_orgtype_id
     JOIN ad_ref_list arl ON arl.value::text = ci.z_cndn_type::text
  WHERE arl.name::text ~~* 'Klaim Kematian DOC'::text AND ci.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND aot.name::text = 'PT'::text AND aot.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar AND aot.ad_client_id = ci.ad_client_id;





-- adempiere.reportdailysalesdocmarketing_view source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.reportdailysalesdocmarketing_view
AS SELECT co.ad_client_id,
    co.ad_org_id,
    co.dateordered,
    co.documentno AS do_do,
    bp.value AS kode_cust,
    bp.name AS mitra,
    bp2.name AS kandang,
    cl.address1 AS alamat,
    au.phone AS telp,
    col.qtyentered AS quantity,
    mp.value AS material_code,
    mw.name AS warehouse,
    mp.name AS item,
    co.box,
    'Null'::text AS ekspedisi,
    co.description AS keterangan,
    co.m_warehouse_id AS warehouse_id,
    "substring"(mp.name::text, '\((.+)\)'::text) AS jenis_vaksin
   FROM c_order co
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bp ON bp.c_bpartner_id = co.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bp2 ON bp2.c_bpartner_id = co.bill_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location bpl ON co.bill_location_id = bpl.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user au ON au.ad_user_id = co.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline col ON col.c_order_id = co.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse mw ON mw.m_warehouse_id = co.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product mp ON mp.m_product_id = col.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location cl ON bpl.c_location_id = cl.c_location_id
  WHERE (co.c_doctypetarget_id IN ( SELECT c_doctype.c_doctype_id
           FROM c_doctype
          WHERE c_doctype.name::text = 'SO DOC FS'::text));


-- adempiere.req_cperp_lspr source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.req_cperp_lspr
AS SELECT a.ad_client_id,
    a.ad_org_id,
    a.help,
    a.documentno AS req_no,
    b.line AS reqline,
    b.qty AS req_qty,
    b.m_product_id,
    b.c_uom_id,
    a.datedoc,
    e.documentno AS po_no,
    d.line AS orderline,
    d.qtyordered,
    e.dateordered,
    e.datepromised,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN a.c_doctype_id <> 1000018::numeric THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                    ELSE 'Complete'::text
                END AS "case") AS po_status,
    e.mm_incoterms_id,
    e.salesrep_id,
    h.name AS comp_name,
    k.city,
    l.name AS vend_name,
    n.address1,
    n.city AS vend_city,
    product.value AS material_no,
    product.name AS material_name,
    product_category.value AS purchase_group,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN e.documentno IS NULL AND a.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN 'Outstanding'::text
                    ELSE 'Complete'::text
                END AS "case") AS pr_status,
    d.pricelist AS price,
    uom.x12de355 AS uomsimbol,
    d.description,
    (d.z_savings - d.pricelist) * d.qtyordered AS saving,
    a.createdby AS pr_created,
    "left"(a.datedoc::character varying::text, 4) AS tahun,
    "substring"(a.datedoc::character varying::text, 6, 2) AS bulan,
    ( SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN to_char(e.dateordered - a.datedoc, 'dd'::text) IS NULL THEN '00'::text
                    ELSE to_char(e.dateordered - a.datedoc, 'dd'::text)
                END AS to_char) AS leadtime_popr,
    location.city AS plant,
    l.value AS vendor_id,
    h.value AS bukrs,
    a.z_pic AS zzpic,
    warehouse.name AS lgobe,
    a.documentno,
        CASE
            WHEN (( SELECT DISTINCT ad_wf_activity.created
               FROM ad_wf_activity
              WHERE "left"(ad_wf_activity.textmsg::text, 10) = a.documentno::text AND a.c_doctype_id = 1000018::numeric AND ad_wf_activity.ad_wf_node_id = 177::numeric)) IS NULL THEN ( SELECT DISTINCT ad_wf_activity.created
               FROM ad_wf_activity
              WHERE "left"(ad_wf_activity.textmsg::text, 13) = a.documentno::text AND a.c_doctype_id = 1000018::numeric AND ad_wf_activity.ad_wf_node_id = 177::numeric)
            ELSE ( SELECT DISTINCT ad_wf_activity.created
               FROM ad_wf_activity
              WHERE "left"(ad_wf_activity.textmsg::text, 10) = a.documentno::text AND a.c_doctype_id = 1000018::numeric AND ad_wf_activity.ad_wf_node_id = 177::numeric)
        END AS created,
    999 AS mandt,
    currency.iso_code,
    c.name AS pr_creator
   FROM m_requisition a
     LEFT JOIN m_requisitionline b ON b.m_requisition_id = a.m_requisition_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user c ON a.ad_user_id = c.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline d ON b.c_orderline_id = d.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN c_order e ON d.c_order_id = e.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse warehouse ON e.m_warehouse_id = warehouse.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org h ON e.ad_org_id = h.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo i ON h.ad_org_id = i.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location k ON i.c_location_id = k.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner l ON e.c_bpartner_id = l.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner_location m ON e.c_bpartner_location_id = m.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location n ON m.c_location_id = n.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product product ON b.m_product_id = product.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category product_category ON product.m_product_category_id = product_category.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON b.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo plant ON e.ad_org_id = plant.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location location ON plant.c_location_id = location.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_currency currency ON e.c_currency_id = currency.c_currency_id;



-- adempiere.rv_fact_acct source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.rv_fact_acct
AS SELECT f.ad_client_id,
    f.ad_org_id,
    f.isactive,
    f.created,
    f.createdby,
    f.updated,
    f.updatedby,
    f.fact_acct_id,
    f.c_acctschema_id,
    f.account_id,
    f.datetrx,
    f.dateacct,
    f.c_period_id,
    f.ad_table_id,
    f.record_id,

    f.amtsourcedr,

    f.amtsourcedr - f.amtsourcecr AS amtsource,
    f.amtacctdr,
    f.amtacctcr,
    f.amtacctdr - f.amtacctcr AS amtacct,
        CASE
            WHEN (f.amtsourcedr - f.amtsourcecr) = 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE (f.amtacctdr - f.amtacctcr) / (f.amtsourcedr - f.amtsourcecr)
        END AS rate,
    f.c_uom_id,
    f.qty,

    p.guaranteedaysmin,
    p.help,
    p.imageurl,
    p.isactive AS m_product_isactive,
    p.isdropship,

    p.isinvoiceprintdetails,
    p.ispicklistprintdetails,
    p.ispurchased,
    p.isselfservice,
    p.issold,
    p.isstocked,
    p.issummary AS m_product_issummary,
    p.isverified,
    p.iswebstorefeatured,
    p.lowlevel,
    p.m_attributeset_id AS m_product_m_attributeset_id,
    p.m_attributesetinstance_id AS m_product_m_asi_id,
    p.m_freightcategory_id,
    p.m_locator_id AS m_product_m_locator_id,
    p.processing AS m_product_processing,
    p.producttype,
    p.r_mailtext_id,
    p.salesrep_id AS m_product_salesrep_id,
    p.s_expensetype_id,
    p.shelfdepth,
    p.shelfheight,
    p.shelfwidth,
    p.sku,
    p.s_resource_id,

    p.unitsperpallet,
    p.updated AS m_product_updated,
    p.updatedby AS m_product_updatedby,
