    f.docstatus
   FROM fact_acct f
     JOIN ad_org o ON f.ad_org_id = o.ad_org_id
     JOIN c_elementvalue ev ON f.account_id = ev.c_elementvalue_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bp ON f.c_bpartner_id = bp.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product p ON f.m_product_id = p.m_product_id;

-- adempiere.rv_fact_simple source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.rv_fact_simple
AS SELECT ad_client_id,
    ad_org_id,
    isactive,
    created,
    createdby,
    updated,
    updatedby,
    c_project_id,
    c_acctschema_id,
    account_id,
    accountvalue,
    accounttype,
    dateacct,
    to_char(dateacct, 'YYYY-MM'::text) AS finyear_mth,
    sum(amtacctdr - amtacctcr) AS amtacct,
    to_char(dateacct, '"FY"YYYY'::text) AS finyear,
        CASE accounttype
            WHEN 'A'::bpchar THEN 'B'::text
            WHEN 'E'::bpchar THEN 'P'::text
            WHEN 'L'::bpchar THEN 'B'::text
            WHEN 'M'::bpchar THEN 'B'::text
            WHEN 'O'::bpchar THEN 'B'::text
            WHEN 'R'::bpchar THEN 'P'::text
            ELSE '9. Unknown'::text
        END AS "case"
   FROM rv_fact_acct
  WHERE postingtype = 'A'::bpchar
  GROUP BY ad_client_id, ad_org_id, isactive, created, createdby, updated, updatedby, c_project_id, c_acctschema_id, account_id, accountvalue, accounttype, dateacct, amtacct;



-- adempiere.rv_storage source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.rv_storage
AS SELECT s.ad_client_id,
    s.ad_org_id,
    s.m_product_id,
    p.value,
    p.name,
    p.description,
    p.upc,
    p.sku,
    p.c_uom_id,
    p.m_product_category_id,
    p.classification,
    p.weight,
    p.volume,
    p.versionno,
    p.guaranteedays,
    p.guaranteedaysmin,
    s.m_locator_id,
    l.m_warehouse_id,
    l.x,
    l.y,
    l.z,
    s.qtyonhand,
    s.qtyreserved,
        CASE
            WHEN COALESCE(lt.isavailableforreservation, 'Y'::bpchar) = 'Y'::bpchar THEN s.qtyonhand
            ELSE 0::numeric
        END - s.qtyreserved AS qtyavailable,
    s.qtyordered,
    s.datelastinventory,
    s.m_attributesetinstance_id,
    asi.m_attributeset_id,
    asi.serno,
    asi.lot,
    asi.m_lot_id,
    asi.guaranteedate,
    daysbetween(asi.guaranteedate::timestamp with time zone, statement_timestamp()) AS shelflifedays,
    daysbetween(asi.guaranteedate::timestamp with time zone, statement_timestamp())::numeric - p.guaranteedaysmin AS goodfordays,
        CASE
            WHEN COALESCE(p.guaranteedays, 0::numeric) > 0::numeric THEN round(daysbetween(asi.guaranteedate::timestamp with time zone, statement_timestamp())::numeric / p.guaranteedays * 100::numeric, 0)
            ELSE NULL::numeric
        END AS shelfliferemainingpct,
    s.isactive AS m_storage_isactive,
    s.updated AS m_storage_updated,
    s.updatedby AS m_storage_updatedby,
    l.ad_org_id AS m_locator_ad_org_id,
    l.isactive AS m_locator_isactive,
    l.isdefault,
    l.priorityno,
    l.value AS m_locator_value,
    p.ad_org_id AS m_product_ad_org_id,
    p.copyfrom AS m_product_copyfrom,
    p.created AS m_product_created,
    p.createdby AS m_product_createdby,
    p.c_revenuerecognition_id,
    p.c_subscriptiontype_id,
    p.c_taxcategory_id,
    p.descriptionurl,
    p.discontinued AS m_product_discontinued,
    p.discontinuedat AS m_product_discontinuedat,
    p.documentnote,
    p.group1,
    p.group2,
    p.help,
    p.imageurl,
    p.isactive AS m_product_isactive,
    p.isbom,
    p.isdropship,
    p.isexcludeautodelivery,
    p.isinvoiceprintdetails,
    p.ispicklistprintdetails,
    p.ispurchased,
    p.isselfservice,
    p.issold,
    p.isstocked,
    p.issummary AS m_product_issummary,
    p.isverified,
    p.iswebstorefeatured,
    p.lowlevel,
    p.m_attributeset_id AS m_product_m_attributeset_id,
    p.m_attributesetinstance_id AS m_product_m_asi_id,
    p.m_freightcategory_id,
    p.m_locator_id AS m_product_m_locator_id,
    p.processing AS m_product_processing,
    p.producttype,
    p.r_mailtext_id,
    p.salesrep_id AS m_product_salesrep_id,
    p.s_expensetype_id,
    p.shelfdepth,
    p.shelfheight,
    p.shelfwidth,
    p.s_resource_id,
    p.unitsperpack,
    p.unitsperpallet,
    p.updated AS m_product_updated,
    p.updatedby AS m_product_updatedby,
    asi.ad_org_id AS m_asi_ad_org_id,
    asi.created AS m_asi_created,
    asi.createdby AS m_asi_createdby,
    asi.description AS m_asi_description,
    asi.isactive AS m_asi_isactive,
    asi.updated AS m_asi_updated,
    asi.updatedby AS m_asi_updatedby
   FROM m_storage s
     JOIN m_locator l ON s.m_locator_id = l.m_locator_id
     LEFT JOIN m_locatortype lt ON l.m_locatortype_id = lt.m_locatortype_id
     JOIN m_product p ON s.m_product_id = p.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_attributesetinstance asi ON s.m_attributesetinstance_id = asi.m_attributesetinstance_id;


-- adempiere.rv_storage_per_product source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.rv_storage_per_product
AS SELECT strg.ad_client_id,
    strg.ad_org_id,
    p.m_product_id,
    p.value,
    p.name,
    p.description,
    p.upc,
    p.sku,
    p.c_uom_id,
    p.m_product_category_id,
    p.classification,
    p.weight,
    p.volume,
    p.versionno,
    p.guaranteedays,
    p.guaranteedaysmin,
    strg.sumqtyonhand,
    l.m_locator_id,
    'N'::text AS kode
   FROM m_product p
     JOIN ( SELECT rv_storage.m_product_id,
            rv_storage.m_locator_id,
            sum(rv_storage.qtyonhand) AS sumqtyonhand,
            rv_storage.ad_client_id,
            rv_storage.ad_org_id
           FROM rv_storage
          GROUP BY rv_storage.m_product_id, rv_storage.m_locator_id, rv_storage.ad_client_id, rv_storage.ad_org_id) strg ON p.m_product_id = strg.m_product_id
     JOIN m_locator l ON strg.m_locator_id = l.m_locator_id
  WHERE strg.sumqtyonhand <> 0::numeric
UNION
 SELECT strg.ad_client_id,
    strg.ad_org_id,
    p.m_product_id,
    p.value,
    p.name,
    p.description,
    p.upc,
    p.sku,
    p.c_uom_id,
    p.m_product_category_id,
    p.classification,
    p.weight,
    p.volume,
    p.versionno,
    p.guaranteedays,
    p.guaranteedaysmin,
    strg.sumqtyonhand,
    l.m_locator_id,
    'Y'::text AS kode
   FROM m_product p
     JOIN ( SELECT rv_storage.m_product_id,
            rv_storage.m_locator_id,
            sum(rv_storage.qtyonhand) AS sumqtyonhand,
            rv_storage.ad_client_id,
            rv_storage.ad_org_id
           FROM rv_storage
          GROUP BY rv_storage.m_product_id, rv_storage.m_locator_id, rv_storage.ad_client_id, rv_storage.ad_org_id) strg ON p.m_product_id = strg.m_product_id
     JOIN m_locator l ON strg.m_locator_id = l.m_locator_id;


-- adempiere.salesorderhe_v source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.salesorderhe_v
AS SELECT co.ad_client_id,
    orginfo.parent_org_id,
    co.ad_org_id,
    orginfo.ad_orgtype_id,
    prd.c_period_id,
    co.c_doctypetarget_id,
    co.docstatus,
    co.documentno,
    co.datepromised,
    co.c_bpartner_id,
    co.c_bpartner_location_id,
    concat(prod.value, ' - ', prod.name) AS product,
    coline.qtyentered,
    coline.priceentered,
    coline.linenetamt
   FROM c_order co
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline coline ON co.c_order_id = coline.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_orginfo orginfo ON co.ad_org_id = orginfo.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_period prd ON co.datepromised >= prd.startdate AND co.datepromised <= prd.enddate AND prd.ad_org_id = orginfo.parent_org_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON coline.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id
  WHERE (co.c_doctypetarget_id IN ( SELECT c_doctype.c_doctype_id
           FROM c_doctype
          WHERE c_doctype.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['SO HE'::character varying::text, 'TO HE'::character varying::text, 'TO HE GP'::character varying::text]))) AND (co.docstatus = ANY (ARRAY['DR'::bpchar, 'CO'::bpchar, 'VO'::bpchar]));


-- adempiere.unsend_to_integration source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.unsend_to_integration
AS SELECT ord.ad_client_id,
    ord.documentno,
    ord.dateordered,
    ord.datepromised,
    bp.name AS bpname,
    bp2.name AS shiptoname,
    bp2.z_kode_theos AS shipto,
    prd.name AS productname,
    prd.value AS productvalue,
    oline.qtyentered,
    ceiling(oline.qtyentered * ord.tolerance / 100::numeric) AS qtytolerance,
    ceiling(oline.qtyentered * ord.tolerance / 100::numeric) * oline.pricelist AS disctolerance,
    ceiling(oline.qtyentered * ord.tolerance / 100::numeric) * oline.pricelist + oline.qtyentered * oline.discountamt AS totaldiscount,
    oline.linenetamt,
    oline.taxamt,
    ord.c_order_id,
    bp2.c_bpartner_id
   FROM c_order ord
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline oline ON ord.c_order_id = oline.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype doc ON ord.c_doctype_id = doc.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bp ON ord.c_bpartner_id = bp.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bp2 ON ord.bill_bpartner_id = bp2.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prd ON oline.m_product_id = prd.m_product_id
  WHERE (doc.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['SO DOC FS'::character varying::text, 'SO DOC FS Trading'::character varying::text])) AND (bp.value::text ~~ '%C9%'::text OR bp.value::text = '199C400001'::text OR bp.value::text = '200C400010'::text) AND ord.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND ord.z_issendinteg <> 'Y'::bpchar AND ord.invoicecustomergenerated = 'Y'::bpchar;


-- adempiere.v_lspo_git source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.v_lspo_git
AS SELECT DISTINCT 920 AS clien,
    (org.name::text || ' '::text) || COALESCE(ce.name, ''::character varying)::text AS comp,
    org.value AS werks,
    b.documentno AS po_no,
    a.line,
    k.value AS sku,
    k.name AS material_name,
    b.updated,
    b.dateordered,
    d.value AS lifnr,
    d.name AS vndnm,
    a.priceentered,
    e.iso_code,
    a.qtyentered,
    f.name AS unit,
    g.name AS createdby,
    n.name AS zzpic,
    l.name AS incoterms,
    m.value AS purchase_g,
    m.value AS ekgrp,
    a.qtyordered,
    round(a.priceentered + ct.rate * a.priceentered / 100::numeric, 0) AS priceppn,
    array_to_string(ARRAY( SELECT mit.documentno
           FROM m_matchpo mmo
             JOIN m_inoutline mie ON mmo.m_inoutline_id = mie.m_inoutline_id
             JOIN m_inout mit ON mie.m_inout_id = mit.m_inout_id
          WHERE mmo.c_orderline_id = a.c_orderline_id), ', '::text) AS grno,
    ( SELECT sum(mmo.qty) AS sum
           FROM m_matchpo mmo
             JOIN m_inoutline mie ON mmo.m_inoutline_id = mie.m_inoutline_id
             JOIN m_inout mit ON mie.m_inout_id = mit.m_inout_id
          WHERE mmo.c_orderline_id = a.c_orderline_id) AS qtydeliv,
        CASE
            WHEN (( SELECT sum(mmo.qty) AS sum
               FROM m_matchpo mmo
                 JOIN m_inoutline mie ON mmo.m_inoutline_id = mie.m_inoutline_id
                 JOIN m_inout mit ON mie.m_inout_id = mit.m_inout_id
              WHERE mmo.c_orderline_id = a.c_orderline_id
             HAVING sum(mmo.qty) <> 0::numeric)) < a.qtyordered THEN 'Partial'::text
            WHEN b.documentno IS NULL THEN ''::text
            WHEN b.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar THEN 'Void'::text
            WHEN b.docstatus = 'CL'::bpchar AND j.documentno IS NULL THEN 'Close'::text
            WHEN j.documentno IS NULL OR j.docstatus = 'RE'::bpchar THEN 'Outstanding'::text
            ELSE 'Complete'::text
        END AS po_status,
    a.updated::date AS c_orderline_date,
    b.updated::date AS c_order_date,
    h.updated::date AS m_matchpo_date,
    i.updated::date AS m_inoutline_date,
    j.updated::date AS m_inout_date,
    a.datepromised::date AS datepromised,
    p.address1,
    p.address2,
    concat(p.address1, ' ', p.address2) AS street,
    p.postal,
    p.c_region_id,
        CASE
            WHEN a.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar THEN ''::text
            ELSE 'L'::text
        END AS loekz,
    a.linenetamt,
    round((a.priceentered + ct.rate * a.priceentered / 100::numeric) * b.z_exchangerate, 2) AS priceplusppn,
    ct.name AS taxcode,
    ( SELECT string_agg(mr.documentno::text, '; '::text) AS string_agg
           FROM m_matchreq mreq
             JOIN m_requisitionline mrl ON mreq.m_requisitionline_id = mrl.m_requisitionline_id
             JOIN m_requisition mr ON mrl.m_requisition_id = mr.m_requisition_id
          WHERE mreq.c_orderline_id = a.c_orderline_id) AS prnumber,
    NULL::text AS prline,
    b.tolerance AS tolerancepo
   FROM c_orderline a
     JOIN c_order b ON a.c_order_id = b.c_order_id
     JOIN m_product k ON a.m_product_id = k.m_product_id
     JOIN ad_org org ON b.ad_org_id = org.ad_org_id
     JOIN m_product c ON a.m_product_id = c.m_product_id
     JOIN c_bpartner d ON b.c_bpartner_id = d.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_currency e ON b.c_currency_id = e.c_currency_id
     JOIN c_uom f ON a.c_uom_id = f.c_uom_id
     JOIN ad_user g ON b.createdby = g.ad_user_id
     JOIN ad_user n ON b.salesrep_id = n.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN m_matchpo h ON a.c_orderline_id = h.c_orderline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inoutline i ON h.m_inoutline_id = i.m_inoutline_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inout j ON i.m_inout_id = j.m_inout_id
     JOIN mm_incoterms l ON b.mm_incoterms_id = l.mm_incoterms_id
     JOIN m_product_category m ON b.m_product_category_id = m.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse o ON a.m_warehouse_id = o.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location p ON o.c_location_id = p.c_location_id
     JOIN c_tax ct ON a.c_tax_id = ct.c_tax_id
     LEFT JOIN c_elementvalue ce ON b.user1_id = ce.c_elementvalue_id
  WHERE b.c_doctype_id = 1001304::numeric
  ORDER BY b.dateordered;

-- adempiere.weekly_distribution_view source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.weekly_distribution_view
AS SELECT co.ad_client_id,
    co.ad_org_id,
    co.c_order_id,
    co.m_warehouse_id,
    ( SELECT mw.name
           FROM m_warehouse mw
          WHERE mw.m_warehouse_id = co.m_warehouse_id) AS warehouse,
    co.documentno,
    co.dateordered,
    to_char(co.dateordered, 'DD-Mon'::text) AS date_char,
    cb.z_kode_fmdc AS area,
    cb.c_bpartner_id,
    COALESCE(( SELECT sum(col.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM c_orderline col
             JOIN m_product mp ON mp.m_product_id = col.m_product_id
          WHERE col.c_order_id = co.c_order_id AND mp.value::text ~~ '%A3%'::text), 0::numeric) AS value_a3,
    COALESCE(( SELECT sum(col.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM c_orderline col
             JOIN m_product mp ON mp.m_product_id = col.m_product_id
          WHERE col.c_order_id = co.c_order_id AND mp.value::text ~~ '%A2%'::text), 0::numeric) AS value_a2,
    COALESCE(( SELECT sum(col.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM c_orderline col
             JOIN m_product mp ON mp.m_product_id = col.m_product_id
          WHERE col.c_order_id = co.c_order_id AND mp.value::text ~~ '%A1%'::text), 0::numeric) AS value_a1,
    COALESCE(( SELECT sum(col.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM c_orderline col
             JOIN m_product mp ON mp.m_product_id = col.m_product_id
          WHERE col.c_order_id = co.c_order_id AND mp.value::text ~~ '%YF%'::text), 0::numeric) AS value_yf,
    COALESCE(( SELECT sum(col.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM c_orderline col
             JOIN m_product mp ON mp.m_product_id = col.m_product_id
          WHERE col.c_order_id = co.c_order_id AND mp.value::text ~~ '%C%'::text), 0::numeric) AS value_c
   FROM c_order co
     JOIN c_bpartner cb ON cb.c_bpartner_id = co.c_bpartner_id
  WHERE co.c_doctypetarget_id = (( SELECT cd2.c_doctype_id
           FROM c_doctype cd2
          WHERE cd2.name::text = 'SO DOC FS'::text)) AND co.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND cb.z_kode_fmdc IS NOT NULL
  ORDER BY co.ad_client_id, co.m_warehouse_id, co.ad_org_id, co.dateordered, cb.z_kode_fmdc;


-- adempiere.z_breeder_daily_rcv_he source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_breeder_daily_rcv_he
AS SELECT vemr.ad_client_id,
    vemr.ad_org_id,
    ao2.name AS compname,
    vemr.branchcode,
    vemr.branchname,
    vemr.categoryname,
    vemr.fromcompcode,
    vemr.fromcompname,
    vemr.fromfarmcode,
    vemr.fromfarmname,
    ao.parent_org_id,
    cp.enddate,
    cbl.name,
    cb.c_bpartner_id,
    cbl.c_bpartner_location_id,
    mw.m_warehouse_id,
    vemr.sonumber,
    vemr.productid,
    vemr.periodedlv,
    vemr.isgenerated,
    ( SELECT round(mp2.pricelist, 0) AS pricelist
           FROM m_pricelist mp,
            m_pricelist_version mpv,
            m_productprice mp2
          WHERE mp.ad_org_id = ao.parent_org_id AND mp.name::text ~~ '%PEMBELIAN%HE%'::text AND mp.m_pricelist_id = mpv.m_pricelist_id AND mpv.m_pricelist_version_id = mp2.m_pricelist_version_id AND mp2.m_product_id = (( SELECT m_product.m_product_id
                   FROM m_product
                  WHERE m_product.value::text = vemr.productid::text)) AND mpv.validfrom = (( SELECT max(mpv1.validfrom) AS max
                   FROM m_pricelist_version mpv1
                  WHERE mpv1.m_pricelist_id = mpv.m_pricelist_id AND mpv1.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar AND mpv1.validfrom < cp.enddate)) AND mpv.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id AND mp.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar AND mpv.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar) AS pricehe,
    sum(vemr.qtyfarm) + sum(vemr.qtyhatch) AS qtyegg
   FROM vw_erp_daily_rcvhe vemr,
    ad_orginfo ao,
    c_period cp,
    c_bpartner_location cbl,
    c_bpartner cb,
    c_bp_group cbg,
    m_warehouse mw,
    ad_org ao2
  WHERE ao.parent_org_id = ao2.ad_org_id AND vemr.status_mr::text = 'PO'::text AND ao.ad_org_id = vemr.ad_org_id AND vemr.c_period_id = cp.c_period_id AND "substring"(cbl.name::text, 1, 3) = vemr.fromfarmcode::text AND cbl.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id AND cbl.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar AND cb.c_bp_group_id = cbg.c_bp_group_id AND cbg.name::text = 'Vendor/Customer Lain - Lain Hubungan Istimewa'::text AND ao2.prefix::text = "substring"(cb.value::text, 1, 3) AND cb.ad_org_id = ao.parent_org_id AND mw.z_org_npwp = ao.ad_org_id AND mw.value::text ~~ '%RR%'::text
  GROUP BY vemr.ad_client_id, vemr.ad_org_id, ao2.name, vemr.branchcode, vemr.branchname, vemr.categoryname, vemr.fromcompcode, vemr.fromcompname, vemr.fromfarmcode, vemr.fromfarmname, ao.parent_org_id, cp.enddate, cbl.name, cb.c_bpartner_id, cbl.c_bpartner_location_id, mw.m_warehouse_id, vemr.sonumber, vemr.productid, vemr.periodedlv, vemr.isgenerated
UNION
 SELECT vemr.ad_client_id,
    vemr.ad_org_id,
    ao2.name AS compname,
    vemr.branchcode,
    vemr.branchname,
    vemr.categoryname,
    vemr.fromcompcode,
    vemr.fromcompname,
    vemr.fromfarmcode,
    vemr.fromfarmname,
    ao.parent_org_id,
    cp.enddate,
    cbl.name,
    cb.c_bpartner_id,
    cbl.c_bpartner_location_id,
    mw.m_warehouse_id,
    vemr.sonumber,
    vemr.productid,
    vemr.periodedlv,
    vemr.isgenerated,
    ( SELECT round(mp2.pricelist, 0) AS pricelist
           FROM m_pricelist mp,
            m_pricelist_version mpv,
            m_productprice mp2
          WHERE mp.ad_org_id = ao.parent_org_id AND mp.name::text ~~ '%PEMBELIAN%HE%'::text AND mp.m_pricelist_id = mpv.m_pricelist_id AND mpv.m_pricelist_version_id = mp2.m_pricelist_version_id AND mp2.m_product_id = (( SELECT m_product.m_product_id
                   FROM m_product
                  WHERE m_product.value::text = vemr.productid::text)) AND mpv.validfrom = (( SELECT max(mpv1.validfrom) AS max
                   FROM m_pricelist_version mpv1
                  WHERE mpv1.m_pricelist_id = mpv.m_pricelist_id AND mpv1.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar AND mpv1.validfrom < cp.enddate)) AND mpv.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id AND mp.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar AND mpv.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar) AS pricehe,
    sum(vemr.qtyfarm) + sum(vemr.qtyhatch) AS qtyegg
   FROM vw_erp_daily_rcvhe vemr,
    ad_orginfo ao,
    c_period cp,
    c_bpartner_location cbl,
    c_bpartner cb,
    c_bp_group cbg,
    m_warehouse mw,
    ad_org ao2
  WHERE ao.parent_org_id = ao2.ad_org_id AND vemr.status_mr::text = 'PO'::text AND ao.ad_org_id = vemr.ad_org_id AND vemr.c_period_id = cp.c_period_id AND "substring"(cbl.name::text, 1, 3) = vemr.fromfarmcode::text AND cbl.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar AND cbl.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id AND cb.c_bp_group_id = cbg.c_bp_group_id AND cbg.name::text = 'Vendor/Customer Pihak Ketiga'::text AND ao2.prefix::text = "substring"(cb.value::text, 1, 3) AND cb.ad_org_id = ao.parent_org_id AND mw.z_org_npwp = ao.ad_org_id AND mw.value::text ~~ '%RR%'::text
  GROUP BY vemr.ad_client_id, vemr.ad_org_id, ao2.name, vemr.branchcode, vemr.branchname, vemr.categoryname, vemr.fromcompcode, vemr.fromcompname, vemr.fromfarmcode, vemr.fromfarmname, ao.parent_org_id, cp.enddate, cbl.name, cb.c_bpartner_id, cbl.c_bpartner_location_id, mw.m_warehouse_id, vemr.sonumber, vemr.productid, vemr.periodedlv, vemr.isgenerated

 SELECT vemr.ad_client_id,
    vemr.ad_org_id,
    ao2.name AS compname,

    vemr.fromcompcode,
    vemr.fromcompname,
    vemr.fromfarmcode,
    vemr.fromfarmname,

                   FROM m_pricelist_version mpv1

    sum(vemr.qtyfarm) + sum(vemr.qtyhatch) AS qtyegg
   FROM vw_erp_daily_rcvhe vemr,
    ad_orginfo ao,

    c_bpartner_location cbl,
    c_bpartner cb,
    c_bp_group cbg,
    m_warehouse mw,
    ad_org ao2
  WHERE ao.parent_org_id = ao2.ad_org_id AND vemr.status_mr::text = 'MEMO'::text AND ao.ad_org_id = vemr.ad_org_id AND vemr.c_period_id = cp.c_period_id AND "substring"(cbl.name::text, 1, 3) = vemr.fromfarmcode::text AND cbl.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar AND cbl.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id AND cb.c_bp_group_id = cbg.c_bp_group_id AND cbg.name::text = 'Cabang'::text AND ao2.prefix::text = "substring"(cb.value::text, 1, 3) AND cb.ad_org_id = ao.parent_org_id AND mw.z_org_npwp = ao.ad_org_id AND mw.value::text ~~ '%RR%'::text
  GROUP BY vemr.ad_client_id, vemr.ad_org_id, ao2.name, vemr.branchcode, vemr.branchname, vemr.categoryname, vemr.fromcompcode, vemr.fromcompname, vemr.fromfarmcode, vemr.fromfarmname, ao.parent_org_id, cp.enddate, cbl.name, cb.c_bpartner_id, cbl.c_bpartner_location_id, mw.m_warehouse_id, vemr.sonumber, vemr.productid, vemr.periodedlv, vemr.isgenerated;



-- adempiere.z_breeder_fms_grpo source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_breeder_fms_grpo
AS SELECT tbla.ad_client_id,
    tbla.ad_org_id,
    tbla.m_inout_id,
    tbla.m_inoutline_id,
    tbla.documentnomr,
    tbla.c_order_id,
    tbla.documentnopo,
    tbla.movementdate,
    tbla.docstatus,
    tbla.categoryname,
    tbla.createdby,
    tbla.name,
    tbla.line,
    tbla.m_product_id,
    tbla.productcode,
    tbla.productname,
    tbla.qtyentered,
    tbla.uom,
    tbla.warehousecode,
    tbla.warehousename,
    tbla.updated,
    COALESCE(NULL::numeric, tbla.qtyentered * tblb.dividerate, tbla.qtyentered) AS qty_conv,
    COALESCE(NULL::character varying, tblb.uom_to, tbla.uom)::text AS uom_conv,
    tblb.multiplyrate,
    tblb.dividerate
   FROM ( SELECT DISTINCT io.ad_client_id,
            io.ad_org_id,
            io.m_inout_id,
            mi.m_inoutline_id,
            io.documentno AS documentnomr,
            ord.c_order_id,
            ord.documentno AS documentnopo,
            io.movementdate,
            io.docstatus,
            prodcat.name AS categoryname,
            io.createdby::text AS createdby,
            part.name,
            mi.line,
            prod.m_product_id,
            prod.value AS productcode,
            prod.name AS productname,
            mi.qtyentered,
            cu.name AS uom,
            mw.value AS warehousecode,
            mw.name AS warehousename,
            io.updated
           FROM c_order ord,
            c_bpartner part,
            m_inout io,
            ad_client ac,
            m_inoutline mi,
            m_product prod,
            m_product_category prodcat,
            c_uom cu,
            m_locator ml,
            m_warehouse mw
          WHERE io.c_order_id = ord.c_order_id AND ord.c_bpartner_id = part.c_bpartner_id AND ord.ad_client_id = ac.ad_client_id AND mi.m_inout_id = io.m_inout_id AND mi.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id AND (prod.m_product_category_id = prodcat.m_product_category_id OR prod.materialtype_id = prodcat.m_product_category_id) AND mi.c_uom_id = cu.c_uom_id AND mi.m_locator_id = ml.m_locator_id AND ml.m_warehouse_id = mw.m_warehouse_id AND io.documentno::text ~~ '%GR%'::text AND (io.docstatus = ANY (ARRAY['CO'::bpchar, 'RE'::bpchar])) AND ac.value::text = 'Poultry Breeder'::text AND (prodcat.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['FEED'::character varying::text, 'MEDICINE'::character varying::text, 'VACCINE'::character varying::text]))) tbla
     LEFT JOIN ( SELECT cuc.m_product_id,
            cu.name AS uom_to,
            cuc.multiplyrate,
            cuc.dividerate
           FROM c_uom_conversion cuc,
            c_uom cu
          WHERE cuc.c_uom_to_id = cu.c_uom_id) tblb ON tbla.m_product_id = tblb.m_product_id;


-- adempiere.z_breeder_fms_grpo_live source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_breeder_fms_grpo_live
AS SELECT tbla.ad_client_id,
    tbla.ad_org_id,
    tbla.m_inout_id,
    tbla.m_inoutline_id,
    tbla.documentnomr,
    tbla.c_order_id,
    tbla.documentnopo,
    tbla.movementdate,
    tbla.docstatus,
    tbla.categoryname,
    tbla.createdby,
    tbla.name,
    tbla.line,
    tbla.m_product_id,
    tbla.productcode,
    tbla.productname,
    tbla.qtyentered,
    tbla.uom,
    tbla.warehousecode,
    tbla.warehousename,
    tbla.updated,
    COALESCE(NULL::numeric, tbla.qtyentered * tblb.dividerate, tbla.qtyentered) AS qty_conv,
    COALESCE(NULL::character varying, tblb.uom_to, tbla.uom)::text AS uom_conv,
    tblb.multiplyrate,
    tblb.dividerate
   FROM ( SELECT DISTINCT io.ad_client_id,
            io.ad_org_id,

            mw.value AS warehousecode,
            mw.name AS warehousename,
            io.updated
           FROM c_order ord,
            c_bpartner part,
            m_inout io,
            ad_client ac,
            m_inoutline mi,
            m_product prod,
            m_product_category prodcat,
            c_uom cu,
            m_locator ml,
            m_warehouse mw
          WHERE io.c_order_id = ord.c_order_id AND ord.c_bpartner_id = part.c_bpartner_id AND ord.ad_client_id = ac.ad_client_id AND mi.m_inout_id = io.m_inout_id AND mi.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id AND (prod.m_product_category_id = prodcat.m_product_category_id OR prod.materialtype_id = prodcat.m_product_category_id) AND mi.c_uom_id = cu.c_uom_id AND mi.m_locator_id = ml.m_locator_id AND ml.m_warehouse_id = mw.m_warehouse_id AND io.documentno::text ~~ '%GR%'::text AND (io.docstatus = ANY (ARRAY['CO'::bpchar, 'RE'::bpchar])) AND ac.value::text = 'Poultry Breeder'::text AND (prodcat.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['FEED'::character varying::text, 'MEDICINE'::character varying::text, 'VACCINE'::character varying::text]))
        UNION
         SELECT DISTINCT io.ad_client_id,
            io.ad_org_id,
            io.m_inout_id,
            mi.m_inoutline_id,
            io.documentno AS documentnomr,
            ord.c_order_id,
            ord.documentno AS documentnopo,
            io.movementdate,
            io.docstatus,

            mw.value AS warehousecode,
            mw.name AS warehousename,
            io.updated
           FROM c_order ord,
            c_bpartner part,
            m_inout io,
            ad_client ac,
            m_inoutline mi,
            m_product prod,
            m_product_category prodcat,
            c_uom cu,
            m_locator ml,
            m_warehouse mw
          WHERE io.c_order_id = ord.c_order_id AND ord.c_bpartner_id = part.c_bpartner_id AND ord.ad_client_id = ac.ad_client_id AND mi.m_inout_id = io.m_inout_id AND mi.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id AND (prod.m_product_category_id = prodcat.m_product_category_id OR prod.materialtype_id = prodcat.m_product_category_id) AND mi.c_uom_id = cu.c_uom_id AND mi.m_locator_id = ml.m_locator_id AND ml.m_warehouse_id = mw.m_warehouse_id AND io.documentno::text ~~ '%GR%'::text AND io.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar AND (io.ad_org_id = ANY (ARRAY[1001391::numeric, 1001516::numeric, 1001495::numeric, 1001400::numeric, 1001525::numeric, 1001515::numeric, 1001405::numeric, 1001407::numeric, 1001524::numeric, 1001494::numeric, 1001389::numeric, 1001402::numeric, 1001403::numeric, 1001404::numeric, 1001401::numeric, 1001406::numeric])) AND ac.value::text = 'Poultry Breeder'::text AND (prodcat.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['FEED'::character varying::text, 'MEDICINE'::character varying::text, 'VACCINE'::character varying::text]))) tbla
     LEFT JOIN ( SELECT cuc.m_product_id,

-- adempiere.z_breeder_gi_others source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_breeder_gi_others
AS SELECT mi.ad_client_id,
    mi.ad_org_id,
    mi.m_inventory_id,
    mi2.m_inventoryline_id,
    mi.documentno,
    mi.description,
    mi.movementdate,
    mw.value AS warehousecode,
    mw.name AS warehousenm,
    cd.name AS doctype,
    mp.value AS prodcode,
    mp.name AS prodname,
    mpc.name AS prodcategory,
    mi2.qtyinternaluse AS qtycperp,
    cu2.name AS uomcperp,
    mi2.qty AS qtyfms,
    cu.name AS uomfms
   FROM m_inventory mi,
    c_doctype cd,
    m_warehouse mw,
    m_inventoryline mi2,
    c_uom cu,
    c_uom cu2,
    m_product mp,
    m_product_category mpc
  WHERE mi.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND mi.c_doctype_id = cd.c_doctype_id AND (cd.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['Goods Issue Others (FEED)'::character varying::text, 'Goods Issue Ekspedisi'::character varying::text])) AND mi.m_warehouse_id = mw.m_warehouse_id AND mi.m_inventory_id = mi2.m_inventory_id AND mi2.m_product_c_uom_id = cu.c_uom_id AND mi2.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id AND mp.c_uom_id = cu2.c_uom_id AND mp.m_product_category_id = mpc.m_product_category_id;

    zgdd.delivery_date AS est_deliverydate,
    zgdd.z_estdeliverytime AS est_deliverytime,
    zgdd.z_group_delivery_doc_by,
    zgdd.z_truckno_ekspedisi,
    zgdd.documentno,
    zgdd.docstatus,
    zgdd.c_doctypetarget_id,
    zgdd.z_actdeliverydate AS act_deliverydate,
    zgdd.z_deliverytime AS act_deliverytime,
    zgdd.z_drivername,
    zgl.line,
    zgl.c_order_id,
    co.documentno AS documentso,
    zgl.c_bpartner_id,
    mp.value,
    mp.name,
    zgl.qtyentered,
    zgl.qtytolerance,
    zgl.z_grade_product
   FROM z_grouping_delivery_doc zgdd,
    z_gdd_line zgl,
    ad_org ao,
    c_order co,
    m_product mp
  WHERE zgdd.docstatus::text = 'CO'::text AND zgdd.z_grouping_delivery_doc_id = zgl.z_grouping_delivery_doc_id AND zgdd.z_unit_id = ao.ad_org_id AND co.c_order_id = zgl.c_order_id AND zgl.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id AND zgl.qtyentered <> 0::numeric
UNION
 SELECT zgdd.z_grouping_delivery_doc_id,
    zgl.z_gdd_line_id,
    ao.ad_org_id,
    ao.description AS branchcode,
    ao.name AS branchname,
    zgdd.delivery_date AS est_deliverydate,
    zgdd.z_estdeliverytime AS est_deliverytime,
    zgdd.z_group_delivery_doc_by,
    zgdd.z_truckno_ekspedisi,
    zgdd.documentno,
    zgdd.docstatus,
    zgdd.c_doctypetarget_id,
    zgdd.z_actdeliverydate AS act_deliverydate,
    zgdd.z_deliverytime AS act_deliverytime,
    zgdd.z_drivername,
    zgl.line,
    zgl.z_splitso_id AS c_order_id,
    zs.documentnosplit AS documentso,
    zgl.c_bpartner_id,
    mp.value,
    mp.name,
    zgl.qtyentered,
    zgl.qtytolerance,
    zgl.z_grade_product
   FROM z_grouping_delivery_doc zgdd,
    z_gdd_line zgl,
    ad_org ao,
    z_splitso zs,
    m_product mp
  WHERE zgdd.docstatus::text = 'CO'::text AND zgdd.z_grouping_delivery_doc_id = zgl.z_grouping_delivery_doc_id AND zgdd.z_unit_id = ao.ad_org_id AND zs.z_splitso_id = zgl.z_splitso_id AND zgl.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id AND zgl.qtyentered <> 0::numeric;


-- adempiere.z_breeder_prodcost source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_breeder_prodcost
AS SELECT ztpc.ad_client_id,
    ztpc.ad_org_id,
    ac.value AS clientname,
    ztpc.m_product_id,
    mp.value AS productcode,
    mp.name AS productname,
    ztpc.m_attributesetinstance_id,
    ma.description AS asi,
    ztpc.m_costelement_id,
    mc2.name AS costelement,
    ztpc.currentcostprice,
    ztpc.periode
   FROM z_tbl_product_cost ztpc,
    ad_client ac,
    m_product mp,
    m_attributesetinstance ma,
    m_costelement mc2
  WHERE ztpc.ad_client_id = ac.ad_client_id AND ztpc.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id AND ztpc.m_attributesetinstance_id = ma.m_attributesetinstance_id AND ztpc.m_costelement_id = mc2.m_costelement_id
  ORDER BY ztpc.periode, mp.name;

-- adempiere.z_breeder_so_docfs source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_breeder_so_docfs
AS SELECT tbl1.ad_client_id,
    tbl1.ad_org_id,
    tbl1.c_order_id,
    tbl1.c_orderline_id,
    COALESCE(NULL::character varying, tbl2.documentnosplit, tbl1.documentno) AS documentno,
    tbl1.c_orderline_id || COALESCE(NULL::character varying, tbl2.documentnosplit, tbl1.documentno)::text AS doclineid,
    tbl1.doctype,
    tbl1.datecreated,
    tbl1.dateordered,
    tbl1.warehousecode,
    tbl1.warehousenm,
    tbl1.warehousecode_to,
    tbl1.warehousenm_to,
    tbl1.box,
    COALESCE(NULL::numeric, tbl2.qtybox, tbl1.qtybox) AS qtybox,
    tbl1.custname,
    tbl1.custcode,
    replace(tbl1.shiptonm::text, ''''::text, ''::text) AS shiptonm,
    tbl1.shiptocode,
    tbl1.shiptocodeinteg,
    replace(tbl1.shiptotelpno::text, ''''::text, ''::text) AS shiptotelpno,
    tbl1.provinsi,
    tbl1.kabupaten,
    tbl1.kecamatan,
    tbl1.desa,
    tbl1.kodedesa,
    tbl1.prodcode,
    tbl1.prodname,
    tbl1.prodvaccine,
    tbl1.grade,
    COALESCE(NULL::numeric, tbl2.qtysplit, tbl1.qty) AS qty
   FROM ( SELECT co.ad_client_id,
            co.ad_org_id,
            co.c_order_id,
            co2.c_orderline_id,
            co.documentno,
            cd.name AS doctype,
            co.datepromised AS dateordered,
            co.dateordered AS datecreated,
            mw.value AS warehousecode,
            mw.name AS warehousenm,
            ( SELECT ao1.description
                   FROM ad_org ao1
                  WHERE ao1.ad_org_id = co.z_org_penerima_id) AS warehousecode_to,
            ( SELECT ao1.name
                   FROM ad_org ao1
                  WHERE ao1.ad_org_id = co.z_org_penerima_id) AS warehousenm_to,
            co.box,
            co.qtybox,
            cb.name AS custname,
            cb.value AS custcode,
            cb2.name AS shiptonm,
            cb2.value AS shiptocode,
            cb2.z_kode_theos AS shiptocodeinteg,
            cbl.phone AS shiptotelpno,
            ( SELECT p.provinsi
                   FROM provinsi p
                  WHERE cbl.provinsi_id = p.provinsi_id) AS provinsi,
            ( SELECT k.kabupaten
                   FROM kabupaten k
                  WHERE cbl.kabupaten_id = k.kabupaten_id) AS kabupaten,
            ( SELECT k2.kecamatan
                   FROM kecamatan k2
                  WHERE cbl.kecamatan_id = k2.kecamatan_id) AS kecamatan,
            ( SELECT d.desa
                   FROM desa d
                  WHERE cbl.desa_id = d.desa_id) AS desa,
            ( SELECT d.kodedesa
                   FROM desa d
                  WHERE cbl.desa_id = d.desa_id) AS kodedesa,
            mp.value AS prodcode,
            mp.name AS prodname,
            mp.description AS prodvaccine,
            mp.classification AS grade,
            co2.qtyordered AS qty
           FROM c_order co,
            c_doctype cd,
            m_warehouse mw,
            c_bpartner cb,
            c_bpartner cb2,
            c_bpartner_location cbl,
            m_product mp,
            c_orderline co2
          WHERE co.c_doctype_id = cd.c_doctype_id AND (cd.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['SO DOC FS'::character varying::text, 'SO DOC EXPORT'::character varying::text, 'DOC Sumbangan'::character varying::text])) AND co.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND mw.m_warehouse_id = co.m_warehouse_id AND co.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id AND co.bill_bpartner_id = cb2.c_bpartner_id AND co.bill_location_id = cbl.c_bpartner_location_id AND co.c_order_id = co2.c_order_id AND co2.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id AND co2.qtyordered <> 0::numeric
        UNION
         SELECT co.ad_client_id,
            co.ad_org_id,
            co.c_order_id,
            co2.c_orderline_id,
            co.documentno,
            cd.name AS doctype,
            co.datepromised AS dateordered,
            co.dateordered AS datecreated,
                CASE
                    WHEN mw.value::text = ANY (ARRAY['2000251'::character varying::text, '2030251'::character varying::text, '2160/251'::character varying::text, '2010/251'::character varying::text, '9090/241'::character varying::text, '2100/251'::character varying::text, '2050/251'::character varying::text, '2170/251'::character varying::text, '2130/251'::character varying::text, '2070/251'::character varying::text, '2120/251'::character varying::text, '2150/250'::character varying::text, '6310/251'::character varying::text, '2080/251'::character varying::text, '2020/251'::character varying::text, '2090/251'::character varying::text, '2060/251'::character varying::text, '2230251'::character varying::text]) THEN (( SELECT "substring"(cbl2.name::text, 1, 40) AS "substring"
                       FROM c_bpartner_location cbl2
                      WHERE cbl2.c_bpartner_location_id = co.z_asal_warehouse_id))::character varying
                    ELSE mw.value
                END AS warehousecode,
            mw.name AS warehousenm,
            ( SELECT ao1.description
                   FROM ad_org ao1
                  WHERE ao1.ad_org_id = co.z_org_penerima_id) AS warehousecode_to,
            ( SELECT ao1.name
                   FROM ad_org ao1
                  WHERE ao1.ad_org_id = co.z_org_penerima_id) AS warehousenm_to,
            co.box,
            co.qtybox,
            cb.name AS custname,
            cb.value AS custcode,
            cb2.name AS shiptonm,
            cb2.value AS shiptocode,
            cb2.z_kode_theos AS shiptocodeinteg,
            cbl.phone AS shiptotelpno,
            ( SELECT p.provinsi
                   FROM provinsi p
                  WHERE cbl.provinsi_id = p.provinsi_id) AS provinsi,
            ( SELECT k.kabupaten
                   FROM kabupaten k
                  WHERE cbl.kabupaten_id = k.kabupaten_id) AS kabupaten,
            ( SELECT k2.kecamatan
                   FROM kecamatan k2
                  WHERE cbl.kecamatan_id = k2.kecamatan_id) AS kecamatan,
            ( SELECT d.desa
                   FROM desa d
                  WHERE cbl.desa_id = d.desa_id) AS desa,
            ( SELECT d.kodedesa
                   FROM desa d
                  WHERE cbl.desa_id = d.desa_id) AS kodedesa,
            mp.value AS prodcode,
            mp.name AS prodname,
            mp.description AS prodvaccine,
            mp.classification AS grade,
            co2.qtyordered AS qty
           FROM c_order co,
            c_doctype cd,
            m_warehouse mw,
            c_bpartner cb,
            c_bpartner cb2,
            c_bpartner_location cbl,
            m_product mp,
            c_orderline co2
          WHERE co.c_doctype_id = cd.c_doctype_id AND (cd.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['SO DOC FS Trading'::character varying::text])) AND co.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND mw.m_warehouse_id = co.m_warehouse_id AND co.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id AND co.bill_bpartner_id = cb2.c_bpartner_id AND co.bill_location_id = cbl.c_bpartner_location_id AND co.c_order_id = co2.c_order_id AND co2.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id AND co2.qtyordered <> 0::numeric) tbl1
     LEFT JOIN ( SELECT zs.c_orderline_id,
            zs.documentnosplit,
            zs.m_product_id,
            mp2.value AS prodcode,
            zs.qtybox,
            zs.qtysplit
           FROM z_splitso zs,
            m_product mp2,
            c_order co,
            c_orderline co2
          WHERE co.c_order_id = co2.c_order_id AND co2.c_orderline_id = zs.c_orderline_id AND zs.m_product_id = mp2.m_product_id) tbl2 ON tbl1.c_orderline_id = tbl2.c_orderline_id AND tbl1.prodcode::text = tbl2.prodcode::text;

                   FROM c_bpartner_location cbl2
                  WHERE cbl2.c_bpartner_location_id = co.z_asal_warehouse_id) AS warehousecode,

           FROM c_order co,

          WHERE co.c_doctype_id = cd.c_doctype_id AND (cd.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['SO DOC FS Trading'::character varying::text])) AND co.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND mw.m_warehouse_id = co.m_warehouse_id AND co.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id AND co.bill_bpartner_id = cb2.c_bpartner_id AND co.bill_location_id = cbl.c_bpartner_location_id AND co.c_order_id = co2.c_order_id AND co2.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id AND co2.qtyordered <> 0::numeric) tbl1
     LEFT JOIN ( SELECT zs.c_orderline_id,
            zs.documentnosplit,
            zs.m_product_id,

            mw2.value AS warehousecode,

           FROM z_splitso zs,
            m_product mp2,
            m_warehouse mw2) tbl2 ON tbl1.c_orderline_id = tbl2.c_orderline_id AND tbl1.warehousecode::text = tbl2.warehousecode::text AND tbl1.prodcode::text = tbl2.prodcode::text;

          WHERE co.c_doctype_id = cd.c_doctype_id AND (cd.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['SO DOC FS Trading'::character varying::text])) AND co.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND mw.m_warehouse_id = co.m_warehouse_id AND co.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id AND co.bill_bpartner_id = cb2.c_bpartner_id AND co.bill_location_id = cbl.c_bpartner_location_id AND co.c_order_id = co2.c_order_id AND co2.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id AND co2.qtyordered <> 0::numeric) tbl1
     LEFT JOIN ( SELECT zs.c_orderline_id,
            zs.documentnosplit,
            zs.m_product_id,
            mp2.value AS prodcode,
            zs.qtybox,
            zs.qtysplit
           FROM z_splitso zs,
            m_product mp2,
            c_order co,
            c_orderline co2
          WHERE co.c_order_id = co2.c_order_id AND co2.c_orderline_id = zs.c_orderline_id AND zs.m_product_id = mp2.m_product_id) tbl2 ON tbl1.c_orderline_id = tbl2.c_orderline_id AND tbl1.prodcode::text = tbl2.prodcode::text;

-- adempiere.z_breeder_so_docfs_x source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_breeder_so_docfs_x
AS SELECT tbl1.ad_client_id,
    tbl1.ad_org_id,
    tbl1.c_order_id,
    tbl1.c_orderline_id,
    COALESCE(NULL::character varying, tbl2.documentnosplit, tbl1.documentno) AS documentno,
    tbl1.c_orderline_id || COALESCE(NULL::character varying, tbl2.documentnosplit, tbl1.documentno)::text AS doclineid,
    tbl1.doctype,
    tbl1.datecreated,
    tbl1.dateordered,
    tbl1.warehousecode,
    tbl1.warehousenm,
    tbl1.warehousecode_to,
    tbl1.warehousenm_to,
    tbl1.box,
    COALESCE(NULL::numeric, tbl2.qtybox, tbl1.qtybox) AS qtybox,
    tbl1.custname,
    tbl1.custcode,
    replace(tbl1.shiptonm::text, ''''::text, ''::text) AS shiptonm,
    tbl1.shiptocode,
    tbl1.c_bpartner_id,
    tbl1.shiptocodeinteg,
    replace(tbl1.shiptotelpno::text, ''''::text, ''::text) AS shiptotelpno,
    tbl1.provinsi,
    tbl1.kabupaten,
    tbl1.kecamatan,
    tbl1.desa,
    tbl1.kodedesa,
    tbl1.prodcode,
    tbl1.prodname,
    tbl1.prodvaccine,
    tbl1.grade,
    COALESCE(NULL::numeric, tbl2.qtysplit, tbl1.qty) AS qty
   FROM ( SELECT co.ad_client_id,
            co.ad_org_id,
            co.c_order_id,
            co2.c_orderline_id,
            co.documentno,
            cd.name AS doctype,
            co.datepromised AS dateordered,
            co.dateordered AS datecreated,
            mw.value AS warehousecode,
            mw.name AS warehousenm,
            ( SELECT ao1.description
                   FROM ad_org ao1
                  WHERE ao1.ad_org_id = co.z_org_penerima_id) AS warehousecode_to,
            ( SELECT ao1.name
                   FROM ad_org ao1
                  WHERE ao1.ad_org_id = co.z_org_penerima_id) AS warehousenm_to,
            co.box,
            co.qtybox,
            cb.name AS custname,
            cb.value AS custcode,
            cb2.name AS shiptonm,
            cb2.value AS shiptocode,
            cb2.c_bpartner_id,
            cb2.z_kode_theos AS shiptocodeinteg,
                CASE
                    WHEN cbl.phone IS NULL THEN ( SELECT DISTINCT au.phone
                       FROM ad_user au
                      WHERE au.c_bpartner_id = cb2.c_bpartner_id
                     LIMIT 1)
                    ELSE cbl.phone
                END AS shiptotelpno,
            ( SELECT p.provinsi
                   FROM provinsi p
                  WHERE cbl.provinsi_id = p.provinsi_id) AS provinsi,
            ( SELECT k.kabupaten
                   FROM kabupaten k
                  WHERE cbl.kabupaten_id = k.kabupaten_id) AS kabupaten,
            ( SELECT k2.kecamatan
                   FROM kecamatan k2
                  WHERE cbl.kecamatan_id = k2.kecamatan_id) AS kecamatan,
            ( SELECT d.desa
                   FROM desa d
                  WHERE cbl.desa_id = d.desa_id) AS desa,
            ( SELECT d.kodedesa
                   FROM desa d
                  WHERE cbl.desa_id = d.desa_id) AS kodedesa,
            mp.value AS prodcode,
            mp.name AS prodname,
            mp.description AS prodvaccine,
            mp.classification AS grade,
            co2.qtyordered AS qty
           FROM c_order co,
            c_doctype cd,
            m_warehouse mw,
            c_bpartner cb,
            c_bpartner cb2,
            c_bpartner_location cbl,
            m_product mp,
            c_orderline co2
          WHERE co.c_doctype_id = cd.c_doctype_id AND (cd.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['SO DOC FS'::character varying::text, 'SO DOC EXPORT'::character varying::text, 'DOC Sumbangan'::character varying::text])) AND co.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND mw.m_warehouse_id = co.m_warehouse_id AND co.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id AND co.bill_bpartner_id = cb2.c_bpartner_id AND co.bill_location_id = cbl.c_bpartner_location_id AND co.c_order_id = co2.c_order_id AND co2.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id AND co2.qtyordered <> 0::numeric
        UNION
         SELECT co.ad_client_id,
            co.ad_org_id,
            co.c_order_id,
            co2.c_orderline_id,
            co.documentno,
            cd.name AS doctype,
            co.datepromised AS dateordered,
            co.dateordered AS datecreated,
                CASE
                    WHEN mw.value::text = ANY (ARRAY['2000251'::character varying::text, '2030251'::character varying::text, '2160/251'::character varying::text, '2010/251'::character varying::text, '9090/241'::character varying::text, '2100/251'::character varying::text, '2050/251'::character varying::text, '2170/251'::character varying::text, '2130/251'::character varying::text, '2070/251'::character varying::text, '2120/251'::character varying::text, '2150/250'::character varying::text, '6310/251'::character varying::text, '2080/251'::character varying::text, '2020/251'::character varying::text, '2090/251'::character varying::text, '2060/251'::character varying::text]) THEN (( SELECT "substring"(cbl2.name::text, 1, 40) AS "substring"
                       FROM c_bpartner_location cbl2
                      WHERE cbl2.c_bpartner_location_id = co.z_asal_warehouse_id))::character varying
                    ELSE mw.value
                END AS warehousecode,
            mw.name AS warehousenm,

                     LIMIT 1)
                    ELSE cbl.phone

    cd.name AS doctype,
    co.datepromised AS dateordered,
    co.dateordered AS datecreated,
    mw.value AS warehousecode,
    mw.name AS warehousenm,
    co.box,
    co.qtybox,
    cb.name AS custname,
    cb.value AS custcode,
    cbl.name AS shiptonm,
    cbl.phone AS shiptotelpno,
    ( SELECT p.provinsi
           FROM provinsi p
          WHERE cbl.provinsi_id = p.provinsi_id) AS provinsi,
    ( SELECT k.kabupaten
           FROM kabupaten k
          WHERE cbl.kabupaten_id = k.kabupaten_id) AS kabupaten,
    ( SELECT k2.kecamatan
           FROM kecamatan k2
          WHERE cbl.kecamatan_id = k2.kecamatan_id) AS kecamatan,
    ( SELECT d.desa
           FROM desa d
          WHERE cbl.desa_id = d.desa_id) AS desa,
    ( SELECT d.kodedesa
           FROM desa d
          WHERE cbl.desa_id = d.desa_id) AS kodedesa,
    mp.value AS prodcode,
    mp.name AS prodname,
    mp.description AS prodvaccine,
    mp.classification AS grade,
    co2.qtyordered AS qty
   FROM c_order co,

-- adempiere.z_breeder_trf_item source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_breeder_trf_item
AS SELECT DISTINCT tbla.ad_client_id,
    tbla.ad_org_id,
    tbla.hdr_id,
    tbla.dtl_id,
    tbla.documentno,
    tbla.description,
    tbla.movementdate,
    tbla.doctype,
    tbla.docstatus,
    tbla.warehousecode,
    tbla.warehousenm,
    tbla.documentgr,
    tbla.docstatusgr,
    tbla.warehousecode_to,
    tbla.warehousenm_to,
    tbla.m_product_id,
    tbla.prodcode,
    tbla.prodname,
    tbla.prodcategory,
    tbla.qty,
    tbla.uom,
    COALESCE(NULL::numeric, tbla.qty * tblb.dividerate, tbla.qty) AS qty_conv,
    COALESCE(NULL::character varying, tblb.uom_to, tbla.uom)::text AS uom_conv,
    tblb.multiplyrate,
    tblb.dividerate
   FROM ( SELECT DISTINCT mm.ad_client_id,
            mm.ad_org_id,
            mm.m_movement_id AS hdr_id,
            mm2.m_movementline_id AS dtl_id,
            mm.documentno,
            mm.description,
            mm.movementdate,
            cd.name AS doctype,
            mm.docstatus,
            ml.value AS warehousecode,
            mw.name AS warehousenm,
            mm3.documentno AS documentgr,
            mm3.docstatus AS docstatusgr,
            ml2.value AS warehousecode_to,
            mw2.name AS warehousenm_to,
            mp.m_product_id,
            mp.value AS prodcode,
            mp.name AS prodname,
            mpc.name AS prodcategory,
            mm2.movementqty AS qty,
            cu.name AS uom
           FROM m_movement mm,
            m_movementconfirm mm3,
            c_doctype cd,
            m_movementline mm2,
            m_locator ml,
            m_warehouse mw,
            m_product mp,
            m_product_category mpc,
            c_uom cu,
            m_locator ml2,
            m_warehouse mw2,
            ad_org ao
          WHERE mm.m_movementconfirm_id IS NOT NULL AND mm.c_doctype_id = cd.c_doctype_id AND mm.m_movement_id = mm2.m_movement_id AND mm2.m_locator_id = ml.m_locator_id AND ml.m_warehouse_id = mw.m_warehouse_id AND mm2.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id AND mp.m_product_category_id = mpc.m_product_category_id AND mm2.c_uom_id = cu.c_uom_id AND mm2.m_locatorto_id = ml2.m_locator_id AND ml2.m_warehouse_id = mw2.m_warehouse_id AND mm.m_movementconfirm_id = mm3.m_movementconfirm_id AND mm.ad_org_id = ao.ad_org_id AND (ao.z_product_line::text = ANY (ARRAY['511440'::character varying::text, '3121'::character varying::text])) AND ao.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar
        UNION
         SELECT DISTINCT mi.ad_client_id,
            mi.ad_org_id,
            mi.m_inout_id AS hdr_id,
            mi2.m_inoutline_id AS dtl_id,
            mi.documentno,
            mi.description,
            mi.movementdate,
            cd.name AS doctype,
            mi.docstatus,
            mw.value AS warehousecode,
            mw.name AS warehousenm,
            '-'::text AS documentgr,
            '-'::text AS docstatusgr,
            cb.name AS warehousecode_to,
            cbl.name AS warehousenm_to,
            mp.m_product_id,
            mp.value AS prodcode,
            mp.name AS prodname,
            mpc.name AS prodcategory,
            mi2.qtyentered AS qty,
            cu.name AS uom
           FROM m_inout mi,
            c_doctype cd,
            c_bpartner cb,
            m_warehouse mw,
            c_bpartner_location cbl,
            m_inoutline mi2,
            m_product mp,
            m_product_category mpc,
            c_uom cu,
            ad_org ao
          WHERE mi.c_doctype_id = cd.c_doctype_id AND (cd.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['DO FEED'::character varying::text, 'DO TO FEED'::character varying::text, 'DO VMD'::character varying::text, 'DO TO VMD'::character varying::text, 'DO Box/Karton'::character varying::text])) AND mi.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id AND mi.m_warehouse_id = mw.m_warehouse_id AND mi.c_bpartner_location_id = cbl.c_bpartner_location_id AND mi.m_inout_id = mi2.m_inout_id AND mi2.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id AND mp.m_product_category_id = mpc.m_product_category_id AND mi2.c_uom_id = cu.c_uom_id AND mi.ad_org_id = ao.ad_org_id AND (ao.z_product_line::text = ANY (ARRAY['511440'::character varying::text, '3121'::character varying::text])) AND ao.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar
        UNION
         SELECT co.ad_client_id,
            co.ad_org_id,
            co.c_order_id AS hdr_id,
            co2.c_orderline_id AS dtl_id,
            co.documentno,
            co.description,
            co.dateordered,
            cd.name AS doctype,
            co.docstatus,
            mw.value AS warehousecode,
            mw.name AS warehousenm,
            '-'::text AS documentgr,
            '-'::text AS docstatusgr,
            cb.name AS warehousecode_to,
            cbl.name AS warehousecnm_to,
            mp.m_product_id,
            mp.value AS prodcode,
            mp.name AS prodname,
            mpc.name AS prodcategory,
            co2.qtyentered AS qty,
            cu.name AS uom
           FROM c_order co,
            c_orderline co2,
            c_doctype cd,
            c_bpartner cb,
            m_warehouse mw,
            c_bpartner_location cbl,
            m_product mp,
            m_product_category mpc,
            c_uom cu,
            ad_org ao
          WHERE co.c_doctype_id = cd.c_doctype_id AND (cd.name::text = ANY (ARRAY['TO FEED'::character varying::text, 'TO VMD'::character varying::text])) AND co.c_order_id = co2.c_order_id AND co.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id AND co.m_warehouse_id = mw.m_warehouse_id AND co.c_bpartner_location_id = cbl.c_bpartner_location_id AND co2.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id AND mp.m_product_category_id = mpc.m_product_category_id AND co2.c_uom_id = cu.c_uom_id AND co.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND co.ad_org_id = ao.ad_org_id AND (ao.z_product_line::text = ANY (ARRAY['511440'::character varying::text, '3121'::character varying::text])) AND ao.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar) tbla
     LEFT JOIN ( SELECT DISTINCT cuc.m_product_id,
            cu.name AS uom_to,
            cuc.multiplyrate,
            cuc.dividerate
           FROM c_uom_conversion cuc,
            c_uom cu
          WHERE cuc.c_uom_to_id = cu.c_uom_id) tblb ON tbla.m_product_id = tblb.m_product_id
  WHERE tbla.docstatus = ANY (ARRAY['CO'::bpchar, 'RE'::bpchar]);

-- adempiere.z_cip_project source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_cip_project
AS SELECT f.ad_client_id,
    f.ad_org_id,
    f.isactive,
    f.created,
    f.createdby,
    f.updated,
    f.updatedby,
    f.fact_acct_id,
    f.c_acctschema_id,
    f.account_id,
    f.datetrx,
    f.dateacct,
    f.c_period_id,
    f.ad_table_id,
    f.record_id,
    f.line_id,
    f.gl_category_id,
    f.gl_budget_id,
    f.c_tax_id,
    f.m_locator_id,
    f.postingtype,
    f.c_currency_id,
    f.amtsourcedr,
    f.amtsourcecr,
    f.amtsourcedr - f.amtsourcecr AS amtsource,
    f.amtacctdr,
    f.amtacctcr,
    f.amtacctdr - f.amtacctcr AS amtacct,
        CASE
            WHEN (f.amtsourcedr - f.amtsourcecr) = 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE (f.amtacctdr - f.amtacctcr) / (f.amtsourcedr - f.amtsourcecr)
        END AS rate,
    f.c_uom_id,
    f.qty,
    f.m_product_id,
    f.c_bpartner_id,
    f.ad_orgtrx_id,
    f.c_locfrom_id,
    f.c_locto_id,
    f.c_salesregion_id,
    f.c_project_id,
    f.c_campaign_id,
    f.c_activity_id,
    f.user1_id,
    f.user2_id,
    f.a_asset_id,
    f.description,
    o.value AS orgvalue,
    o.name AS orgname,
    ev.value AS accountvalue,
    ev.name,
    ev.accounttype,
    bp.value AS bpartnervalue,
    bp.name AS bpname,
    bp.c_bp_group_id,
    p.value AS productvalue,
    p.name AS productname,
    p.upc,
    p.m_product_category_id,
    f.c_projectphase_id,
    f.c_projecttask_id,
    f.c_subacct_id,
    f.userelement1_id,
    f.userelement2_id,
    o.description AS ad_org_description,
    o.isactive AS ad_org_isactive,
    ev.ad_org_id AS c_elementvalue_ad_org_id,
    ev.c_bankaccount_id,
    ev.c_currency_id AS c_elementvalue_c_currency_id,
    ev.c_element_id,
    ev.c_elementvalue_id,
    ev.description AS c_elementvalue_description,
    ev.isactive AS c_elementvalue_isactive,
    ev.isbankaccount,
    ev.isforeigncurrency,
    ev.issummary AS c_elementvalue_issummary,
    bp.acqusitioncost AS c_bp_acqusitioncost,
    bp.actuallifetimevalue AS c_bp_actuallifetimevalue,
    bp.ad_language AS c_bp_ad_language,
    bp.ad_orgbp_id AS c_bp_ad_orgbp_id,
    bp.ad_org_id AS c_bp_ad_org_id,
    bp.bpartner_parent_id AS c_bp_bpartner_parent_id,
    bp.c_dunning_id AS c_bp_c_dunning_id,
