     LEFT JOIN c_project cp ON cp.c_project_id = f.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype cd ON cd.c_doctype_id = cp.c_doctypetarget_id
  WHERE cp.c_doctypetarget_id = 1001406::numeric AND (ev.value::text = ANY (ARRAY['280018'::character varying::text, '280019'::character varying::text]));


-- adempiere.z_feed_in_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_feed_in_reportview
AS SELECT DISTINCT tas.ad_client_id,
    tas.ad_org_id,
    tas.m_warehouse_id AS m_warehouse_to_id,
    tas.z_tbl_alokasistocktransfer_id,
    mc.documentno,
    tas.datedoc,
    tas.updatedby,
    tas.updated,
    tas.createdby,
    tas.created,
    tat.m_warehouse_to_id AS m_warehouse_id,
    whto.value AS wh_value,
    whto.name AS wh_name,
    wh.value AS whto_value,
    wh.name AS whto_name,
    mc.isapproved,
    prod.value AS prod_value,
    btrim(upper(btrim(prod.name::text, ' '::text)), 'UNVALUATED'::text) AS prod_name,
    prodc.name AS prodcat_name,
        CASE
            WHEN prodc.name::text ~~* '%feed%'::text AND uom.name::text ~~* '%kg%'::text THEN tat.qtyentered * (( SELECT cuc.dividerate
               FROM c_uom_conversion cuc
              WHERE cuc.c_uom_id = tat.c_uom_id AND cuc.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id))
            ELSE tat.qtyentered
        END AS qtyentered,
    tat.qtyentered AS qtyentered_asli,
        CASE
            WHEN prodc.name::text ~~* '%feed%'::text AND uom.name::text ~~* '%kg%'::text THEN 'ZAK'::character varying
            ELSE uom.name
        END AS uom_name,
    uom.name AS uom_name_asli,
    COALESCE(( SELECT kos.currentcostprice
           FROM m_product produk
             JOIN m_cost kos ON kos.m_product_id = produk.m_product_id
             JOIN m_costelement co ON co.m_costelement_id = kos.m_costelement_id
          WHERE produk.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id AND kos.m_attributesetinstance_id = mufl.m_attributesetinstance_id AND co.name::text = 'Average PO'::text AND produk.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id
         LIMIT 1), 0::numeric) AS map_price,
    loc.m_locator_id,
    loc.value AS loc_value,
    ( SELECT tabukpet.m_warehouse_id
           FROM z_tbl_buku_peternak tabukpet
          WHERE tabukpet.z_locator_feed_id = loc.m_locator_id OR tabukpet.z_locator_vmd_id = loc.m_locator_id OR tabukpet.z_locator_central_id = loc.m_locator_id
         LIMIT 1) AS warehouse_parent_id,
    yus_creat.name AS created_name,
    yus_updt.name AS updated_name,
    mc.docstatus AS docstatus_mc,
    muf.m_movement_id,
    tat.m_movementconfirm_id
   FROM z_tbl_alokasistocktransfer tas
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_alokasistocktfln tat ON tat.z_tbl_alokasistocktransfer_id = tas.z_tbl_alokasistocktransfer_id
     LEFT JOIN m_locator loc ON loc.m_locator_id = tat.m_locatorto_id
     JOIN m_movementconfirm mc ON mc.m_movementconfirm_id = tat.m_movementconfirm_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse whto ON whto.m_warehouse_id = tat.m_warehouse_to_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse wh ON wh.m_warehouse_id = tas.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON prod.m_product_id = tat.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category prodc ON prodc.m_product_category_id = prod.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON uom.c_uom_id = tat.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN m_movement muf ON muf.m_movement_id = tat.m_movement_id
     LEFT JOIN m_movementline mufl ON mufl.m_movement_id = muf.m_movement_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user yus_creat ON yus_creat.ad_user_id = tas.createdby
     LEFT JOIN ad_user yus_updt ON yus_updt.ad_user_id = tas.updatedby
  WHERE mc.z_issent = 'N'::bpchar AND (tas.ad_org_id IN ( SELECT bp.ad_org_id
           FROM z_parameter_reporting pr
             JOIN z_parameter_report_bp bp ON bp.z_parameter_reporting_id = pr.z_parameter_reporting_id
          WHERE pr.name::text = 'Function Swine Breeder'::text)) AND tas.docstatus::text = 'CO'::text AND (mc.docstatus = 'IN'::bpchar OR mc.docstatus = 'DR'::bpchar);


-- adempiere.z_fmdc_medicine_pir_v source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_fmdc_medicine_pir_v
AS SELECT zze.ad_client_id,
    ztbp.ad_org_id,
    zze.documentno,
    zze.c_project_id,
    "left"(ao.value::text, 2) AS areid,
    "right"(ao.value::text, 3) AS brcid,
    cb.z_kode_fmdc AS ztsid,
    ztbp.z_kode_fmdc AS famid,
    zze.delivery_date AS recdt,
    "right"(zze.documentno::text, 7) AS donum,
    mp.value AS proid,
    mp.name AS pronm,
    zze.qty AS quant,
    ("left"(ao.value::text, 2) || "right"(ao.value::text, 3)) || ztbp.z_kode_fmdc::text AS zmfid,
    mpc.name
   FROM z_zsd_email zze
     LEFT JOIN m_product mp ON zze.m_product_id = mp.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category mpc ON zze.m_product_category_id = mpc.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category mpc2 ON mpc.m_product_category_parent_id = mpc2.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak ztbp ON zze.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id = ztbp.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org ao ON ztbp.ad_org_id = ao.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user au ON ztbp.salesrep_id = au.ad_user_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner cb ON au.c_bpartner_id = cb.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_project cp ON zze.c_project_id = cp.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype cdchick ON cp.c_doctypetarget_id = cdchick.c_doctype_id
  WHERE (lower(mpc.name::text) ~~ 'medicine'::text OR lower(mpc2.name::text) ~~ 'medicine'::text) AND zze.processed = 'Y'::bpchar AND lower(cdchick.name::text) ~~ 'cycle pir'::text;


-- adempiere.z_fmdc_p4_v source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_fmdc_p4_v
AS SELECT p4.ad_client_id,
    p4.ad_org_id,
    p4.c_project_id,
    "left"(ao.value::text, 2) AS areid,
    "right"(ao.value::text, 3) AS brcid,
    ztbp.z_kode_fmdc AS famid,
    p4.documentno AS nop4,
    p4.datedoc AS tglp4,
    ( SELECT COALESCE(exinv.dateinvoiced, ztpf.tgl_faktur) AS "coalesce"
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
             JOIN c_invoice inv ON ztpf.no_faktur_p4::text = inv.documentno::text AND inv.ad_client_id = ztpf.ad_client_id
             LEFT JOIN c_invoice exinv ON inv.z_ex_invoice_id = exinv.c_invoice_id
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'doc'::text
          ORDER BY ztpf.tgl_faktur
         LIMIT 1) AS tgldocin,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
             JOIN m_product mp ON ztpf.productvalue::text = mp.value::text
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND mp.classification::text ~~ 'A'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'doc'::text) AS docgradea,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
             JOIN m_product mp ON ztpf.productvalue::text = mp.value::text
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND mp.classification::text ~~ 'B'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'doc'::text) AS docgradeb,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
             JOIN m_product mp ON ztpf.productvalue::text = mp.value::text
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND mp.classification::text ~~ 'C'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'doc'::text) AS docgradec,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
             JOIN m_product mp ON ztpf.productvalue::text = mp.value::text
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND lower(mp.classification::text) ~~ 'ext'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'doc'::text) AS docgradeext,
    ( SELECT ztpr.totalqtypenjualanekor
           FROM z_tbl_p4_ringkasan ztpr
          WHERE ztpr.c_project_id = cp.c_project_id AND ztpr.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id) AS sppaekor,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND ztpf.productvalue::text ~~ 'S00'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) ~~ 'ar inv sapronak feed'::text) AS feeds00,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND ztpf.productvalue::text ~~ 'S11'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) ~~ 'ar inv sapronak feed'::text) AS feeds11,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND ztpf.productvalue::text ~~ 'S12%'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) ~~ 'ar inv sapronak feed'::text) AS feeds12,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND ztpf.productvalue::text ~~ 'S00%'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) !~~ 'ar inv sapronak feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) ~~ '%credit%'::text) AS trfoutfeeds00,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND ztpf.productvalue::text ~~ 'S11%'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) !~~ 'ar inv sapronak feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) ~~ '%credit%'::text) AS trfoutfeeds11,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND ztpf.productvalue::text ~~ 'S12%'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) !~~ 'ar inv sapronak feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) ~~ '%credit%'::text) AS trfoutfeeds12,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND ztpf.productvalue::text ~~ 'S00%'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) !~~ 'ar inv sapronak feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) ~~ '%debit%'::text) AS trfinfeeds00,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND ztpf.productvalue::text ~~ 'S11%'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) !~~ 'ar inv sapronak feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) ~~ '%debit%'::text) AS trfinfeeds11,
    ( SELECT sum(ztpf.qtyentered) AS sum
           FROM z_tbl_p4_faktur ztpf
          WHERE ztpf.c_project_id = p4.c_project_id AND ztpf.ad_client_id = p4.ad_client_id AND ztpf.productvalue::text ~~ 'S12%'::text AND lower(ztpf.kategoriname_p4::text) ~~ 'feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) !~~ 'ar inv sapronak feed'::text AND lower(ztpf.doctypename_p4::text) ~~ '%debit%'::text) AS trfinfeeds12,
    p4.dateprinted AS tglprint,
    p4.isprinted
   FROM z_tbl_p4 p4
     LEFT JOIN c_project cp ON p4.c_project_id = cp.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak ztbp ON cp.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id = ztbp.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org ao ON p4.ad_org_id = ao.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype cdchick ON cp.c_doctypetarget_id = cdchick.c_doctype_id
  WHERE lower(cdchick.name::text) ~~ 'cycle pir'::text;


-- adempiere.z_inventory_list_reportview_v2 source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_inventory_list_reportview_v2
AS SELECT orderkey,
    ad_client_id,
    ad_org_id,
    ceklist_peternak_pig,
    m_warehouse_id,
    wh_value,
    wh_name,
    wh_prod,
    prod_name,
    prod_value,
    prod_qty,
    prod_cate,
    m_attributesetinstance_id,
    uom_name_det,
    warehouse_parent_id,
    COALESCE(( SELECT kos.currentcostprice
           FROM m_product produk
             JOIN m_cost kos ON kos.m_product_id = produk.m_product_id
             JOIN m_costelement co ON co.m_costelement_id = kos.m_costelement_id
          WHERE produk.m_product_id = tabel.wh_prod AND kos.m_attributesetinstance_id = tabel.m_attributesetinstance_id AND co.name::text = 'Average PO'::text
         LIMIT 1), 0::numeric) AS map_price,
        CASE
            WHEN prod_cate::text ~~* 'feed'::text THEN 'ZAK'::character varying
            ELSE uom_name_det
        END AS uom_name
   FROM ( SELECT DISTINCT 1 AS orderkey,
            tbp.ad_client_id,
            tbp.ad_org_id,
            tbp.name AS ceklist_peternak_pig,
            wh.m_warehouse_id,
            wh.value AS wh_value,
            wh.name AS wh_name,
            stg.m_product_id AS wh_prod,
            replace(upper(prod.name::text), 'UNVALUATED'::text, ''::text) AS prod_name,
            prod.value AS prod_value,
                CASE
                    WHEN prodc.name::text ~~ 'FEED'::text THEN sum(stg.qtyonhand * (( SELECT cuc.dividerate
                       FROM c_uom_conversion cuc
                      WHERE cuc.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id
                     LIMIT 1)))
                    ELSE sum(stg.qtyonhand)
                END AS prod_qty,
            prodc.name AS prod_cate,
            stg.m_attributesetinstance_id,
            uom.name AS uom_name_det,
            wh.m_warehouse_id AS warehouse_parent_id
           FROM z_tbl_buku_peternak tbp
             JOIN m_warehouse wh ON wh.m_warehouse_id = tbp.m_warehouse_id
             LEFT JOIN m_locator loc ON loc.m_warehouse_id = wh.m_warehouse_id AND loc.value::text = wh.value::text
             LEFT JOIN m_storage stg ON stg.m_locator_id = loc.m_locator_id AND stg.ad_org_id = loc.ad_org_id
             LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON prod.m_product_id = stg.m_product_id
             LEFT JOIN m_product_category prodc ON prodc.m_product_category_id = prod.m_product_category_id
             LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON uom.c_uom_id = prod.c_uom_id
          WHERE prodc.name::text ~~ 'PIG'::text
          GROUP BY 1::integer, tbp.ad_client_id, tbp.ad_org_id, tbp.name, wh.m_warehouse_id, wh.value, wh.name, stg.m_product_id, (replace(upper(prod.name::text), 'UNVALUATED'::text, ''::text)), prod.value, prodc.name, stg.m_attributesetinstance_id, uom.name
        UNION
         SELECT DISTINCT 2 AS orderkey,
            tbp.ad_client_id,
            tbp.ad_org_id,
            tbp.name AS ceklist_peternak_pig,
            whfit.m_warehouse_id AS whfit_m_warehouse_id,
            whfit.value AS fit_val,
            whfit.name AS fit_name,
            stgf.m_product_id AS wh_prod_f,
            replace(upper(prodf.name::text), 'UNVALUATED'::text, ''::text) AS prod_name_f,
            prodf.value AS prod_value_f,
                CASE
                    WHEN prodc.name::text ~~ 'FEED'::text THEN sum(stgf.qtyonhand * (( SELECT cuc.dividerate
                       FROM c_uom_conversion cuc
                      WHERE cuc.m_product_id = prodf.m_product_id
                     LIMIT 1)))
                    ELSE sum(stgf.qtyonhand)
                END AS prod_qty_f,
            prodc.name AS prod_cate,
            stgf.m_attributesetinstance_id,
            uom.name AS uom_name_det,
            tbp.m_warehouse_id AS warehouse_parent_id
           FROM z_tbl_buku_peternak tbp
             JOIN m_locator fit ON fit.m_locator_id = tbp.z_locator_feed_id
             LEFT JOIN m_warehouse whfit ON whfit.m_warehouse_id = fit.m_warehouse_id
             LEFT JOIN m_storage stgf ON stgf.m_locator_id = fit.m_locator_id
             LEFT JOIN m_product prodf ON prodf.m_product_id = stgf.m_product_id
             LEFT JOIN m_product_category prodc ON prodc.m_product_category_id = prodf.m_product_category_id
             LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON uom.c_uom_id = prodf.c_uom_id
          WHERE prodc.name::text ~~ 'FEED'::text
          GROUP BY 2::integer, tbp.ad_client_id, tbp.ad_org_id, tbp.name, whfit.m_warehouse_id, whfit.value, whfit.name, stgf.m_product_id, (replace(upper(prodf.name::text), 'UNVALUATED'::text, ''::text)), prodf.value, prodc.name, stgf.m_attributesetinstance_id, uom.name, tbp.m_warehouse_id
        UNION
         SELECT DISTINCT 3 AS orderkey,
            tbp.ad_client_id,
            tbp.ad_org_id,
            tbp.name AS ceklist_peternak_pig,
            whcen.m_warehouse_id AS whcen_m_warehouse_id,
            whcen.value AS cen_val,
            whcen.name AS cen_name,
            stgc.m_product_id AS wh_prod_c,
            replace(upper(prodc.name::text), 'UNVALUATED'::text, ''::text) AS prod_name_c,
            prodc.value AS prod_value_c,
                CASE
                    WHEN prodcat.name::text ~~ 'FEED'::text THEN sum(stgc.qtyonhand * (( SELECT cuc.dividerate
                       FROM c_uom_conversion cuc
                      WHERE cuc.m_product_id = prodc.m_product_id
                     LIMIT 1)))
                    ELSE sum(stgc.qtyonhand)
                END AS prod_qty_c,
            prodcat.name AS prod_cate,
            stgc.m_attributesetinstance_id,
            uom.name AS uom_name_det,
            tbp.m_warehouse_id AS warehouse_parent_id
           FROM z_tbl_buku_peternak tbp
             JOIN m_locator cen ON cen.m_locator_id = tbp.z_locator_central_id
             LEFT JOIN m_warehouse whcen ON whcen.m_warehouse_id = cen.m_warehouse_id
             LEFT JOIN m_storage stgc ON stgc.m_locator_id = cen.m_locator_id
             LEFT JOIN m_product prodc ON prodc.m_product_id = stgc.m_product_id
             LEFT JOIN m_product_category prodcat ON prodcat.m_product_category_id = prodc.m_product_category_id
             LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON uom.c_uom_id = prodc.c_uom_id
          WHERE prodcat.name::text ~~ 'SEMEN'::text
          GROUP BY 3::integer, tbp.ad_client_id, tbp.ad_org_id, tbp.name, whcen.m_warehouse_id, whcen.value, whcen.name, stgc.m_product_id, (replace(upper(prodc.name::text), 'UNVALUATED'::text, ''::text)), prodc.value, prodcat.name, stgc.m_attributesetinstance_id, uom.name, tbp.m_warehouse_id
        UNION
         SELECT DISTINCT 4 AS orderkey,
            tbp.ad_client_id,
            tbp.ad_org_id,
            tbp.name AS ceklist_peternak_pig,
            whvmd.m_warehouse_id AS whmvd_m_warehouse_id,
            whvmd.value AS vmd_val,
            whvmd.name AS vmd_name,
            stgv.m_product_id AS wh_prod_v,
            replace(upper(prodv.name::text), 'UNVALUATED'::text, ''::text) AS prod_name_v,
            prodv.value AS prod_value_v,
                CASE
                    WHEN prodc.name::text ~~ 'FEED'::text THEN sum(stgv.qtyonhand * (( SELECT cuc.dividerate
                       FROM c_uom_conversion cuc
                      WHERE cuc.m_product_id = prodv.m_product_id
                     LIMIT 1)))
                    ELSE sum(stgv.qtyonhand)
                END AS prod_qty_v,
            prodc.name AS prod_cate,
            stgv.m_attributesetinstance_id,
            uom.name AS uom_name_det,
            tbp.m_warehouse_id AS warehouse_parent_id
           FROM z_tbl_buku_peternak tbp
             JOIN m_locator vmd ON vmd.m_locator_id = tbp.z_locator_vmd_id
             LEFT JOIN m_warehouse whvmd ON whvmd.m_warehouse_id = vmd.m_warehouse_id
             LEFT JOIN m_storage stgv ON stgv.m_locator_id = vmd.m_locator_id
             LEFT JOIN m_product prodv ON prodv.m_product_id = stgv.m_product_id
             LEFT JOIN m_product_category prodc ON prodc.m_product_category_id = prodv.m_product_category_id
             LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON uom.c_uom_id = prodv.c_uom_id
          WHERE prodc.name::text ~~ 'MEDICINE'::text OR prodc.name::text ~~ 'VACCINE'::text
          GROUP BY 4::integer, tbp.ad_client_id, tbp.ad_org_id, tbp.name, whvmd.m_warehouse_id, whvmd.value, whvmd.name, stgv.m_product_id, (replace(upper(prodv.name::text), 'UNVALUATED'::text, ''::text)), prodv.value, prodc.name, stgv.m_attributesetinstance_id, uom.name, tbp.m_warehouse_id) tabel
  WHERE m_warehouse_id IS NOT NULL AND prod_name IS NOT NULL;


-- adempiere.z_inventory_stock_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_inventory_stock_reportview
AS SELECT inven.ad_client_id,
    inven.ad_org_id,
    inven.m_warehouse_id,
    inven.movementdate,
    inven.m_inventory_id,
    inven.documentno,
    wh.value AS wh_value,
    wh.name AS wh_name,
    inven.created,
    inven.updated,
    inven.updatedby,
    prod.value AS prd_value,
    btrim(upper(btrim(prod.name::text, ' '::text)), 'UNVALUATED'::text) AS prd_name,
        CASE
            WHEN prodc.name::text ~~* 'feed'::text AND uom.name::text ~~* '%kg%'::text THEN invenl.qtycount * (( SELECT cuc.dividerate
               FROM c_uom_conversion cuc
              WHERE cuc.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id))
            ELSE invenl.qtycount
        END AS qtycount,
    uom.c_uom_id,
        CASE
            WHEN prodc.name::text ~~* 'feed'::text THEN 'ZAK'::character varying
            ELSE uom.name
        END AS uom_name,
    COALESCE(( SELECT kos.currentcostprice
           FROM m_product produk
             JOIN m_cost kos ON kos.m_product_id = produk.m_product_id
             JOIN m_costelement co ON co.m_costelement_id = kos.m_costelement_id
          WHERE produk.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id AND kos.m_attributesetinstance_id = invenl.m_attributesetinstance_id AND co.name::text = 'Average PO'::text AND produk.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id
         LIMIT 1), 0::numeric) AS map_price,
    loc.m_locator_id,
    loc.value AS loc_value,
    COALESCE(( SELECT tabukpet.m_warehouse_id
           FROM z_tbl_buku_peternak tabukpet
          WHERE tabukpet.z_locator_feed_id = loc.m_locator_id OR tabukpet.z_locator_vmd_id = loc.m_locator_id OR tabukpet.z_locator_central_id = loc.m_locator_id
         LIMIT 1), inven.m_warehouse_id) AS warehouse_parent_id,
    prodc.m_product_category_id,
    prodc.name AS prodcat_name
   FROM m_inventory inven
     LEFT JOIN m_inventoryline invenl ON invenl.m_inventory_id = inven.m_inventory_id
     LEFT JOIN m_locator loc ON loc.m_locator_id = invenl.m_locator_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse wh ON wh.m_warehouse_id = inven.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON prod.m_product_id = invenl.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON uom.c_uom_id = prod.c_uom_id
     JOIN c_doctype dt ON dt.c_doctype_id = inven.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category prodc ON prodc.m_product_category_id = prod.m_product_category_id
  WHERE inven.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND (inven.ad_org_id IN ( SELECT bp.ad_org_id
           FROM z_parameter_reporting pr
             JOIN z_parameter_report_bp bp ON bp.z_parameter_reporting_id = pr.z_parameter_reporting_id
          WHERE pr.name::text = 'Function Swine Breeder'::text)) AND dt.name::text = 'Physical Inventory'::text;


-- adempiere.z_laporan_penjualan_lb_v source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_laporan_penjualan_lb_v
AS SELECT inv.ad_client_id,
    ( SELECT orin.parent_org_id
           FROM ad_org org
             JOIN ad_orginfo orin ON org.ad_org_id = orin.ad_org_id
          WHERE org.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id) AS ad_org_id,
    ( SELECT org.name
           FROM ad_org org
          WHERE org.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id) AS childname,
    ( SELECT orin.ad_orgtype_id
           FROM ad_org org
             JOIN ad_orginfo orin ON org.ad_org_id = orin.ad_org_id
          WHERE org.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id) AS ad_orgtype_id,
    ( SELECT ortyp.name
           FROM ad_org org
             JOIN ad_orginfo orin ON org.ad_org_id = orin.ad_org_id
             JOIN ad_orgtype ortyp ON orin.ad_orgtype_id = ortyp.ad_orgtype_id
          WHERE org.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id) AS typeorg,
    inv.dateinvoiced AS tglinvoice,
    ord.documentno AS nosppa,
    inv.documentno AS invdocno,
    ord.c_bpartner_id,
    bp.name AS custname,
    pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id,
    bpt.name AS namapeternak,
    orlin.m_product_id,
    ( SELECT prod.value
           FROM m_product prod
          WHERE prod.m_product_id = orlin.m_product_id) AS kodeprod,
    orlin.z_qty_ekor_real AS birds,
    orlin.qtyentered AS qtykg,
    orlin.qtyentered / orlin.z_qty_ekor_real AS abw,
    orlin.pricelist AS price,
    orlin.discountamt AS disc,
    orlin.pricelist - orlin.discountamt AS netprice,
    (orlin.pricelist - orlin.discountamt) * orlin.qtyentered AS totalsales,
    ord.isfinalcatching
   FROM c_order ord
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON ord.c_order_id = orlin.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bp ON ord.c_bpartner_id = bp.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_invoice inv ON ord.c_order_id = inv.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_project pro ON ord.c_project_id = pro.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak bpt ON pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id = bpt.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id
  WHERE (ord.c_doctypetarget_id IN ( SELECT dtyp.c_doctype_id
           FROM c_doctype dtyp
          WHERE dtyp.name::text ~~ 'SPPA'::text)) AND (inv.c_doctypetarget_id IN ( SELECT dtyp.c_doctype_id
           FROM c_doctype dtyp
          WHERE dtyp.name::text ~~ 'AR Invoice LB'::text)) AND ord.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND inv.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND bpt.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar;


-- adempiere.z_laporan_sppa_batal_v source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_laporan_sppa_batal_v
AS SELECT ord.ad_client_id,
    ord.ad_org_id,
    ( SELECT loc.city
           FROM ad_org orgg
             JOIN ad_orginfo orgin ON orgg.ad_org_id = orgin.ad_org_id
             JOIN c_location loc ON orgin.c_location_id = loc.c_location_id
          WHERE orgg.ad_org_id = (( SELECT orginf.parent_org_id
                   FROM ad_org org
                     JOIN ad_orginfo orginf ON org.ad_org_id = orginf.ad_org_id
                  WHERE org.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id AND org.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id)) AND orgg.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id) AS orgloc,
    ( SELECT org.name
           FROM ad_org org
          WHERE org.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id AND org.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id) AS orgchild,
    ord.dateordered AS tglsppa,
    ord.description,
    COALESCE((( SELECT zcon.z_string2
           FROM z_config zcon
          WHERE zcon.z_config_id = ord.z_alasan_batal_id))::text, 'AUTO VOID BY SISTEM'::text) AS alasanbatal,
    ord.documentno AS nosppa,
    ( SELECT inv.documentno
           FROM c_invoice inv
          WHERE inv.c_doctypetarget_id = (( SELECT dtyp.c_doctype_id
                   FROM c_doctype dtyp
                  WHERE dtyp.name::text ~~ 'AR Invoice LB'::text AND dtyp.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id)) AND ord.c_order_id = inv.c_order_id
         LIMIT 1) AS invdocno,
    bp.name AS custname,
    ord.c_bpartner_id,
    buku.name AS namapeternak,
    buku.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id,
    ( SELECT prod.value
           FROM m_product prod
          WHERE prod.m_product_id = orlin.m_product_id) AS kodeprod,
    orlin.m_product_id,
    orlin.z_qty_ekor_est AS qtyekor,
    orlin.z_qty_kg_est AS qtykg,
    orlin.z_pricelist_estimasi AS price,
    orlin.discountamtest AS disc,
        CASE
            WHEN orlin.z_qty_ekor_est = 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE orlin.z_qty_kg_est / orlin.z_qty_ekor_est
        END AS abw,
    orlin.z_pricelist_estimasi - orlin.discountamtest AS netprice,
    ( SELECT per.c_period_id
           FROM c_period per
          WHERE to_char(per.startdate, 'mm yyyy'::text) = to_char(ord.dateordered, 'mm yyyy'::text) AND per.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id) AS c_period_id,
    ( SELECT adis.name
           FROM z_tbl_alasandiskon adis
          WHERE adis.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id = orlin.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id) AS keterangan
   FROM c_order ord
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON ord.c_order_id = orlin.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bp ON ord.c_bpartner_id = bp.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_project pro ON ord.c_project_id = pro.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak buku ON pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id = buku.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id
  WHERE (ord.c_doctypetarget_id IN ( SELECT dtyp.c_doctype_id
           FROM c_doctype dtyp
          WHERE dtyp.name::text ~~ 'SPPA'::text)) AND ord.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar
UNION ALL
 SELECT ord.ad_client_id,
    ( SELECT orginf.parent_org_id
           FROM ad_org org
             JOIN ad_orginfo orginf ON org.ad_org_id = orginf.ad_org_id
          WHERE org.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id AND org.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id) AS ad_org_id,
    ( SELECT loc.city
           FROM ad_org orgg
             JOIN ad_orginfo orgin ON orgg.ad_org_id = orgin.ad_org_id
             JOIN c_location loc ON orgin.c_location_id = loc.c_location_id
          WHERE orgg.ad_org_id = (( SELECT orginf.parent_org_id
                   FROM ad_org org
                     JOIN ad_orginfo orginf ON org.ad_org_id = orginf.ad_org_id
                  WHERE org.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id AND org.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id)) AND orgg.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id) AS orgloc,
    ( SELECT org.name
           FROM ad_org org
          WHERE org.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id AND org.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id) AS orgchild,
    ord.dateordered AS tglsppa,
    ord.description,
    COALESCE((( SELECT zcon.z_string2
           FROM z_config zcon
          WHERE zcon.z_config_id = ord.z_alasan_batal_id))::text, 'AUTO VOID BY SISTEM'::text) AS alasanbatal,
    ord.documentno AS nosppa,
    ( SELECT inv.documentno
           FROM c_invoice inv
          WHERE inv.c_doctypetarget_id = (( SELECT dtyp.c_doctype_id
                   FROM c_doctype dtyp
                  WHERE dtyp.name::text ~~ 'AR Invoice LB'::text AND dtyp.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id)) AND ord.c_order_id = inv.c_order_id
         LIMIT 1) AS invdocno,
    bp.name AS custname,
    ord.c_bpartner_id,
    buku.name AS namapeternak,
    buku.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id,
    ( SELECT prod.value
           FROM m_product prod
          WHERE prod.m_product_id = orlin.m_product_id) AS kodeprod,
    orlin.m_product_id,
    orlin.z_qty_ekor_est AS qtyekor,
    orlin.z_qty_kg_est AS qtykg,
    orlin.z_pricelist_estimasi AS price,
    orlin.discountamtest AS disc,
        CASE
            WHEN orlin.z_qty_ekor_est = 0::numeric THEN 0::numeric
            ELSE orlin.z_qty_kg_est / orlin.z_qty_ekor_est
        END AS abw,
    orlin.z_pricelist_estimasi - orlin.discountamtest AS netprice,
    ( SELECT per.c_period_id
           FROM c_period per
          WHERE to_char(per.startdate, 'mm yyyy'::text) = to_char(ord.dateordered, 'mm yyyy'::text) AND per.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id) AS c_period_id,
    ( SELECT adis.name
           FROM z_tbl_alasandiskon adis
          WHERE adis.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id = orlin.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id) AS keterangan
   FROM c_order ord
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON ord.c_order_id = orlin.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bp ON ord.c_bpartner_id = bp.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_project pro ON ord.c_project_id = pro.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak buku ON pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id = buku.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id
  WHERE (ord.c_doctypetarget_id IN ( SELECT dtyp.c_doctype_id
           FROM c_doctype dtyp
          WHERE dtyp.name::text ~~ 'SPPA'::text)) AND ord.docstatus = 'VO'::bpchar;



-- adempiere.z_listing_invoice_v source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_listing_invoice_v
AS SELECT ord.ad_client_id,
    ord.ad_org_id,
    ( SELECT orgg.name
           FROM ad_org orgg
          WHERE orgg.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id) AS childname,
    inv.dateinvoiced AS tglinvoice,
    ord.z_catching_date AS tgltangkap,
    ord.documentno AS nosppa,
    inv.documentno AS invdocno,
    ord.c_bpartner_id,
    bp.name AS custname,
    pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id,
    buku.name AS namapeternak,
    orlin.m_product_id,
    ( SELECT prod.value
           FROM m_product prod
          WHERE prod.m_product_id = orlin.m_product_id) AS kodeprod,
    orlin.z_qty_ekor_real AS birds,
    orlin.qtyentered AS qtykg,
    orlin.qtyentered / orlin.z_qty_ekor_real AS abw,
    orlin.pricelist AS price,
    orlin.discountamt AS disc,
    orlin.pricelist - orlin.discountamt AS netprice,
    inv.totallines,
    inv.grandtotal,
    ( SELECT per.c_period_id
           FROM c_period per
          WHERE to_char(per.startdate, 'mm yyyy'::text) = to_char(ord.z_catching_date, 'mm yyyy'::text) AND per.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id AND per.ad_org_id = (( SELECT ao.parent_org_id
                   FROM ad_org orgg
                     JOIN ad_orginfo ao ON orgg.ad_org_id = ao.ad_org_id
                  WHERE orgg.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id))) AS c_period_id,
    adis.name AS keterangan,
    orlin.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id
   FROM c_order ord
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON ord.c_order_id = orlin.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bp ON ord.c_bpartner_id = bp.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_invoice inv ON ord.c_order_id = inv.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_project pro ON ord.c_project_id = pro.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak buku ON pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id = buku.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype dtyp ON ord.c_doctypetarget_id = dtyp.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype doctp ON inv.c_doctypetarget_id = doctp.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_alasandiskon adis ON adis.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id = orlin.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id
  WHERE dtyp.name::text ~~ 'SPPA'::text AND doctp.name::text ~~ 'AR Invoice LB'::text AND ord.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND inv.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar
UNION ALL
 SELECT ord.ad_client_id,
    inv.ad_org_id,
    ( SELECT orgg.name
           FROM ad_org orgg
          WHERE orgg.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id) AS childname,
    inv.dateinvoiced AS tglinvoice,
    ord.z_catching_date AS tgltangkap,
    ord.documentno AS nosppa,
    inv.documentno AS invdocno,
    ord.c_bpartner_id,
    bp.name AS custname,
    pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id,
    buku.name AS namapeternak,
    orlin.m_product_id,
    ( SELECT prod.value
           FROM m_product prod
          WHERE prod.m_product_id = orlin.m_product_id) AS kodeprod,
    orlin.z_qty_ekor_real AS birds,
    orlin.qtyentered AS qtykg,
    orlin.qtyentered / orlin.z_qty_ekor_real AS abw,
    orlin.pricelist AS price,
    orlin.discountamt AS disc,
    orlin.pricelist - orlin.discountamt AS netprice,
    inv.totallines,
    inv.grandtotal,
    ( SELECT per.c_period_id
           FROM c_period per
          WHERE to_char(per.startdate, 'mm yyyy'::text) = to_char(ord.z_catching_date, 'mm yyyy'::text) AND per.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id AND per.ad_org_id = inv.ad_org_id) AS c_period_id,
    adis.name AS keterangan,
    orlin.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id
   FROM c_order ord
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON ord.c_order_id = orlin.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bp ON ord.c_bpartner_id = bp.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_invoice inv ON ord.c_order_id = inv.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_project pro ON ord.c_project_id = pro.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak buku ON pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id = buku.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype dtyp ON ord.c_doctypetarget_id = dtyp.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype doctp ON inv.c_doctypetarget_id = doctp.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_alasandiskon adis ON adis.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id = orlin.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id
  WHERE dtyp.name::text ~~ 'SPPA'::text AND doctp.name::text ~~ 'AR Invoice LB'::text AND ord.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND inv.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar;



-- adempiere.z_monthly_sum_df_v source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_monthly_sum_df_v
AS SELECT bpt.ad_client_id,
    bpt.ad_org_id,
    org.name AS organisasi,
    prodc.m_product_category_id,
    mov.movementdate AS datee,
    mov.m_movement_id,
    mov.documentno AS nodf,
    prodc.name AS prodcnm,
    prod.m_product_id,
    prod.value AS product_code,
    prod.name AS product_name,
    uom.c_uom_id,
    uom.x12de355 AS uom,
        CASE
            WHEN mov.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN movl.movementqty
            ELSE 0::numeric
        END AS transferout,
        CASE
            WHEN mov.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN movl.movementqty
            ELSE 0::numeric
        END AS intransit,
    mov.docstatus,
    mov.c_bpartner_id
   FROM m_movement mov
     LEFT JOIN m_movementline movl ON movl.m_movement_id = mov.m_movement_id
     LEFT JOIN m_locator loc ON movl.m_locator_id = loc.m_locator_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak bpt ON loc.m_warehouse_id = bpt.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org org ON bpt.ad_org_id = org.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON movl.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category prodc ON prod.m_product_category_id = prodc.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON prod.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
  WHERE mov.docstatus = ANY (ARRAY['CO'::bpchar, 'IP'::bpchar])
UNION
 SELECT mov.ad_client_id,
    mov.ad_org_id,
    org.name AS organisasi,
    prodc.m_product_category_id,
    mov.movementdate AS datee,
    mov.m_movement_id,
    mov.documentno AS nodf,
    prodc.name AS prodcnm,
    prod.m_product_id,
    prod.value AS product_code,
    prod.name AS product_name,
    uom.c_uom_id,
    uom.x12de355 AS uom,
        CASE
            WHEN mov.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar THEN movl.movementqty
            ELSE 0::numeric
        END AS transferout,
        CASE
            WHEN mov.docstatus = 'IP'::bpchar THEN movl.movementqty
            ELSE 0::numeric
        END AS intransit,
    mov.docstatus,
    mov.c_bpartner_id
   FROM m_movement mov
     LEFT JOIN m_movementline movl ON movl.m_movement_id = mov.m_movement_id
     LEFT JOIN m_locator loc ON movl.m_locator_id = loc.m_locator_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak bpt ON loc.m_warehouse_id = bpt.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org org ON bpt.ad_org_id = org.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON movl.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category prodc ON prod.m_product_category_id = prodc.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON prod.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
  WHERE mov.docstatus = ANY (ARRAY['CO'::bpchar, 'IP'::bpchar]);


-- adempiere.z_mr_for_cpma_v source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_mr_for_cpma_v
AS SELECT mi.movementdate,
    mi.ad_org_id,
    mi.ad_client_id,
    mi.m_warehouse_id,
    mi.c_doctype_id,
    mi.c_order_id,
    mi.documentno,
    mil.line,
    prod.value AS produk_value,
    prod.name AS produk,
    mil.qtyentered,
    uom.name AS uom,
    mi.isactive,
    mi.created,
    mi.createdby,
    mi.updated,
    mi.updatedby,
    ( SELECT yus.name
           FROM ad_user yus
          WHERE yus.ad_user_id = mi.createdby) AS createdbyusername,
    ( SELECT par.name
           FROM c_bpartner par
             JOIN ad_user yus ON par.c_bpartner_id = yus.c_bpartner_id
          WHERE yus.ad_user_id = mi.createdby) AS createdbybpname,
    ( SELECT yus.name
           FROM ad_user yus
          WHERE yus.ad_user_id = mi.updatedby) AS updatedbyusername,
    ( SELECT par.name
           FROM c_bpartner par
             JOIN ad_user yus ON par.c_bpartner_id = yus.c_bpartner_id
          WHERE yus.ad_user_id = mi.updatedby) AS updatedbybpname,
    org.name AS organisasi,
    mi.m_inout_id,
    mi.isapproved,
    dt.name AS doctype,
    ( SELECT refl.name
           FROM ad_ref_list refl
             JOIN ad_reference reff ON reff.ad_reference_id = refl.ad_reference_id
          WHERE reff.name::text = '_Document Status'::text AND refl.value::bpchar = mi.docstatus) AS document_status,
    wh.name AS warehouse_name,
    ord.documentno AS po_docno
   FROM m_inout mi
     LEFT JOIN m_inoutline mil ON mi.m_inout_id = mil.m_inout_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON mil.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON mil.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org org ON mi.ad_org_id = org.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype dt ON mi.c_doctype_id = dt.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse wh ON mi.m_warehouse_id = wh.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN c_order ord ON mi.c_order_id = ord.c_order_id;


-- adempiere.z_po_dom_sap_v source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_po_dom_sap_v
AS SELECT ord.c_order_id,
    ord.ad_org_id,
    ord.ad_client_id,
    ord.documentno,
    'ZQ01'::text AS a,
    org.value AS org_value,
    produk.group1,
    produk.group2,
    ord.dateordered::date AS dateordered,
    ord.datepromised::date AS datepromised,
    ship.dutiesshipperaccount,
    ship.shipperaccount,
    produk.value AS prod_value,
    ' '::text AS uom,
    ordline.qtyentered,
    (org.name::text || ' / '::text) || ord.documentno::text AS namaorg,
    ord.c_bpartner_id
   FROM c_order ord
     LEFT JOIN ad_org org ON ord.ad_org_id = org.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline ordline ON ord.c_order_id = ordline.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product produk ON ordline.m_product_id = produk.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON ordline.c_uom_id = uom.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner partner ON ord.c_bpartner_id = partner.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bp_shippingacct ship ON ship.c_bpartner_id = partner.c_bpartner_id;


-- adempiere.z_pricelistpenjualan_cpma_v source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_pricelistpenjualan_cpma_v
AS SELECT ar.ad_role_id,
    vp.ad_client_id,
    ao.name AS z_plv_cpma_organization,
    mp.name AS z_plv_cpma_pricelistpenjualan,
    vp.documentno AS z_plv_cpma_documentno,
    cd.name AS z_plv_cpma_targetdocumenttype,
    vp.name AS z_plv_cpma_name,
    vp.validfrom AS z_plv_cpma_validfrom,
    vp.name AS z_plv_cpma_pricelistversion,
    p.name AS z_plv_cpma_product,
    p.value AS z_plv_cpma_productcode,
    pp.pricelist AS z_plv_cpma_listprice,
    mp.m_pricelist_id AS z_plv_pricelist_id,
    vp.m_pricelist_version_id AS z_plv_version_id,
    vp.isapproved,
    arl.name AS documentstatus,
    vp.created,
    au1.name AS createdbyname,
    vp.updatedby,
    au2.name AS updatedbyname,
    pp.m_productprice_id,
    u.name AS uomname,
    vp.ad_org_id,
    ( SELECT ad_table.ad_table_id
           FROM ad_table
          WHERE lower(ad_table.name::text) ~~ 'price list version'::text) AS ad_table_id,
        CASE
            WHEN upper(ar.name::text) ~~ '%GM FINANCE%'::text THEN vp.isapprovedbygmfinance
            WHEN upper(ar.name::text) ~~ '%GM MARKETING%'::text THEN vp.isapprovedbygmmarketing
            WHEN upper(ar.name::text) ~~ '%HEAD MARKETING DOC%'::text THEN vp.isapprovedbyhomdoc
            WHEN upper(ar.name::text) ~~ '%RH JABAR%'::text THEN vp.isapprovedbyrhjabar
            ELSE NULL::bpchar
        END AS isapprovedbyrole
   FROM m_pricelist_version vp
     LEFT JOIN m_productprice pp ON pp.m_pricelist_version_id = vp.m_pricelist_version_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_org ao ON ao.ad_org_id = vp.ad_org_id
     LEFT JOIN m_pricelist mp ON mp.m_pricelist_id = vp.m_pricelist_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype cd ON cd.c_doctype_id = vp.c_doctypetarget_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product p ON p.m_product_id = pp.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user au1 ON au1.ad_user_id = vp.createdby
     LEFT JOIN ad_user au2 ON au2.ad_user_id = vp.createdby
     LEFT JOIN ad_ref_list arl ON arl.value::text = vp.docstatus::text AND arl.ad_reference_id = 131::numeric
     LEFT JOIN c_uom u ON u.c_uom_id = p.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_role ar ON upper(ar.name::text) ~~ '%GM FINANCE%'::text OR upper(ar.name::text) ~~ '%GM MARKETING%'::text OR upper(ar.name::text) ~~ '%HEAD MARKETING DOC%'::text OR upper(ar.name::text) ~~ '%RH JABAR%'::text
  WHERE lower(mp.name::text) ~~ '%penjualan%'::text;

    p.r_mailtext_id,

    p.s_expensetype_id,
    p.shelfdepth,

   FROM m_cost c

-- adempiere.z_realisasi_sppa_per_peternak_v source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_realisasi_sppa_per_peternak_v
AS SELECT ord.ad_client_id,
    ord.ad_org_id,
    ( SELECT orgg.name
           FROM ad_org orgg
          WHERE orgg.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id) AS childname,
    ( SELECT ortyp.name
           FROM ad_org orgg
             JOIN ad_orginfo orin ON orgg.ad_org_id = orin.ad_org_id
             JOIN ad_orgtype ortyp ON orin.ad_orgtype_id = ortyp.ad_orgtype_id
          WHERE orgg.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id) AS tipe,
    ord.dateordered AS tglsppa,
    pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id,
    buku.name AS namapeternak,
    orlin.m_product_id,
    prod.value AS kodeprod,
    sum(orlin.z_qty_ekor_real) AS birds,
    sum(orlin.qtyentered) AS qtykg,
    sum(orlin.pricelist) AS price,
    sum(orlin.discountamt) AS disc,
    sum(orlin.linenetamt) AS total
   FROM c_order ord
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON ord.c_order_id = orlin.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_project pro ON ord.c_project_id = pro.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak buku ON pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id = buku.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype dtyp ON ord.c_doctypetarget_id = dtyp.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON prod.m_product_id = orlin.m_product_id
  WHERE dtyp.name::text ~~ 'SPPA'::text AND ord.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar
  GROUP BY ord.ad_client_id, ord.ad_org_id, ord.dateordered, pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id, buku.name, orlin.m_product_id, prod.value
UNION
 SELECT ord.ad_client_id,
    ( SELECT orginf.parent_org_id
           FROM ad_org org
             JOIN ad_orginfo orginf ON org.ad_org_id = orginf.ad_org_id
          WHERE org.ad_client_id = ord.ad_client_id AND org.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id) AS ad_org_id,
    ( SELECT orgg.name
           FROM ad_org orgg
          WHERE orgg.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id) AS childname,
    ( SELECT ortyp.name
           FROM ad_org orgg
             JOIN ad_orginfo orin ON orgg.ad_org_id = orin.ad_org_id
             JOIN ad_orgtype ortyp ON orin.ad_orgtype_id = ortyp.ad_orgtype_id
          WHERE orgg.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id) AS tipe,
    ord.dateordered AS tglsppa,
    pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id,
    buku.name AS namapeternak,
    orlin.m_product_id,
    prod.value AS kodeprod,
    sum(orlin.z_qty_ekor_real) AS birds,
    sum(orlin.qtyentered) AS qtykg,
    sum(orlin.pricelist) AS price,
    sum(orlin.discountamt) AS disc,
    sum(orlin.linenetamt) AS total
   FROM c_order ord
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline orlin ON ord.c_order_id = orlin.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_project pro ON ord.c_project_id = pro.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak buku ON pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id = buku.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype dtyp ON ord.c_doctypetarget_id = dtyp.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON prod.m_product_id = orlin.m_product_id
  WHERE dtyp.name::text ~~ 'SPPA'::text AND ord.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar
  GROUP BY ord.ad_client_id, ord.ad_org_id, ord.dateordered, pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id, buku.name, orlin.m_product_id, prod.value;


-- adempiere.z_reportview_prod source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_reportview_prod
AS SELECT prod.ad_org_id,
    prod.ad_client_id,
    prod.m_product_id::text AS m_product_id,
    prod.value,
    prodcat.name AS product_category,
    prod.name,
    uomp.name AS uom,
    prod.updated
   FROM m_product prod
     JOIN m_product_category prodcat ON prod.m_product_category_id = prodcat.m_product_category_id
     JOIN c_uom uomp ON prod.c_uom_id = uomp.c_uom_id
  WHERE prod.isactive = 'Y'::bpchar;


-- adempiere.z_salesbyreasons_v source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_salesbyreasons_v
AS SELECT ord.ad_client_id,
    ord.ad_org_id,
    fph.z_no_fakturpajak AS nofaktur,
    fph.z_tgl_fakturpajak AS tglfaktur,
    bp.value AS customercode,
    bp.name AS customername,
    inv.c_invoice_id AS noinv,
    inv.documentno AS noinv1,
    ordl.z_qty_ekor_real AS qty,
    ordl.qtyentered AS weight,
    invline1.priceentered AS upbuy,
    ordl.z_hargapasar AS posko,
    ordl.z_hargapasar * ordl.qtyentered AS nominalposko,
    0 AS var,
    0 AS minimprice,
    0 AS nominalhet,
    ordl.discountamtest AS estdisc,
    invline.priceentered AS upsell,
    ordl.discountamt AS discperkg,
    ordl.priceentered AS netprice,
    ordl.priceentered * ordl.qtyentered AS sales,
    ordl.discountamt * ordl.qtyentered AS discount,
    ordl.priceentered * ordl.qtyentered - ordl.discountamt * ordl.qtyentered AS netsales,
    0 AS bh,
    0 AS sah,
    0 AS ah,
    0 AS total,
    adis.value,
    adis.name AS alasandiskon,
    prod.value AS codebarang,
    bpt.value AS codepeternak,
    bpt.name AS namapeternak,
    ord.documentno AS nosppa
   FROM c_order ord
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline ordl ON ord.c_order_id = ordl.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON ordl.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN c_invoice inv ON ord.c_order_id = inv.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_invoiceline invline ON inv.c_invoice_id = invline.c_invoice_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bp ON ord.c_bpartner_id = bp.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_invoice inv1 ON ord.documentno::text = inv1.poreference::text
     LEFT JOIN c_invoiceline invline1 ON inv1.c_invoice_id = invline1.c_invoice_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_alasandiskon adis ON ordl.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id = adis.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id
     LEFT JOIN c_project pro ON ord.c_project_id = pro.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak bpt ON pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id = bpt.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id
     LEFT JOIN z_fakturpajak_header fph ON inv.documentno::text = fph.z_invoice_no::text
  WHERE ord.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND (ord.c_doctypetarget_id IN ( SELECT dtyp.c_doctype_id
           FROM c_doctype dtyp
          WHERE dtyp.name::text ~~ 'SPPA'::text))
UNION ALL
 SELECT ord.ad_client_id,
    ( SELECT orinf.parent_org_id
           FROM ad_org org
             JOIN ad_orginfo orinf ON org.ad_org_id = orinf.ad_org_id
          WHERE org.ad_org_id = ord.ad_org_id) AS ad_org_id,
    fph.z_no_fakturpajak AS nofaktur,
    fph.z_tgl_fakturpajak AS tglfaktur,
    bp.value AS customercode,
    bp.name AS customername,
    inv.c_invoice_id AS noinv,
    inv.documentno AS noinv1,
    ordl.z_qty_ekor_real AS qty,
    ordl.qtyentered AS weight,
    invline1.priceentered AS upbuy,
    ordl.z_hargapasar AS posko,
    ordl.z_hargapasar * ordl.qtyentered AS nominalposko,
    0 AS var,
    0 AS minimprice,
    0 AS nominalhet,
    ordl.discountamtest AS estdisc,
    invline.priceentered AS upsell,
    ordl.discountamt AS discperkg,
    ordl.priceentered AS netprice,
    ordl.priceentered * ordl.qtyentered AS sales,
    ordl.discountamt * ordl.qtyentered AS discount,
    ordl.priceentered * ordl.qtyentered - ordl.discountamt * ordl.qtyentered AS netsales,
    0 AS bh,
    0 AS sah,
    0 AS ah,
    0 AS total,
    adis.value,
    adis.name AS alasandiskon,
    prod.value AS codebarang,
    bpt.value AS codepeternak,
    bpt.name AS namapeternak,
    ord.documentno AS nosppa
   FROM c_order ord
     LEFT JOIN c_orderline ordl ON ord.c_order_id = ordl.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON ordl.m_product_id = prod.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN c_invoice inv ON ord.c_order_id = inv.c_order_id
     LEFT JOIN c_invoiceline invline ON inv.c_invoice_id = invline.c_invoice_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner bp ON ord.c_bpartner_id = bp.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_invoice inv1 ON ord.documentno::text = inv1.poreference::text
     LEFT JOIN c_invoiceline invline1 ON inv1.c_invoice_id = invline1.c_invoice_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_alasandiskon adis ON ordl.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id = adis.z_tbl_alasandiskon_id
     LEFT JOIN c_project pro ON ord.c_project_id = pro.c_project_id
     LEFT JOIN z_tbl_buku_peternak bpt ON pro.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id = bpt.z_tbl_buku_peternak_id
     LEFT JOIN z_fakturpajak_header fph ON inv.documentno::text = fph.z_invoice_no::text
  WHERE ord.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND (ord.c_doctypetarget_id IN ( SELECT dtyp.c_doctype_id
           FROM c_doctype dtyp
          WHERE dtyp.name::text ~~ 'SPPA'::text));


-- adempiere.z_send_data_mrtomypig_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_send_data_mrtomypig_reportview
AS SELECT io.documentno,
    werhos.value AS werhos_val,
    werhos.name AS werhos_name,
    io.m_warehouse_id,
    io.movementdate AS dateordered,
    prod.name AS prod_name,
    prod.value AS prod_val,
        CASE
            WHEN dt.name::text = 'GR Feed'::text THEN iol.movementqty * cuc.dividerate
            ELSE iol.movementqty
        END AS movementqty,
        CASE
            WHEN dt.name::text = 'GR Feed'::text THEN uomto.name
            ELSE uom.name
        END AS uom_name,
    bpg.name,
    io.m_inout_id,
    io.ad_client_id,
    io.ad_org_id,
    io.z_issent,
    io.updated,
    io.updatedby,
    io.createdby,
    dt.name AS doctype_name,
    procat.name AS prodcat_name,
    mpc.name AS materialtypename,
    prod.producttype
   FROM m_inout io
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse werhos ON werhos.m_warehouse_id = io.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN m_inoutline iol ON iol.m_inout_id = io.m_inout_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON prod.m_product_id = iol.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category procat ON procat.m_product_category_id = prod.m_product_category_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uom ON uom.c_uom_id = iol.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bp_group bpg ON bpg.c_bp_group_id = io.c_project_bp_group_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype dt ON dt.c_doctype_id = io.c_doctype_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom_conversion cuc ON cuc.c_uom_id = cuc.c_uom_id AND cuc.m_product_id = iol.m_product_id
     LEFT JOIN c_uom uomto ON cuc.c_uom_to_id = uomto.c_uom_id
     LEFT JOIN m_product_category mpc ON prod.materialtype_id = mpc.m_product_category_id
  WHERE (io.ad_org_id IN ( SELECT org.ad_org_id
           FROM ad_org org
             JOIN ad_ref_list refl ON refl.value::text = org.z_product_line::text
             JOIN ad_reference refe ON refe.ad_reference_id = refl.ad_reference_id
          WHERE refe.name::text = 'Z_Production_Line'::text AND refl.name::text = 'PIG FARM'::text)) AND io.docstatus = 'CO'::bpchar AND bpg.name::text ~~* '%cofarm%'::text;

-- adempiere.z_sppb_qty_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_sppb_qty_reportview
AS SELECT sppb.ad_client_id,
    sppb.ad_org_id,
    sppb.z_sppb_id,
    sppb.updated,
    yus_upt.name AS upt_name,
    yus_crt.name AS crt_username,
    sppb.created,
    sppb.isactive,
    sppb.docstatus,
    sppb.documentno,
    sppb.z_persetujuan_sppb_id,
    sppb.m_inventory_id,
    sppb.c_doctypetarget_id,
    sppb.z_catching_date,
    sppb.salesrep_id,
    sppb.user1_id,
    sppb.m_pricelist_id,
    sppb.totallines,
    sppb.grandtotal,
    "left"(sppb.dateordered::text, 10) AS dateordered,
    wer.m_warehouse_id,
    wer.c_location_id,
    sppb.c_bpartner_id,
    sppb.c_bpartner_location_id,
    wer.name AS warehouse_name,
    (((COALESCE(loc.address1::text || ', '::text, ''::text) || COALESCE(loc.address2::text || ', '::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(loc.address3::text || ', '::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(loc.address4::text || ', '::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(loc.city, ''::character varying)::text AS warehouse_location,
    par.name AS bpartner_name,
    (((COALESCE(bparloc.address1::text || ', '::text, ''::text) || COALESCE(bparloc.address2::text || ', '::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(bparloc.address3::text || ', '::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(bparloc.address4::text || ', '::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(bparloc.city, ''::character varying)::text AS bpartner_location,
    sppbl.line,
    sppbl.m_product_id,
    sppbl.z_qty_ekor_est,
    sppbl.z_abw_est,
    sppbl.z_qty_kg_est,
    sppbl.z_extra_kg,
    sppbl.z_price_per_kg,
    sppbl.z_pricelist_estimasi,
    sppbl.z_actual_price_est,
    sppbl.z_add_price,
    prod.name AS prod_name,
    prod.value AS prod_value,
    sppbl.discountamtest,
    sppbl.z_net_discount_est,
    pp1.z_alasan_diskon AS alasandiskonest_id,
    sppbl.c_tax_id,
    sppbl.z_qty_ekor_real,
    sppbl.z_abw_real,
    sppbl.qtyentered,
    sppbl.z_extra_kg_real,
    sppbl.z_price_per_kg_real,
    sppbl.z_pricelist_real,
    sppbl.z_actual_price_real,
    sppbl.z_add_price_real,
    sppbl.z_discount_real,
    sppbl.z_net_discount_real,
    sppbl.z_harga_realisasi,
    pp2.z_alasan_diskon AS z_parampig_alasandiskon_id,
    sppbl.linenetamt,
    sppb.isapproved,
    sppb.c_doctype_id,
    dt.name AS doctype_name,
    NULL::numeric AS btb_line,
    NULL::text AS btb_sex,
    NULL::numeric AS btb_qty,
    NULL::numeric AS btb_bruto,
    NULL::numeric AS btb_keranjang,
    NULL::numeric AS btb_neto,
    NULL::numeric AS btb_pig_cycle,
    NULL::numeric AS btb_piglist
   FROM z_sppb sppb
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse wer ON wer.m_warehouse_id = sppb.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN z_sppb_line sppbl ON sppbl.z_sppb_id = sppb.z_sppb_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user yus_upt ON yus_upt.ad_user_id = sppb.updatedby
     LEFT JOIN ad_user yus_crt ON yus_crt.ad_user_id = sppb.createdby
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON prod.m_product_id = sppbl.m_product_id
     JOIN c_location loc ON loc.c_location_id = wer.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner par ON par.c_bpartner_id = sppb.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location bparloc ON bparloc.c_location_id = sppb.c_bpartner_location_id
     LEFT JOIN z_parampig_alasandiskon pp1 ON pp1.z_parampig_alasandiskon_id = sppbl.alasandiskonest_id
     LEFT JOIN z_parampig_alasandiskon pp2 ON pp2.z_parampig_alasandiskon_id = sppbl.z_parampig_alasandiskon_id
     LEFT JOIN c_doctype dt ON dt.c_doctype_id = sppb.c_doctype_id
  WHERE (sppb.ad_org_id IN ( SELECT bp.ad_org_id
           FROM z_parameter_reporting pr
             JOIN z_parameter_report_bp bp ON bp.z_parameter_reporting_id = pr.z_parameter_reporting_id
          WHERE pr.name::text = 'Function Swine Breeder'::text));


-- adempiere.z_sppb_reportview source

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW adempiere.z_sppb_reportview
AS SELECT sppb.ad_client_id,
    sppb.ad_org_id,
    sppb.z_sppb_id,
    sppb.updated,
    yus_upt.name AS upt_name,
    yus_crt.name AS crt_username,
    sppb.created,
    sppb.isactive,
    sppb.docstatus,
    sppb.documentno,
    "left"(sppb.dateordered::text, 10) AS dateordered,
    wer.m_warehouse_id,
    wer.c_location_id,
    sppb.c_bpartner_id,
    sppb.c_bpartner_location_id,
    wer.name AS warehouse_name,
    (((COALESCE(loc.address1::text || ', '::text, ''::text) || COALESCE(loc.address2::text || ', '::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(loc.address3::text || ', '::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(loc.address4::text || ', '::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(loc.city, ''::character varying)::text AS warehouse_location,
    par.name AS bpartner_name,
    (((COALESCE(bparloc.address1::text || ', '::text, ''::text) || COALESCE(bparloc.address2::text || ', '::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(bparloc.address3::text || ', '::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(bparloc.address4::text || ', '::text, ''::text)) || COALESCE(bparloc.city, ''::character varying)::text AS bpartner_location,
    sppbl.m_product_id,
    sppbl.z_qty_ekor_est,
    sppbl.z_qty_kg_est,
    prod.name AS prod_name,
    prod.value AS prod_value,
    sppbl.discountamtest,
    sppbl.z_net_discount_est,
    pp1.z_alasan_diskon AS alasandiskonest_id,
    sppbl.z_discount_real,
    sppbl.z_net_discount_real,
    pp2.z_alasan_diskon AS z_parampig_alasandiskon_id,
    sppb.isapproved,
    sppb.c_doctype_id,
    dt.name AS doctype_name
   FROM z_sppb sppb
     LEFT JOIN m_warehouse wer ON wer.m_warehouse_id = sppb.m_warehouse_id
     LEFT JOIN z_sppb_line sppbl ON sppbl.z_sppb_id = sppb.z_sppb_id
     LEFT JOIN ad_user yus_upt ON yus_upt.ad_user_id = sppb.updatedby
     LEFT JOIN ad_user yus_crt ON yus_crt.ad_user_id = sppb.createdby
     LEFT JOIN m_product prod ON prod.m_product_id = sppbl.m_product_id
     JOIN c_location loc ON loc.c_location_id = wer.c_location_id
     LEFT JOIN c_bpartner par ON par.c_bpartner_id = sppb.c_bpartner_id
     LEFT JOIN c_location bparloc ON bparloc.c_location_id = sppb.c_bpartner_location_id
